mayampi maṃ daṭṭhukāmamha yakkha    
yassānukampāya idhāgatosi    
lābhā hi tassa yassa tuvaṃ pihesīti .

Yo kappako sambhavanāmadheyyo  
upāsako kocchabhaṇḍūpajīvī  
jānātha naṃ tumhākaṃ pesiyo so  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 153 -->
<a id="page-153"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 153]*

Mā ca kho naṃ hīḷittha supesalo soti .

Jānāmase yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi yakkha    
na kho taṃ jānāmase edisoti    
mayampi naṃ pūjayissāma yakkha    
sutvāna tuyhaṃ vacanaṃ uḷāranti .

Ye kecimasmiṃ sabbe manussā    
daharā mahantā atha vāpi majjhimā    
sabbeva te ālabhantu vimānaṃ    
passantu puññānaṃ phalaṃ kadariyāti .

Te tattha sabbeva ahaṃ pureti  
taṃ kappakaṃ tattha purakkhitvā  
sabbeva te ālabhiṃsu [^153-1] vimānaṃ  
masakkasāraṃ viya vāsavassa  
te tattha sabbeva ahaṃ pureti  
upāsakattaṃ paṭidesayiṃsu [^153-2]  
pāṇātipātā viratā ahesuṃ  
loke adinnaṃ parivajjayiṃsu  
amajjapā no ca musā bhaṇiṃsu  
sakena dārena ahesuṃ tuṭṭhā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 154 -->
<a id="page-154"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 154]*

te tattha sabbeva ahaṃ pureti    
upāsakattaṃ paṭidesayitvā    
pakkāmi satthā anumodamāno    
yakkhiddhiyā anumato punappunaṃ    
gantvāna te sindhusovīrabhūmiṃ    
dhanatthikā uddayaṃ patthayānā    
yathā payogā paripuṇṇalābhā    
paccāgamama pātaliputtamakkhataṃ    
gantvāna te saṃ gharaṃ sotthivanto    
puttehi dārehi samaṅgibhūtā    
ānandacittā sumanā patītā    
akaṃsu serissa mahaṃ uḷāraṃ    
serissakaṃ pariveṇaṃ māpayiṃsu    
etādisā sappurisāna sevanā    
mahiddhiyā dhammaguṇāna sevanā    
ekassa atthāya upāsakassa    
sabbeva sattā sukhitā ahesunti .

Serissakavimānaṃ dasamaṃ .

[85] 1[^154-1] uccamidaṃ maṇithūṇaṃ vimānaṃ  
samantato dvādasa yojanāni  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 155 -->
<a id="page-155"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 155]*

kūṭāgārā sattasatā uḷārā    
veḷuriyatthambhā ruciratthatā subhā    
tatthacchasi pivasi khādasi ca    
dibbā ca vīṇā pavadanti vaggū    
dibbā rasā kāmaguṇettha pañca    
nāriyo ca naccanti suvaṇṇachannā    
kena tetādiso vaṇṇo      kena te idhamijjhati    
uppajjanti ca te bhogā     ye keci manaso piyā    
pucchāmi taṃ deva mahānubhāva    
manussabhūto kimakāsi puññaṃ    
kenāsi evañjalitānubhāvo    
vaṇṇo ca te sabbadisā pabhāsatīti .

So devaputto attamano     moggallānena pucchito  
pañhaṃ puṭṭho viyākāsi       yassa kammassidaṃ phalaṃ  
dunnikkhittaṃ mālaṃ sunikkhipitvā  
patiṭṭhapetvā sugatassa thūpe  
mahiddhiko camhi mahānubhāvo  
dibbehi kāmehi samaṅgibhūto  
tena metādiso vaṇṇo      tena me idhamijjhati  
uppajjanti ca me bhogā     ye keci manaso piyā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 156 -->
<a id="page-156"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 156]*

tenamhi evañjalitānubhāvo    
vaṇṇo ca me sabbadisā pabhāsatīti .

Sunikkhittavimānaṃ ekādasamaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
dve daliddā dve vihārā    bhaṭako gopālakaṇṭhako    
anekavaṇṇamaṭṭhakuṇḍalī         serissako sunikkhittaṃ    
purisānaṃ sattamo vaggoti .

Bhāṇavāraṃ catutthaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 157 -->
<a id="page-157"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 157]*

Suttantapiṭake khuddakanikāyassa    
petavatthu    
namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa .

[86] [^157-1] khettūpamā arahanto        dāyakā kassakūpamā    
bījūpamaṃ deyyadhammaṃ          etto [^157-1] nibbattate phalaṃ    
etaṃ bījaṃ [^157-2] kasikhettaṃ      petānaṃ dāyakassa ca    
taṃ petā paribhuñjanti        dātā puññena vaḍḍhati    
idheva kusalaṃ katvā         pete ca paṭipūjiya    
saggañca kamati ṭhānaṃ         kammaṃ katvāna bhaddakanti .

Khettūpamāpetavatthu paṭhamaṃ .

[87] [^157-2] kāyo te sabbaso vaṇṇo    sabbā obhāsate disā    
mukhaṃ te sūkarasseva         kiṃ kammamakarā [^157-3] pureti [^157-4] .

Kāyena saññato āsiṃ       vācāyāsiṃ asaññato  
tena metādiso vaṇṇo      yathā passasi nārada  
tantyāhaṃ nārada brūmi       sāmaṃ diṭṭhamidantayā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 158 -->
<a id="page-158"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 158]*

Mākāsi mukhasā pāpaṃ        mā kho sūkaramukho ahūti .

Sūkarapetavatthu dutiyaṃ .

[88] [^158-3] dibbaṃ subhaṃ dhāresi vaṇṇadhātuṃ    
vehāyasaṃ tiṭṭhasi antalikkhe    
mukhañca te kimiyo pūtigandhaṃ    
khādanti kiṃ kammamakāsi pubbeti [^158-1] .

Samaṇo ahaṃ pāpo [^158-2] dukkhavāco    
tapassirūpo mukhasā asaññato    
laddhā ca me tapasā vaṇṇadhātu    
mukhañca me pesuniyena pūti .

Tayidaṃ tayā nārada sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ    
anukampakā ye kusalā vadeyyuṃ    
mā pesunaṃ mā ca musā abhāṇi    
yakkho tuvaṃ hohisi kāmakāmīti .

Pūtimukhapetvatthu tatiyaṃ .

[89] [^158-4] yaṅkiñcārammaṇaṃ katvā       dajjā dānamamaccharī    
pubbe peteva [^158-3] ārabbha   atha vā vatthudevatā    
cattāro va [^158-4] mahārāje   lokapāle yasassino [^158-5]    
kuveraṃ dhataraṭṭhañca          virūpakkhaṃ [^158-6] virūḷhakaṃ    
#[^158-1] ma. Itisaddo natthi .  [^158-2] ma. Pāpoti duṭṭhavāco . Sī. Pāpo duṭṭhavāco .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 159 -->
<a id="page-159"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 159]*

Te ceva [^159-1] pūjitā honti   dāyakā ca anipphalā .

Na hi ruṇṇaṃ vā soko vā    yāvaññā paridevanā    
na taṃ petassa atthāya       evaṃ tiṭṭhanti ñātayo [^159-2] .

Ayañca kho dakkhiṇā dinnā    saṅghamhi suppatiṭṭhitā    
dīgharattaṃ hitāyassa          ṭhānaso upakappatīti .

Piṭṭhadhītalikapetavatthu catutthaṃ .

[90] [^159-5] tirokuḍḍesu [^159-3] tiṭṭhanti     sandhisiṅghāṭakesu ca    
dvārabāhāsu tiṭṭhanti       āgantvāna sakaṃ gharaṃ .

Pahūte annapānamhi         khajjabhojje upaṭṭhite    
na tesaṃ koci sarati         sattānaṃ kammapaccayā .

Evaṃ dadanti ñātīnaṃ         ye honti anukampakā    
suciṃ paṇītaṃ kālena          kappiyaṃ pānabhojanaṃ    
idaṃ vo ñātīnaṃ hotu        sukhitā hontu ñātayo .

Te ca tattha samāgantvā     ñātipetā samāgatā    
pahūte annapānamhi         sakkaccaṃ anumodare    
ciraṃ jīvantu no ñātī        yesaṃ hetu labhāmase    
amhākañca katā pūjā       dāyakā ca anipphalā .

Na hi tattha kasi atthi        gorakkhettha na vijjati    
vaṇijjā tādisī natthi        hiraññena kayākayaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 160 -->
<a id="page-160"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 160]*

Ito dinnena yāpenti      petā kālakatā tahiṃ    
unname [^160-1] udakaṃ vuṭṭhaṃ      yathā ninnaṃ pavattati    
evameva ito dinnaṃ        petānaṃ upakappati .

Yathā vārivahā pūrā        paripūrenti sāgaraṃ    
evameva ito dinnaṃ        petānaṃ upakappati .

Adāsi me akāsi me       ñātimittā sakhā ca me    
petānaṃ dakkhiṇaṃ dajjā       pubbe katamanussaraṃ    
na hi ruṇṇaṃ vā soko vā    yāvaññā paridevanā    
na taṃ petassa [^160-2] atthāya    evaṃ tiṭṭhanti ñātayo .

Ayañca kho dakkhiṇā dinnā    saṅghamhi suppatiṭṭhitā    
dīgharattaṃ hitāyassa          ṭhānaso upakappati .

So ñātidhammo ca ayaṃ nidassito    
petāna pūjā ca katā uḷārā    
balañca bhikkhūnamanuppadinnaṃ    
tumhehi puññaṃ pasutaṃ anappakanti .

Tirokuḍḍapetavatthu pañcamaṃ .

[91] [^160-6] naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāsi        duggandhā pūti vāyasi    
makkhikāhi parikiṇṇā         kā nu tvaṃ idha tiṭṭhasīti .

Ahaṃ bhadante petīmhi        duggatā yamalokikā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 161 -->
<a id="page-161"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 161]*

Pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatā [^161-1]    
kālena pañca puttāni       sāyaṃ pañca punāpare    
vijāyitvāna khādāmi        tepi na honti me alaṃ .

Pariḍayhati dhūmāyati          khudāya hadayaṃ mama    
pānīyaṃ na labhe pātuṃ        passa maṃ byasanaṃ gatanti .

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         puttamaṃsāni khādasīti .

Sapatī me gabbhinī āsi       tassā pāpaṃ acetayiṃ    
sāhaṃ paduṭṭhamanasā          akariṃ gabbhapātanaṃ .

Tassā dvemāsiko gabbho    lohitaññeva pagghari    
tadassā mātā kupitā       mayhaṃ ñātī samānayi    
sapathañca maṃ akāresi        paribhāsāpayi ca maṃ .

Sāhaṃ ghorañca sapathaṃ         musāvādaṃ abhāsiyaṃ [^161-2]    
puttamaṃsāni khādāmi         sacetaṃ pakataṃ [^161-3] mayā .

Tassa kammavipākena         musāvādassa cūbhayaṃ    
puttamaṃsāni khādāmi         pubbalohitamakkhitāti [^161-4] .

Pañcaputtakhādikapetavatthu [^161-5] chaṭṭhaṃ .

[92] [^161-7] naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāsi        duggandhā pūti vāyati    
#[^161-1] yu. Gatāti .  [^161-2] yu. Abhāsissaṃ .  [^161-3] yu. Sapathañca kataṃ .  [^161-4] yu. ...Kāti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 162 -->
<a id="page-162"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 162]*

Makkhikāhi parikiṇṇā         kā nu tvaṃ idha tiṭṭhasīti .

Ahaṃ bhadante petīmhi        duggatā yamalokikā    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokamito gatā    
kālena satta puttāni       sāyaṃ satta punāpare    
vijāyitvāna khādāmi        tepi na honti me alaṃ .

Pariḍayhati dhūmāyati          khudāya hadayaṃ mama    
nibbutiṃ nādhigacchāmi         aggidaḍḍheva [^162-1] ātapeti .

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         puttamaṃsāni khādasīti .

Ahu mayhaṃ duve puttā       ubho sampattayobbanā    
sāhaṃ puttabalūpetā         sāmikaṃ atimaññissaṃ [^162-2] .

Tato me sāmiko kuddho     sapatimaññamānayi [^162-3]    
sā ca gabbhaṃ alabhittha        tassā pāpaṃ acetayiṃ    
sāhaṃ paduṭṭhamanasā          akariṃ gabbhapātanaṃ .

Tassā temāsiko gabbho     pubbalohitako [^162-4] pati    
tadassā mātā kupitā       mayhaṃ ñātī samānayi    
sapathañca maṃ akāresi        paribhāsāpayi ca maṃ .

Sāhaṃ ghorañca sapathaṃ         musāvādañca [^162-5] bhāsissaṃ    
puttamaṃsāni khādāmi         sacetaṃ pakataṃ mayā .

#[^162-1] ma. Aggidaḍḍhāva .  [^162-2] yu. Atimaññasiṃ .  [^162-3] ma. Sapattiṃ mayhamānayi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 163 -->
<a id="page-163"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 163]*

Tassa kammavipākena         musāvādassa cūbhayaṃ    
puttamaṃsāni khādāmi         pubbalohitamakkhitāti .

Sattaputtakhādikapetavatthu sattamaṃ .

[93] [^163-8] kinnu ummattarūpova         lāyitvā haritaṃ tiṇaṃ    
khāda khādāti lapasi         gatasattaṃ jaraggavaṃ .

Na hi annena pānena       mato goṇo samuṭṭhahe    
tvaṃpi [^163-1] bālo ca dummedho  yathā añño [^163-2] ca dummatīti .

Ime pādā idaṃ sīsaṃ        ayaṃ kāyo savāladhi    
nettā tatheva tiṭṭhanti      ayaṃ goṇo samuṭṭhahe    
nayyakassa hatthapādā        kāyo sīsañca dissati    
rudaṃ mattikathūpasmiṃ           nanu tvaññeva dummatīti .

Ādittaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ        ghatasittaṃva pāvakaṃ    
vārinā viya osiñcaṃ        sabbaṃ nibbāpaye daraṃ .

Abbūḷhaṃ vata me sallaṃ       sokaṃ hadayanissitaṃ    
yo me sokaparetassa       pitusokaṃ apānudi    
svāhaṃ abbūḷhasallosmiṃ [^163-4]   sītibhūtosmi nibbuto    
na socāmi na rodāmi       tava sutvāna māṇava .

Evaṃ karonti sappaññā      ye honti anukampakā    
nivattayanti [^163-5] sokamhā     sujāto pitaraṃ yathāti .

Goṇapetavatthu aṭṭhamaṃ .

#[^163-1] yu. Tvaṃsi .  [^163-2] ma. Taññova . Yu. Taññeva .   [^163-3] ma. Abbahī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 164 -->
<a id="page-164"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 164]*

[94] [^164-9] gūthañca muttaṃ rudhirañca [^164-1] pubbaṃ    
paribhuñjati kissa ayaṃ vipāko    
ayaṃ nu kiṃ kammamakāsi nārī    
yā sabbadā lohitapubbabhakkhā    
navāni vatthāni subhāni cevaื    
mudūni suddhāni ca lomasāni    
dinnāni missā kiṭakā bhavanti    
ayaṃ nu kiṃ kammamakāsi nārīti .

Bhariyā mamesā ahu bhadante    
adāyikā maccharinī kadariyā    
sā maṃ dadantaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ    
akkosati paribhāsati ca    
gūthañca muttaṃ rudhirañca [^164-1] pubbaṃ    
paribhuñja tvaṃ asuciṃ sabbakālaṃ .

Etaṃ te paralokasmiṃ hotu    
vatthā ca te kiṭakā [^164-2] bhavantu    
etādisaṃ duccaritaṃ caritvā    
idhāgatā cirarattāya khādatīti .

Mahāpesakārapetavatthu navamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 165 -->
<a id="page-165"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 165]*

[95] 10 kā nu antovimānasmiṃ       tiṭṭhantī nūpanikkhami    
upanikkhamassu bhadde         passāmi [^165-1] taṃ mahiddhikanti [^165-2] .

Aṭṭiyāmi harāyāmi         naggā nikkhamituṃ bahi    
kesehamhi paṭicchannā       puññaṃ me appakaṃ katanti .

Handuttarīyaṃ dāmi [^165-3] te     imaṃ dussaṃ nivāsaya    
imaṃ dussaṃ nivāsetvā       bahi [^165-4] nikkhama sobhaṇe    
upanikkhamassu bhadde         passāmi taṃ mahiddhikanti [^165-2] .

Hatthena hatthe te dinnaṃ     na mayhaṃ upakappati    
esetthupāsako saddho      sammā sambuddhasāvako    
etaṃ acchādayitvāna        mama dakkhiṇamādisa    
tathāhaṃ [^165-5] sukhitā hessaṃ     sabbakāmasamiddhinīti .

Tañca te nahāpayitvāna      vilimpitvāna vāṇijā    
vatthehacchādayitvāna        tassā dakkhiṇamādisuṃ    
samanantarānudiṭṭhe          vipāko upapajjatha [^165-6]    
bhojanacchādanapānīyaṃ         dakkhiṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ    
tato suddhā sucivasanā       kāsikuttamadhārinī    
hasantī vimānā nikkhami       dakkhiṇāya idaṃ phalanti .

Sucittarūpaṃ ruciraṃ            vimānaṃ te ca bhāsati [^165-7]    
devate pucchitācikkha        kissa kammassidaṃ phalanti .

#[^165-1] ma. Passāma .  [^165-2] ma. Mahiṭṭhitanti .  [^165-3] ma. Dadāmi .  [^165-4] ma. Ehi .  [^165-5] yu. Tadāhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 166 -->
<a id="page-166"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 166]*

Bhikkhuno caramānassa         doṇinimmujjanī [^166-1] ahaṃ    
adāsiṃ ujubhūtassa           vippasannena cetasā    
tassa kammassa kusalassa       vipākaṃ dīghamantaraṃ    
anubhomi vimānasmiṃ          tañca dāni parittakaṃ .

Uddhaṃ catūhi māsehi         kālakiriyā bhavissati    
ekantaṃ kaṭukaṃ ghoraṃ         nirayūpapatissahaṃ [^166-2]    
catukkaṇṇaṃ catudvāraṃ         vibhattaṃ bhāgaso mitaṃ    
ayopākārapariyantaṃ         ayasā paṭikujjitaṃ .

Tassa ayomayā bhūmi         jalitā tejasā yuttā    
samantā yojanasataṃ          pharitvā tiṭṭhati sabbadā    
tatthāhaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ         dukkhaṃ vedissaṃ [^166-3] vedanaṃ_    
phalañca pāpakammassa         tasmā socāmidaṃ [^166-4] bhūtanti .

Khalātiyapetavatthu [^166-5] dasamaṃ .

[96] 1[^166-1] purato ca [^166-6] setena paleti hatthinā    
majjhe pana assatarīrathena    
pacchā ca kaññā sivikāyaṃ niyyāti    
obhāsayantī dasa sabbato disā .

Tumhe pana muggarahatthapāṇino    
rudammukhā bhinnapabhinnagattā    
#[^166-1] ma. Doṇinimmajjaniṃ .  Yu. Doṇinimmiñjanaṃ .  [^166-2] ma. Nirayaṃ papatissahaṃ .

#[^166-3] ma. Vedissa .  [^166-4] ma. Socāmahaṃ bhusanti .  [^166-5] ma. Khallāṭiyapetivatku .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 167 -->
<a id="page-167"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 167]*

Manussabhūtā kimakattha pāpaṃ    
yenaññamaññassa pivātha lohitaṃ (iti) .

Purato va yo gacchati kuñjarena    
setena nāgena catukkamena    
amhākaṃ putto ahu so pajeṭṭhako [^167-1]    
dānāni datvāna sukhiṃ [^167-2] pamodati .

Yo so majjhe assatarīrathena    
catubbhi yuttena suvaggitena    
amhākaṃ putto ahu majjhimo so    
amaccharī dānapati [^167-3] virocati .

Yā sā [4] pacchā sivikāya niyyāti    
nārī [^167-5] sapaññā migamandalocanā    
amhākaṃ dhītā ahu sā kaniṭṭhā [^167-6]    
bhāgaḍḍhabhāgena sukhī pamodati .

Ete ca dānāni adaṃsu pubbe    
pasannacittā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ    
mayaṃ pana maccharino ahumhā [^167-7]    
paribhāsakā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ    
ete ca datvā paricārayanti    
#[^167-1] ma. Jeṭṭhaposo . Yu. Jeṭṭhako .  [^167-2] ma. Sukhī .  [^167-3] ma. Dānavatī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 168 -->
<a id="page-168"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 168]*

Mayañca sussāma naḷova dittoti [^168-1] .

Kiṃ tumhākaṃ bhojanaṃ kiṃ sayanaṃ    
kathañca yāpetha supāpadhammino    
pahūtabhogesu anappakesu    
sukhaṃ virāgāya [^168-2] dukkhajja pattā [^168-3] .

Aññamaññaṃ vadhitvāna         pivāma pubbalohitaṃ    
bahuṃ pitvā na dātā homa    nacchādimhamhase [^168-4] mayaṃ .

Icceva maccā paridevayanti    
adāyikā [^168-5] pecca yamassa ṭhāyino    
ye te viriccā [^168-6] adhigamma bhoge    
na bhuñjare nāpi karonti puññaṃ .

Te khuppipāsupagatā parattha    
pacchā ciraṃ jhāyire [^168-7] ḍayhamānā    
kammāni katvāna dukkhudrayāni [^168-8]    
anubhonti dukkhaṃ kaṭukapphalāni .

Ittaraṃ hi dhanadhaññaṃ          ittaraṃ idha jīvitaṃ    
ittaraṃ ittarato ñatvā      dīpaṃ kayirātha paṇḍito .

Ye te evaṃ pajānanti      narā dhammassa kovidā    
te dāne nappamajjanti      sutvā arahataṃ vacoti .

Nāgapetavatthu ekādasamaṃ .

#[^168-1] ma. Chinnoti .  2. Ma. Virādhāya .  [^168-3] ma. Pattāti .  [^168-4] ma. Nacchādimhase .

#[^168-5] ma. Adāyakā .  [^168-6] sī. Viditvā . Ma. Vidicca . Yu. Viviccā .  [^168-7] ma. Jhāyare .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 169 -->
<a id="page-169"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 169]*

[97] 1[^169-2] uragova tacaṃ jiṇṇaṃ          hitvā gacchati santanana    
evaṃ sarīre nibbhoge       pete kālakate sati    
ḍayhamāno na jānāti       ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ    
tasmā etaṃ na socāmi [^169-1]   gato so tassa yā gati [^169-2]    
anabbhito tato āgā       nānuññāto ito gato    
yathāgato tathā gato        tattha kā paridevanā    
ḍayhamāno na jānāti       ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ    
tasmā etaṃ na rodāmi      gato so tassa yā gati [^169-2] .

Sace rode kisā assaṃ      tattha me kiṃ phalaṃ siyā    
ñātimittasuhajjānaṃ [^169-3]       bhiyyo no aratī siyā    
ḍayhamāno na jānāti       ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ    
tasmā etaṃ na rodāmi      gato so tassa yā gati [^169-2] .

Yathāpi dārako candaṃ        gacchantaṃ anurodati    
evaṃ sampadamevetaṃ         yo petaṃ anusocati    
ḍayhamāno na jānāti       ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ    
tasmā etaṃ na rodāmi      gato so tassa yā gati [^169-2] .

Yathāpi brahme udakumbho     bhinno appaṭisandhiyo  
evaṃ sampadamevetaṃ         yo petaṃ anusocati  
ḍayhamāno na jānāti       ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 170 -->
<a id="page-170"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 170]*

Tasmā etaṃ na rodāmi      gato so tassa yā gatīti .

Uragapetavatthu dvādasamaṃ .

Uddānaṃ [^170-1]    
khettūpamā [^170-2] vaṇṇā duve   dhītalīkaṃ tirokuḍḍaṃ    
puttakhādikā goṇagūthaṃ        pāsādaṃ nāgoraga dvādasā    
te vatthūni vagge paṭhame uddānaṃ [^170-3] .

Uragavaggo paṭhamo .

[98] [^170-1] naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāsi        kīsā dhamanisaṇṭhitā [^170-4]    
upphāsuḷike kīsike         kā nu tvaṃ idha tiṭṭhasīti .

Ahaṃ bhadante petīmhi        duggatā yamalokikā    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokamito gatāti .

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         petalokaṃ ito gatāti .

Anukkampakā mayhaṃ nāhesuṃ bhante    
pitā mātā ca atha vāpi ñātakā    
ye maṃ niyojeyyuṃ dadāhi dānaṃ    
pasannacittā samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ .

Ito ahaṃ vassasatāni pañca    
yaṃ evarūpā vicarāmi naggā    
#[^170-1] tassuddānaṃ .

#[^170-2] ma.     Khettaṃ ca sūkaraṃ pūti         piṭṭhaṃ cāpi tirokuṭṭaṃ    
#         pañcāpi sattaputtañca        goṇaṃ pesakārakañca    
#         tathā khallāṭiyaṃ nāgaṃ        dvādasaṃ udagañcevāti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 171 -->
<a id="page-171"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 171]*

Khudāya taṇhāya ca khajjamānā    
pāpassa kammassa phalaṃ mamedaṃ .

Vandāmi taṃ ayya pasannacittā    
anukampa maṃ vīra mahānubhāva    
datvā ca me ādisa yāhi [^171-1] kiñci    
mocehi maṃ duggatiyā bhadanteti .

Sādhūti so tassā [^171-2] paṭisuṇitvā   sārīputto anukampako    
bhikkhūnaṃ ālopaṃ datvā       pāṇimattañca colakaṃ    
thālakassa ca pānīyaṃ         tassā dakkhiṇamādisi    
samanantarā anudiṭṭhe        vipāko upapajjatha .

Bhojanacchādanapānīyaṃ         dakkhiṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ    
tato suddhā sucivasanā       kāsikuttamadhārinī    
vicittavatthābharaṇā          sārīputtaṃ upasaṅkami .

Abhikkantena vaṇṇena        yā tvaṃ tiṭṭhasi devate    
obhāsentī disā sabbā     osadhī viya tārakā .

Kena tetādiso vaṇṇo      kena te idhamijjhati  
uppajjanti ca te bhogā     ye keci manaso piyā  
pucchāmi taṃ devi mahānubhāve  
manussabhūtā kimakāsi puññaṃ  
kenāpi [^171-3] evaṃ jalitānubhāvā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 172 -->
<a id="page-172"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 172]*

Vaṇṇo ca te sabbadisā pabhāsatīti .

Uppaṇḍukiṃ [^172-1] kisaṃ chātaṃ      naggasamuṭitacchaviṃ [^172-2]    
muni kāruṇiko loke        taṃ maṃ adakkhi dukkhitaṃ [^172-3]    
bhikkhūnaṃ ālopaṃ datvā       pāṇimattañca colakaṃ    
thālakassa ca pānīyaṃ         mama dakkhiṇamādisi .

Ālopassa phalaṃ passa        bhattaṃ vassasataṃ dasa    
bhuñjāmi kāmakāminī         anekarasabyañjanaṃ .

Pāṇimattassa colassa        vipākaṃ passa yādisaṃ    
yāvatā nandarājassa        vijitasmiṃ paṭicchadā    
tato bahutarā bhante        vatthānicchādanāni me    
koseyyakambalīyāni         khomakappāsikāni ca    
vipulā ca mahagghā ca        tepākāseva lambare    
sāhaṃ taṃ paridahāmi          yaṃ yaṃ hi manaso piyaṃ    
thālakassa ca pānīyaṃ         vipākaṃ passa yādisaṃ .

Gambhīrā caturassā ca        pokkharaññā [^172-4] sunimmitā    
setodakā supatitthā        sītā appaṭigandhiyā    
padumuppalasañchannā          vārikiñjakkhapūritā    
sāhaṃ ramāmi kīḷāmi         modāmi akutobhayā    
muniṃ kāruṇikaṃ loke         bhante vanditumāgatāti .

Saṃsāramocakapetavatthu paṭhamaṃ .

#[^172-1] yu. Upakaṇḍakiṃ .  [^172-2] sī. Āpatitacchaviṃ . Ma. Naggaṃ sampatitacchaviṃ . Yu. Naggaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 173 -->
<a id="page-173"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 173]*

[99] [^173-2] naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāsi        kīsā dhamanisaṇṭhitā    
upphāsuḷike kīsike         kā nu tvaṃ idha tiṭṭhasīti .

Ahante sakiyā mātā       pubbe aññāsu jātīsu    
uppannā pittivisayaṃ         khuppipāsā samappitā    
chaḍḍitaṃ khipitaṃ kheḷaṃ          siṅghāṇikaṃ silesumaṃ    
vasañca ḍayhamānānaṃ         vijātānañca lohitaṃ    
vaṇitānañca [^173-1] yaṃ ghānaṃ      sīsacchinnañca [^173-2] lohitaṃ    
khudāparetā bhuñjāmi        itthīpurisanissitaṃ    
pubbalohitaṃ bhakkhāmi [^173-3]      pasūnaṃ manussānañca    
aleṇā ca anagārā ca      nīlamañcaparāyanā    
dehi puttaka me dānaṃ       datvā uddisāhi [^173-4] me    
appeva nāma muñceyyaṃ      pubbalohitabhojanāti .

Mātuyā vacanaṃ sutvā        upatissonukampako    
āmantayi moggallānaṃ       anuruddhañca kappinaṃ    
catasso kuṭiyo katvā       saṅghe catuddise adā    
kuṭiyo annapānañca         mātu dakkhiṇamādisi .

Samanantarānudiṭṭhe          vipāko upapajjatha    
bhojanaṃ pānīyaṃ vatthaṃ         dakkhiṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ    
tato suddhā sucivasanā       kāsikuttamadhārinī    
vicittavatthābharaṇā          kolitaṃ upasaṅkami .

#[^173-1] ma. Vaṇikānañca .  [^173-2] ma. Ghāna-sīsacchinnāna lohitaṃ .  [^173-3] sī. --lohitabhakkhāsmi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 174 -->
<a id="page-174"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 174]*

Abhikkantena vaṇṇena        yā tvaṃ tiṭṭhasi devate    
obhāsentī disā sabbā     osadhī viya tārakā    
kena tetādiso vaṇṇo      kena te idhamijjhati    
uppajjanti ca te bhogā     ye keci manaso piyā    
pucchāmi taṃ devi mahānubhāve    
manussabhūtā kimakāsi puññaṃ    
kenāpi [^174-1] evaṃ jalitānubhāvā    
vaṇṇo ca te sabbadisā pabhāsatīti .

Sārīputtassāhaṃ mātā       pubbe aññāsu jātīsu    
uppannā pittivisayaṃ         khuppipāsā samappitā    
chaḍḍitaṃ khipitaṃ kheḷaṃ          siṅghāṇikaṃ kilesumaṃ    
vasañca ḍayhamānānaṃ         vijātānañca lohitaṃ    
vaṇitānañca yaṃ ghānaṃ         sīsacchinnañca lohitaṃ    
khudāparetā bhuñjissaṃ [^174-2]     itthīpurisanissitaṃ    
pubbalohitaṃ bhakkhissaṃ         pasūnaṃ manussānañca    
aleṇā ca anagārā ca      nīlamañcaparāyanā .

Sārīputtassa dānena        modāmi akutobhayā    
muniṃ kāruṇikaṃ loke         taṃ [^174-3] bhante vanditumāgatāti .

Sārīputtattherassa mātupetivatthu dutiyaṃ .

[100] [^174-3] naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāsi kīsā dhamanisaṇṭhitā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 175 -->
<a id="page-175"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 175]*

Upphāsuḷike kīsike         kā nu tvaṃ idha tiṭṭhasīti .

Sāhaṃ mattā tuvaṃ tissā      sapattī [^175-1] te pure ahuṃ    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatāti .

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         petalokaṃ ito gatāti .

Caṇḍī ca pharusā cāsiṃ        issukī maccharī saṭhā 2-    
tāhaṃ duruttaṃ vatvāna        petalokaṃ ito gatāti .

Saccaṃ [^175-3] ahaṃpi jānāmi      yathā tvaṃ caṇḍikā ahu    
aññañca kho taṃ pucchāmi      kenāsi paṃsukuṭṭhitā [^175-4] .

Sīsaṃ nahātā tuvaṃ āsi       sucivatthā alaṅkatā    
ahañca kho [5] adhimattaṃ    samalaṅkatarā [^175-6] tayā    
tassā me pekkhamānāya     sāmikena samantayi    
tato me issā vipulā      kodho me samajāyatha    
tato paṃsuṃ gahetvāna        paṃsunā taṃ vikīrihaṃ [^175-7]    
tassa kammavipākena         tenamhi paṃsukuṭṭhitāti [^175-4] .

Saccaṃ ahaṃpi jānāmi         paṃsunā maṃ tvaṃ okiri    
aññañca kho taṃ pucchāmi      kena khajjāsi kacchuyāti .

Bhesajjahārī ubhayo         vanantaṃ agamimhase    
tvaṃ ca bhesajjamāhāri       ahañca kapikacchuno    
tassā tyājānamānāya      seyyaṃ tyāhaṃ samokiri [^175-8]    
#[^175-1] yu. Sapatī .  [^175-2] sī. Saṭhī .  [^175-3] sī. Yu. Sabbaṃ .Pe.  [^175-4] ma. Paṃsukunthitāti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 176 -->
<a id="page-176"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 176]*

Tassa kammavipākena         tena khajjāmi kacchuyāti .

Saccaṃ ahaṃpi jānāmi         seyyaṃ me tvaṃ samokiri    
aññañca kho taṃ pucchāmi      kenāsi naggiyā tuvanti .

Sahāyānaṃ samayo āsi       ñātīnaṃ samitiṃ ahu    
tavañca āmantitā āsi      sasāmī [^176-1] no ca kho ahaṃ    
tassā tyājānamānāya      dussaṃ tyāhaṃ apānudiṃ    
tassa kammavipākena         tenamhi naggiyā ahanti .

Saccaṃ ahaṃpi jānāmi         dussaṃ me tvaṃ apānudi    
aññañca kho taṃ pucchāmi      kenāsi gūthagandhinīti .

Tava gandhañca mālañca        paccagghañca vilepanaṃ    
gūthakūpe adhāresiṃ [^176-2]       taṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ mayā    
tassa kammavipākena         tenamhi gūthagandhinīti .

Saccaṃ ahaṃpi jānāmi         taṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ tayā    
aññañca kho taṃ pucchāmi      kenāsi duggatā tuvanti .

Ubhinnaṃ samakaṃ āsi          yaṃ gehe vijjate dhanaṃ    
santesu deyyadhammesu       dīpaṃ nākāsimattano    
tassa kammavipākena         tenamhi duggatā ahanti .

Tadeva maṃ tvaṃ avaca         pāpakammaṃ nisevasi  
na hi pāpehi kammehi       sulabhā hosi suggatiṃ [^176-3]  
vāmato maṃ tvaṃ  paccesi     athopi maṃ ussuyyati 4  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 177 -->
<a id="page-177"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 177]*

Passa pāpānaṃ kammānaṃ       vipāko hoti yādiso .

Te gharadāsiyo [^177-1] āsuṃ     tānevābharaṇānime    
te aññe parivārenti      na bhogā honti sassatā    
idāni bhūtassa pitā         āpaṇā gehamehiti    
appeva te dade kiñci      mā su tāva ito gatā [^177-2] .

Naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāmhi       kīsā dhamanisaṇṭhitā    
kopīnaṃ etaṃ itthīnaṃ         mā maṃ bhūtapitāddasa .

Handa kiṃ [3] tyāhaṃ [^177-4] dammi   kiṃ vā ca te [^177-5] karomahaṃ    
yena tvaṃ sukhitā assa       sabbakāmasamiddhinī .

Cattāro bhikkhū saṅghato      cattāro pana puggalā    
aṭṭha bhikkhū bhojayitvā       mama dakkhiṇamādisi    
tadāhaṃ sukhitā hessaṃ        sabbakāmasamiddhinī .

Sādhūti sā paṭisutvā        bhojayitvā aṭṭha bhikkhavo    
vatthehicchādayitvāna        tassā dakkhiṇamādisi .

Samanantarānudiṭṭhe          vipāko upapajjatha    
bhojanacchādanapānīyaṃ         dakkhiṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ    
tato suddhā sucivasanā       kāsikuttamadhārinī    
vicittavatthābharaṇā          sapattiṃ [^177-6] upasaṅkami .

Abhikkantena vaṇṇena        yā tvaṃ tiṭṭhati devate    
#[^177-1] ma. Te gharā tā ca dāsiyo .  [^177-2] ma. Yu. Agā .  [^177-3] ma. Vā .  [^177-4] yu. Kintāhaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 178 -->
<a id="page-178"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 178]*

Obhāsentī disā sabbā     osadhī viya tārakā    
kena tetādiso vaṇṇo      kena te idhamijjhati    
uppajjanti ca te bhogā     ye keci manaso piyā    
pucchāmi taṃ devi mahānubhāve    
manussabhūtā kimakāsi puññaṃ    
kenāsi evaṃ jalitānubhāvā    
vaṇṇo ca te sabbadisā pabhāsatīti .

Ahaṃ mattā tuvaṃ tissā       sapattī te pure ahuṃ    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatā    
tava dānena dinnena [^178-1]     modāmi akutobhayā .

Ciraṃ jīvāhi bhagini           saha sabbehi ñātibhi    
asokaṃ virajaṃ ṭhānaṃ          āvāsaṃ vasavattīnaṃ    
idha dhammaṃ caritvāna         dānaṃ datvāna sobhaṇe .

Vineyya maccheramalaṃ samūlaṃ    
aninditā saggamupesi [^178-2] ṭhānanti .

Mattāpetivatthu tatiyaṃ .

[101] [^178-4] kālī dubbaṇṇarūpāsi         pharusā bhīrudassanā    
piṅgalāsi kaḷārāsi         na taṃ maññāmi mānusinti .

Ahaṃ nandā nandasena        bhariyā te pure ahuṃ    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatāti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 179 -->
<a id="page-179"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 179]*

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         petalokaṃ ito gatāti .

Caṇḍī ca pharusā cāsi [^179-1]     tayi cāsi [^179-2] agāravā    
tāhaṃ duruttaṃ vatvāna        petalokaṃ ito gatāti .

Handuttarīyaṃ dadāmi te       imaṃ dussaṃ nivāsaya    
imaṃ dussaṃ nivāsetvā       ehi nessāmi taṃ gharaṃ    
vatthañca annapānañca        lacchasi tvaṃ gharaṃ gatā    
putte ca te passissasi      suṇisāye [^179-3] ca dakkhasi .

Hatthena hatthe te dinnaṃ     na mayhaṃ upakappati    
bhikkhū ca sīlasampanne        vītarāge bahussute    
tappehi annapānena        mamaṃ dakkhiṇamādisi    
tadāhaṃ sukhitā hessaṃ        sabbakāmasamiddhinīti .

Tato [^179-4] sādhūti so paṭisuṇitvā    dānaṃ vipulamākiri    
annapānaṃ khādanīyaṃ          vatthaṃ senāsanāni ca    
chattaṃ gandhañca mālañca       vividhā ca [^179-5] upāhanā .

Bhikkhū ca sīlasampanne        vītarāge bahussute    
tappetvā annapānena      tassā dakkhiṇamādisi .

Samanantarānudiṭṭhe          vipāko upapajjatha    
bhojanacchādanapānīyaṃ         dakkhiṇāya idaṃ phalaṃ    
#[^179-1] sī. Caṇḍī pharusavācā ca . [^179-2] ma. Cāpi . [^179-3] ma. Suṇisāyo . Yu. Sūtisāye .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 180 -->
<a id="page-180"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 180]*

Tato suddhā sucivasanā       kāsikuttamadhārinī    
vicittavatthābharaṇā          sāmikaṃ upasaṅkami .

Abhikkantena vaṇṇena        yā tvaṃ tiṭṭhasi devate    
obhāsentī disā sabbā     osadhī viya tārakā    
kena tetādiso vaṇṇo      kena te idhamijjhati    
uppajjanti ca te bhogā    ye keci manaso piyā    
pucchāmi taṃ devi mahānubhāve    
manussabhūtā kimakāsi puññaṃ    
kenāsi evaṃ jalitānubhāvā    
vaṇṇo ca te sabbadisā pabhāsatīti .

Ahaṃ nandā nandasena        bhariyā te pure ahuṃ    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatā    
tava dinnena dānena        modāmi akutobhayā .

Ciraṃ jīva gahapati            saha sabbehi ñātibhi    
asokaṃ virajaṃ ṭhānaṃ [^180-1]       āvāsaṃ vasavattīnaṃ    
idha dhammaṃ caritvāna         dānaṃ datvā gahapati .

Vineyya maccheralamalaṃ samūlaṃ    
anindito saggamupesi [^180-2] ṭhānanti .

Nandāpetavatthu catutthaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 181 -->
<a id="page-181"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 181]*

[102] [^181-5] alaṅkato maṭṭhakuṇḍalī    
.Pe.

(vimānavatthusmiṃ sattamassa sunikkhittavaggassa navamavatthusmiṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ)    
maṭṭhakuṇḍalipetavatthu pañcamaṃ .

[103] [^181-6] uṭṭhehi kaṇha kiṃ [^181-1] sesi    ko attho supanena te    
yo ca tuyhaṃ sako bhātā     hadayaṃ cakkhuñca dakkhiṇaṃ    
tassa vātā balīyanti        ghaṭo jappati kesavāti .

Tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        rohiṇeyyassa kesavo    
taramānarūpo [^181-2] vuṭṭhāsi     bhātu sokena addhitoti .

Kiṃ nu ummattarūpova         kevalaṃ dvārakaṃ imaṃ    
saso sasoti lapasi          kīdisaṃ sasamicchasi    
sovaṇṇamayaṃ maṇimayaṃ          lohamayaṃ atha rūpiyamayaṃ    
saṅkhasilāpavāḷamayaṃ          kārayissāmi te sasaṃ    
santi aññepi sasakā        araññavanagocarā    
tepi te ānayissāmi       kīdisaṃ sasamicchasīti .

Nāhaṃ me te sase icche    ye sasā paṭhavīnissitā [^181-3]    
candato sasamicchāmi         taṃ me ohara kesavāti .

So nūna [^181-4] madhuraṃ ñāti      jīvitaṃ vijahissasi    
apatthayaṃ patthayasi           candato sasamicchasīti .

Evañce kaṇha jānāsi      yathaññamanusasāsi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 182 -->
<a id="page-182"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 182]*

Kasmā pure mataṃ puttaṃ       ajjāpi [^182-1] manusocasi .

Na taṃ [^182-2] labbhā manussena    amanussena vā pana    
jāto me māmarī putto     kuto labbhā alabbhiyaṃ    
na mantā mūlabhesajjā       osathehi dhanena vā    
sakkā ānayituṃ kaṇha        yaṃ petamanusocasi .

Mahaddhanā mahābhogā        raṭṭhavantopi khattiyā    
pahūtadhanadhaññāse [^182-3]        tepi no ajarāmarā .

Khattiyā brāhmaṇā vessā   suddā caṇḍālapukkusā    
ete caññe ca jātiyā     tepi no ajarāmarā .

Ye mantaṃ parivattenti       chaḷaṅgaṃ brahmacintitaṃ    
ete caññe ca vijjāya     tepi no ajarāmarā .

Isayo vāpi ye santā      saññatattā tapassino    
sarīraṃ tepi kālena         vijahanti tapassino .

Bhāvitattā arahanto        katakiccā anāsavā    
nikkhipanti imaṃ dehaṃ         puññapāpaparikkhayāti .

Ādittaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ        ghatasittaṃva pāvakaṃ    
vārinā viya osiñcaṃ        sabbaṃ nibbāpaye daraṃ .

Abbūḷhi [^182-4] vata me sallaṃ    sokaṃ hadayanissitaṃ  
yo me sokaparetassa       puttasokaṃ apānudi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 183 -->
<a id="page-183"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 183]*

Svāhaṃ abbūḷhasallosmi      sītibhūtosmi nibbu .

Na socāmi na rodāmi       tava sutvāna bhāsitaṃ [^183-1]    
evaṃ karonti sappaññā      ye honti anukampakā    
nivattayanti [^183-2] sokamhā     ghaṭo jeṭṭhaṃva bhātaraṃ    
yassa etādisā honti      amaccā [^183-3] paricārikā    
subhāsitena anevanti [^183-4]     ghaṭo jeṭṭhaṃva bhātaranti .

Kaṇhapetavatthu chaṭṭhaṃ .

[104] [^183-7] naggo dubbaṇṇarūposi        kīso dhamanisaṇṭhito    
upphāsuliko kīsiko         ko nu tvaṃ asi mārisāti .

Ahaṃ bhadante petomhi       duggato yamalokiko    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatoti .

Kiṃ nu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         petalokaṃ ito gatoti .

Nagaraṃ atthi dasannānaṃ [^183-5]     erakacchanti vissutaṃ    
tattha seṭṭhī pure āsiṃ      dhanapāloti maṃ vidū .

Asīti sakaṭavāhānaṃ          hiraññassa ahosi me    
pahūtaṃ me jātarūpaṃ          muttāveḷuriyā bahū .

Tāva mahādhanassāpi [^183-6]      na me dātuṃ piyaṃ ahu    
#[^183-1] ma. Bhātika .  [^183-2] yu. Vinivattayati .  [^183-3] yu. Amaccaparicārikā .  [^183-4] yu. Anvesi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 184 -->
<a id="page-184"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 184]*

Pidahitvā dvāraṃ bhuñjāmi [^184-1]  mā maṃ yācanakāddasuṃ .

Asaddho maccharī vāsiṃ [^184-2]     kadariyo paribhāsako    
dadantānaṃ karontānaṃ        vārayissaṃ bahujjanaṃ [^184-3] .

Vipāko natthi dānassa       saṃyamassa kuto phalaṃ    
pokkharaññodapānāni        ārāmāni ca ropite    
papāyo ca vināsesiṃ        dugge saṅkamanāni ca .

Svāhaṃ akatakalyāṇo        katapāpo tato cuto    
uppanno pittivisayaṃ [^184-4]      khuppipāsasamappito    
pañcapaññāsavassāni         tato kālakato ahaṃ .

Nābhijānāmi bhuttaṃ vā       pītaṃ vā pana pāniyaṃ    
yo saṃyamo so vināso      yo vināso so saṃyamo    
petā hi kira jānanti       yo [^184-5] saṃyamo so vināso .

Ahaṃ pure saṃyamissaṃ          nādāsiṃ bahuke dhane    
santesu deyyadhammesu       dīpaṃ nākāsimattano .

Svāhaṃ pacchānutappāmi       attakammaphalūpago    
uddhaṃ catūhi māsehi         kālakiriyā bhavissati .

Ekantaṃ kaṭukaṃ ghoraṃ         nirayaṃ papatissahaṃ    
catukkaṇṇaṃ catudvāraṃ         vibhattaṃ bhāgaso mitaṃ    
ayopākārapariyantaṃ         ayasā paṭikujjitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 185 -->
<a id="page-185"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 185]*

Tassa ayomayā bhūmi         jalitā tejasā yuttā    
samantā yojanasataṃ          pharitvā tiṭṭhati sabbadā .

Tatthāhaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ         dukkhaṃ vedissa vedanaṃ    
phalaṃ pāpassa kammassa        tasmā socāmahaṃ bhusaṃ .

Taṃ vo vadāmi bhaddaṃ [^185-1] vo   yāvantettha samāgatā    
mā kattha pāpakaṃ kammaṃ       āviṃ [^185-2] vā yadi vā raho .

Sace taṃ pāpakaṃ kammaṃ        karissatha karotha vā    
na vo dukkhā pamuttatthi [^185-3]   upacchāpi palāyitaṃ [^185-4]    
matteyyā hotha petteyyā  kule jeṭṭhāpacāyikā    
sāmaññā hotha brahmaññā    evaṃ saggaṃ gamissathāti .

Na [^185-5] antalikkhe na samuddamajjhe    
na pabbatānaṃ vivaraṃ pavissa    
na vijjatī so jagatippadeso    
yatraṭṭhito muñceyya pāpakammāti .

Dhanapālapetavatthu sattamaṃ .

[105] [^185-8] naggo kīso pabbajitosi bhante    
rattiṃ kuhiṃ gacchasi kissa hetu    
ācikkha me taṃ api sakkuṇemu    
sabbena vittaṃ paṭipādaye tuvanti .

#[^185-1] ma. Bhaddante .  [^185-2] ma. Āvi .  [^185-3] ma. Pamutyatthi .  [^185-4] ma. Upaccāpi palāyataṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 186 -->
<a id="page-186"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 186]*

Bārāṇasinagaraṃ dūraghuṭṭhaṃ    
tatthāhaṃ gahapati aḍḍhako ahu [^186-1] dinno    
adātā gedhitamano āmisasmiṃ    
dussīlyena yamavisayamhi patto .

So sūcikāya kilamito tehi teneva    
ñātīsu yāmi āmisakiñcihetu [^186-2]    
adānasīlā na ca saddahanti    
dānaṃ phalaṃ hoti paramhi loke .

Dhītā ca mayhaṃ lapate abhikkhaṇaṃ    
dassāmi dānaṃ pitunnaṃ pitāmahānaṃ    
upaṭṭhitaṃ [^186-3] parivisayanti brāhmaṇā    
yāmihaṃ andhakāvindaṃ bhuttuṃ [^186-4] .

Tamavoca rājā tavamanubhaviyāna [^186-5] tampi    
eyyāsi khippaṃ ahampi karissa pūjaṃ    
ācikkha me taṃ yadi atthi hetu    
saddhāyitaṃ hetuvaco [^186-6] suṇoma .

Tathāti vatvā agamāsi tattha    
bhuñjiṃsu bhattaṃ na padakkhiṇārahā    
#[^186-1] ma. Yu. Dīno .  [^186-2] ma. Āmisakiñcikkhahetu .  [^186-3] ma. Tamupakkhataṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 187 -->
<a id="page-187"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 187]*

Pacchā [^187-1] gamī rājagahaṃ punāparaṃ    
pāturahosi purato janādhipassa .

Disvāna petaṃ punadeva [^187-2] āgataṃ    
rājā avoca ahaṃpi kiṃ dadāmi    
ācikkha me taṃ yadi atthi hetu    
yena tuvaṃ cirataraṃ pīṇito siyā .

Buddhañca saṅghaṃ parivisiyāna rāja    
annena pānenapi cīvarena    
taṃ dakkhiṇaṃ ādisa me hitāya    
evaṃ ahaṃ cirataraṃ pīṇito siyā .

Tato ca rājā nipatitvā tāvadeva [^187-3]    
dānaṃ sahatthā atulañca datvā [^187-4]    
saṅghe ca ārocayi pakatiṃ [^187-5] gatāgatassa    
petassa padakkhiṇaṃ ādisittha .

So pūjito ativiya sobhamāno    
pāturahosi purato janādhipassa    
yakkhohamasmiṃ paramiddhipatto    
na mayhaṃ iddhisamasadisā [^187-6] manussā .

Passānubhāvaṃ aparimitaṃ mamayidaṃ    
#[^187-1] ma. Paccā .  [^187-2] yu. Punareva .  [^187-3] ma. Tāvade .  [^187-4] ma. Daditvā saṅghe .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 188 -->
<a id="page-188"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 188]*

Tayānudiṭṭhaṃ atulaṃ daditvā saṅghe    
saṃtappito sasataṃ sadā bahūhi    
yāmi ahaṃ sukhito manussadevāti .

Cūḷaseṭṭhīpetavatthu aṭṭhamaṃ .

[106] [^188-9] yassa atthāya gacchāma       kambojaṃ dhanahārakā    
ayaṃ kāmadado yakkho        imaṃ yakkhaṃ niyāmase    
imaṃ yakkhaṃ gahetvāna        sādhukena pasayha vā    
yānaṃ āropayitvāna        khippaṃ gacchāma dvārakanti .

Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya       nisīdeyya sayeyya vā    
na tassa sākhaṃ bhañjeyya      mittadubbho hi pāpakoti .

Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya       nisīdeyya sayeyya vā    
khandhampi tassa chindeyya      attho ce tādiso siyāti .

Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya       nisīdeyya sayeyya vā    
na tassa [^188-1] pattaṃ bhindeyya   mittadubbho hi pāpakoti .

Yassa rukkhassa chāyāya       nisīdeyya sayeyya vā    
samūlaṃpi taṃ abbuyha [^188-1]       atthopetādiso siyāti .

Yassekarattiṃ hi [^188-2] ghare vaseyya  
yatthannapānaṃ puriso labhetha  
na tassa pāpaṃ manasāpi cetaye 3  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 189 -->
<a id="page-189"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 189]*

Kataññutā sappurisehi vaṇṇitā .

Yassekarattiṃpi ghare vaseyya    
annena pānena upaṭṭhito siyā    
na tassa pāpaṃ manasāpi cetaye [^189-1]    
adubbhapāṇī dahate mittadubbhiṃ .

Yo pubbe katakalyāṇo      pacchā pāpena hiṃsati    
allapāṇihato poso        na so bhadrāni passatīti .

Yo [^189-2] appaduṭṭhassa narassa dussati    
suddhassa posassa anaṅgaṇassa    
tameva bālaṃ pacceti pāpaṃ    
sukhumo rajo pativātaṃva khittoti [^189-2] .

Nāhaṃ devena vā manussena vā    
issariyena vāhaṃ suppasayho(    
yakkhohamasmi paramiddhipatto    
dūraṅgamo vaṇṇabalūpapannoti .

Pāṇi te sabbaso vaṇṇo     pañcadhāro madhussavo    
nānārasā paggharanti        maññehantaṃ purindadaṃ .

Namhi devo na gandhabbo     napi sakko purindado    
petaṃ aṅkura jānāhi        roruvamhā [^189-3] idhāgataṃ .

#[^189-1] ma. Cintaye .  [^189-2] ma. Yo appaduṭṭhassa .Pe. Khittotīti ime pāṭhā natthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 190 -->
<a id="page-190"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 190]*

Kiṃsīlo kiṃsamācāro         roruvamhi [^190-1] pure tvaṃ    
kena te brahmacariyena      puññaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhati .

Tantavāyo pure āsiṃ       roruvasmiṃ [^190-2] tadā ahaṃ    
sukicchavutti kapaṇo          na me vijjati dātave .

Nivesanañca [^190-3] me āsi     asayhassa upantike    
saddhassa dānapatino         katapuññassa lajjino .

Tattha yācanakā yanti        nānāgottā vaṇibbakā    
te ca maṃ tattha pucchanti      asayhassa nivesanaṃ .

Kattha gacchāma [^190-4] bhaddaṃ vo   tattha [^190-5] dānaṃ padīyati    
tesāhaṃ [^190-6] puṭṭho [^190-7] akkhāmi  asayhassa nivesanaṃ .

Paggayha dakkhiṇaṃ bāhuṃ        ettha gacchatha bhaddaṃ vo    
ettha dānaṃ padīyati [8]    tena pāṇi kāmadado    
tena pāṇi madhussavo        tena me brahmacariyena    
puññaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhati .

Na kira tvaṃ adā dānaṃ       sakapāṇīhi kassaci    
parassa dānaṃ anumodamāno    pāṇiṃ paggayha pāvadi .

Tena pāṇi kāmadado        tena pāṇi madhussavo    
tena me brahmacariyena      puññaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhati .

#[^190-1] ma. Roruvasmiṃ .  [^190-2] yu. Bheruvasmiṃ .  [^190-3] sī. Yu. Āvesanañca .

#[^190-4] yu. Tattha gacchāmi .  [^190-5] ma. Yu. Kattha .  [^190-6] yu. Tenāhaṃ .  [^190-7] yu. Vakkhāmi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 191 -->
<a id="page-191"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 191]*

Yo so dānamadā bhante     pasanno sakapāṇīhi    
so hitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ      kinnu so disataṃ gato .

Nāhaṃ jānāmi asayhāsāhino    
aṅgīrasassa gatiṃ āgatiṃ vā    
sutaṃ ca me vessavaṇassa santike    
sakkassa sahabyataṃ gato asayho .

Alameva kātuṃ kalyāṇaṃ       dānaṃ dātuṃ yathārahaṃ    
pāṇiṃ kāmadadaṃ disvā        ko puññaṃ na karissati .

So hi nūna ito gantvā     anuppatvāna dvārakaṃ    
dānaṃ taṃ [^191-1] paṭṭhāpayissāmi   yaṃ mamassa sukhāvahaṃ .

Dassāmi annapānañca        vatthasenāsanāni ca    
papañca udapānañca          dugge saṅkamanāni cāti .

Kena te aṅgulī kuṇḍā [^191-2]   mukhañca kuṇḍalīkataṃ    
akkhīni ca paggharanti         kiṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ tayāti .

Aṅgīrasassa gahapatino        saddhassa gharamesino    
tassāhaṃ dānavissagge       dāne adhikato ahu .

Tattha yācanake disvā       āgate bhojanatthike    
ekamantaṃ apakkamma         akāsiṃ kuṇḍalīmukhaṃ .

Tena me aṅgulī kuṇḍā      mukhañca kuṇḍalīkataṃ    
akkhīni ca [^191-3] paggharanti      taṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ mayāti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 192 -->
<a id="page-192"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 192]*

Dhammena te kāpurisa        mukhañca kuṇḍalīkataṃ    
akkhīni ca paggharanti         yaṃ tvaṃ parassa dānassa    
akāsi kuṇḍalīmukhanti .

Kathaṃ hi dānaṃ dadamāno       kareyya parapattiyaṃ    
annapānaṃ khādanīyaṃ          vatthasenāsanāni cāti .

So hi nūna ito gantvā     anuppatvāna dvārakaṃ    
dānaṃ taṃ paṭṭhāpayissāmi      yaṃ mamassa sukhāvahaṃ .

Dassāmannañca pānañca       vatthasenāsanāni ca    
papañca udapānañca          dugge saṅkammanāni cāti .

Tato hi so nivattitvā      anuppatvāna dvārakaṃ    
dānaṃ paṭṭhayi aṅkuro        yantaṃ [^192-1] assa sukhāvahaṃ .

Adā annañca pānañca       vatthasenāsanāni ca    
papañca udapānañca          vippasannena cetasā .

Ko chāto ko ca tasito     ko vatthaṃ paridahissati [^192-2]    
kassa santāni yoggāni      ito yojentu vāhanaṃ .

Ko chatticchati gandhañca       ko mālaṃ ko upāhanaṃ    
iti su tattha ghosenti       kappakā sūdamāgadhā    
sadā sāyañca pāto ca      aṅkurassa nivesaneti .

Sukhaṃ supati aṅkuro          iti jānāti maṃ jano  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 193 -->
<a id="page-193"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 193]*

Dukkhaṃ supāmi sindhuka [^193-1]      yaṃ na passāmi yācake .

Sukhaṃ supati aṅkuro          iti jānāti maṃ jano    
dukkhaṃ sindhuka [^193-1] supāmi      appake su vaṇibbaketi .

Sakko ce te varaṃ dajjā    tāvatiṃsānamissaro    
kissa sabbassa lokassa       varamāno varaṃ vareti .

Sakko ce me varaṃ dajjā    tāvatiṃsānamissaro    
kāluṭṭhitassa me sato       suriyuggamanaṃ pati .

Dibbā bhakkhā pātubhaveyyuṃ    sīlavanto ca yācakā    
dadato me na khīyetha        datvā nānutappeyyāhaṃ    
dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādeyya        evaṃ sakka [^193-2] varaṃ vareti .

Na sabbavitat_āni pare pavecche    
dadeyya dānañca dhanañca rakkhe    
tasmā hi dānā dhanameva seyyo    
atippadānena kulā na honti .

Idānamatidānañca           na pasaṃsanti paṇḍitā    
tasmā hi dānā dhanameva seyyo    
samena vatteyya sa dhīradhammoti .

Aho vatāre ahameva dajjaṃ  
santo hi maṃ sappurisā bhajeyyuṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 194 -->
<a id="page-194"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 194]*

Meghova ninnāni hi [^194-1] pūrayanto    
santappaye sabbavaṇibbakānaṃ .

Yassa yācanake disvā       mukhavaṇṇo pasīdati    
datvā attamano hoti       taṃ gharaṃ vasato sukhaṃ .

Yassa yācanake disvā       mukhavaṇṇo pasīdati .

Datvā attamano hoti       esā yaññassa [^194-2] sampadā .

Pubbeva dānā sumano       dadaṃ cittaṃ pasādaye [^194-3]    
datvā attamano hoti       esā [^194-4] yaññassa sampadāti .

Saṭṭhivāhasahassāni          aṅkurassa nivesane    
bhojanaṃ dīyate niccaṃ         puññapekkhassa jantuno .

Tisahassāni sūdāni [^194-5]       āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā    
aṅkuraṃ upajīvanti           dāne yaññassa pāvaṭā .

Saṭṭhipurisasahassāni          āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā    
aṅkurassa mahādāne        kaṭṭhaṃ phālenti māṇavā .

Soḷasitthisahassāni          sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā    
aṅkurassa mahādāne        vidhā piṇḍenti nāriyo .

Soḷasitthisahassāni          sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā    
aṅkurassa mahādāne        dabbiggāhā upaṭṭhitā .

#[^194-1] ma. Paripūrayanto .  [^194-2] sī. Yu. Puññassa .  [^194-3] yu. Pasādeyya .  [^194-4] yu. Eso .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 195 -->
<a id="page-195"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 195]*

Bahuṃ bahūnaṃ pādāsi          ciraṃ pādāsi khattiyo    
sakkaccañca sahatthā ca       vittiṃ katvā punappunaṃ .

Bahumāse ca pakkhe ca       utusaṃvaccharāni ca    
mahādānaṃ pavattesi         aṅkuro dīghamantaraṃ .

Evaṃ datvā yajitvā ca      aṅkuro dīghamantaraṃ    
so hitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ      tāvatiṃsūpago ahūti .

Kaṭacchubhikkhaṃ datvāna         anuruddhassa indako    
so hitvā mānusaṃ dehaṃ      tāvatiṃsūpago ahu .

Dasahi ṭhānehi aṅkuraṃ        indako atirocati    
rūpe sadde rase gandhe     phoṭṭhabbe ca manorame    
āyunā yasasā ceva        vaṇṇena ca sukhena ca    
ādhipaccena aṅkuraṃ         indako atirocatīti .

Mahādānaṃ tayā dinnaṃ        aṅkura dīghanantaraṃ    
avidūre nisinnosi          āgaccha mama santiketi [^195-1] .

Tāvatiṃse yadā buddho       silāyaṃ paṇḍukambale    
pārichattakamūlamhi           vihāsi purisuttamo .

Dasasu lokadhātūsu           sannipatitvāna devatā    
payirupāsanti sambuddhaṃ        vasantaṃ nagamuddhani .

Na koci devo vaṇṇena      sambuddhaṃ atirocati    
sabbe deve atikkamma [^195-2]   sambuddhova virocati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 196 -->
<a id="page-196"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 196]*

Yojanāni dasa ceva         aṅkuroyaṃ tadā ahu    
avidūreva buddhassa          indako atirocati .

Oloketvāna sambuddho     aṅkurañcāpi indakaṃ    
dakkhiṇeyyaṃ sambhāvento [^196-1]  idaṃ vacanamabrūvi [^196-2]    
mahādānaṃ tayā dinnaṃ        aṅkura dīghamantaraṃ    
atidūre nisinnosi          āgaccha mama santikaṃ [^196-3] (iti) .

Codito bhāvitattena        aṅkuro idamabrūvi    
kiṃ mayhaṃ tena dānena       dakkhiṇeyyena suññataṃ .

Ayaṃ so indako yakkho      dajjā dānaṃ parittakaṃ    
atirocati amhehi          cando tāragaṇe yathā (iti) .

Ujjaṅgale [^196-4] yathā khette  bījaṃ bahahapi ropitaṃ    
na phalaṃ vipulaṃ hoti [^196-5]       napi toseti kassakaṃ    
tatheva dānaṃ bahukaṃ          dussīlesu patiṭṭhitaṃ    
na phalaṃ vipulaṃ hoti [^196-5]       napi toseti dāyakaṃ .

Yathāpi bhaddake khette      bījaṃ appampi ropitaṃ    
sammādhāraṃ pavecchante      phalaṃ toseti kassakaṃ    
tatheva sīlavantesu          guṇavantesu tādisu    
appakaṃpi kataṃ kāraṃ          puññaṃ hoti mahapphalanti .

Viceyya dānaṃ dātabbaṃ       yattha dinnaṃ mahapphalaṃ    
#[^196-1] yu. Pabhāvento .  [^196-2] ma. Vacanamabravi .  [^196-3] ma. Santiketi .  [^196-4] yu. Ujjhaṅgale .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 197 -->
<a id="page-197"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 197]*

viceyya dānaṃ datvāna       saggaṃ gacchanti dāyakā .

Viceyya dānaṃ sugatappasaṭṭhaṃ    
ye dakkhiṇeyyā idha jīvaloke    
etesu dinnāni mahapphalāni    
bījāni vuttāni yathā sukhetteti .

Aṅkurapetavatthu navamaṃ .

[107] 10 divāvihāragataṃ bhikkhuṃ         gaṅgātīre nisinnakaṃ    
taṃ petī upasaṅkamma         dubbaṇṇā bhīrudassanā    
kesā cassā atidīghā       yāva bhummāvalambare    
kesehi sā paṭicchannā      samaṇaṃ etamabrūvīti .

Pañcapaṇṇāsavassāni         yato kālakatā ahaṃ    
nābhijānāmi bhuttaṃ vā       pītaṃ vā pana pānīyaṃ    
dehi tvaṃ pānīyaṃ bhante      tasitā pānīyāya meti .

Ayaṃ sītodakā gaṅgā        himavantato sandati    
piva etto gahetvāna      kiṃ maṃ yācasi pānīyaṃ (iti) .

Sacāhaṃ bhante gaṅgāya       sayaṃ gaṇhāmi pānīyaṃ    
lohitaṃ me parivattati        tasmā yācāmi pānīyaṃ (iti) .

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         gaṅgā te hoti lohitaṃ (iti) .

Putto me uttaro nāma  saddho āsi upāsako  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 198 -->
<a id="page-198"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 198]*

So ca mayhaṃ akāmāya       samaṇānaṃ pavecchati    
cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañca          paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ .

Tamahaṃ paribhāsāmi           maccherena upaddutā    
yantaṃ mayhaṃ akāmāya        samaṇānaṃ pavecchasi    
cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañca          paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ .

Etante paralokasmiṃ        lohitaṃ hotu uttara    
tassa kammavipākena         gaṅgā me hoti lohitanti .

Uttaramātupetivatthu dasamaṃ .

[108] 1[^198-1] ahaṃ pure pabbajitassa bhikkhuno    
suttaṃ adāsi upagamma yācitā tassa    
vipāko vipulaṃ phalūpalabbhati    
bahū [^198-1] ca me uppajjare vatthakoṭiyo .

Pupphābhikiṇṇaṃ ramitaṃ vimānaṃ    
anekacittaṃ naranārisevitaṃ    
sāhaṃ bhuñjāmi ca pārupāmi ca    
pahūtavittā na ca tāva khīyati .

Tasseva kammassa vipākamanvyā [^198-2]  
sukhañca sātañca idhūpalabbhati  
sāhaṃ gantvā punadeva mānusaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 199 -->
<a id="page-199"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 199]*

Kāhāmi puññāni nayayyaputta manti .

Satta tuvaṃ vassasatā idhāgatā    
jiṇṇā ca vuḍḍhā ca tahiṃ bhavissasi    
sabbeva te kālakatā ca ñātakā    
tvaṃ [^199-1] tattha gantvāna ito karissasīti .

Satteva vassāni idhāgatāya me    
dibbañca sukhañca samappitāya    
sāhaṃ gantvā punareva mānusaṃ    
kāhāmi puññāni nayayyaputta manti .

So taṃ gahetvāna pasayha bāhāyaṃ    
paccānayitvāna punareva [^199-2] theriṃ sudubbalaṃ    
vajjesi aññaṃpi janaṃ idhāgataṃ    
karotha puññāni sukhūpalabbhatīti .

Diṭṭhā mayā akatena sādhunā    
petā vihaññanti tatheva mānusā    
kammañca katvā sukhavedanīyaṃ    
devā manussā ca sukhe ṭhitā pajāti .

Suttapetavatthu ekādasamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 200 -->
<a id="page-200"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 200]*

[109] 1[^200-2] sovaṇṇasopāṇaphalakā        sovaṇṇavālukasanthatā [^200-1]    
tattha sogandhiyā vaggū       sucigandhā manoramā    
nānārukkhehi sañchannā      nānāgandhasameritā    
nānāpadumasañchannā         puṇḍarīkasamohatā [^200-2] .

Surabhī sampavāyanti          manuññā māluteritā    
haṃsakoñcābhirudā ca         cakkavākābhikujitā    
nānādijagaṇākiṇṇā         nānāsaragaṇāyutā    
nānāphaladharā rukkhā        nānāmāladharā [^200-3] vanā .

Na manussesu īdisaṃ          nagaraṃ yādisaṃ idaṃ    
pāsādā ca bahukā tuyhaṃ     sovaṇṇarūpiyamayā    
daddallamānā ābhenti      samantā caturo disā .

Pañca dāsīsatā tuyhaṃ        yā temā paricārikā    
tā kambukāyūradharā         kañcanācelabhūsitā [^200-4] .

Pallaṅkā bahukā tuyhaṃ       sovaṇṇarūpiyamayā [^200-5]    
kadalīmigasañchannā           sajjā [^200-6] goṇakasanthatā    
yattha tuvaṃ vāsupagatā        sabbakāmasamiddhinī .

Sampattāya aḍḍharattāya      tato uṭṭhāya gacchasi    
uyyānabhūmiṃ gantvāna        pokkharaññā samantato .

#[^200-1] yu. ... Saṇṭhitā .  [^200-2] yu. ... Samāgatā .  [^200-3] ma. Nānāpupphadhārā .

#[^200-4] ma. Kañcanāveḷabhūsitā [^200-5] yu. Ruciyāmayā .  [^200-6] yu. Sañjāto  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 201 -->
<a id="page-201"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 201]*

Tassā tīre tuvaṃṭhāsi        harite saddale subhe    
tato te kaṇṇamuṇḍo ca      sunakho aṅgamaṅgāni khādati .

Yadā ca khāyitā āsi       aṭṭhisaṅkhalikā katā    
ogāhasi pokkharaṇiṃ         hoti kāyo yathā pure .

Tato tvaṃ aṅgapaccaṅgī [^201-1]    sucārū piyadassanā    
vatthena pārupitvāna        āyāsi mama santikaṃ .

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         kaṇṇamuṇḍo ca sunakho    
aṅgamaṅgāni khādatīti .

Kimbilāyaṃ gahapati           saddho āsi upāsako    
tassāhaṃ bhariyā āsi        dussīlā aticārinī .

Evamāticaramānāya [^201-2]      sāmiko etadabrūvi [^201-3]    
netaṃ channaṃ paṭirūpaṃ          yaṃ tvaṃ aticarāsi maṃ .

Sāhaṃ ghorañca sapathaṃ         musāvādaṃ abhāsissaṃ    
nāhantaṃ aticarāmi          kāyena uda cetasā    
sacāhantaṃ aticarāmi         kāyena uda cetasā    
ayaṃ kaṇṇamuṇḍo sunakho       aṅgamaṅgāni khādatu .

Tassa kammassa vipākaṃ        musāvādassa cūbhayaṃ    
sattavassasatāni [^201-4] ca       anubhataṃ yatopi me    
#[^201-1] uggacchantī . Ma. Aṅgapaccaṅgā .  [^201-2] ma. So maṃ aticaramānāya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 202 -->
<a id="page-202"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 202]*

Kaṇṇamuṇḍo ca sunakho        aṅgamaṅgāni khādatīti .

Tvañca deva bahūpakāro      atthāya me idhāgato    
sumuttāhaṃ kaṇṇamuṇḍassa       asokā akutobhayā    
nāhaṃ deva namassāmi        yācāmi añjalīkatā    
bhuñja amānuse kāme       rama deva mayā sahāti .

Bhuttā [^202-1] amānusā kāmā   ramitomhi tayā saha    
tāhaṃ subhage yācāmi        khippaṃ paṭinayāhi manti .

Kaṇṇamuṇḍapetavatthu dvādasamaṃ .

[110] 1[^202-3] ahu rājā brahmadatto      pañcālānaṃ rathesabho    
ahorattānamaccayā         rājā kālamakrubbatha [^202-2] .

Tassa āḷāhanaṃ gantvā      bhariyā kandati ubbarī    
brahmadattaṃ apassantī        brahmadattāti kandati .

Isi ca tattha āgacchi        sampannacaraṇamuni    
so ca tattha apucchittha       ye tattha su samāgatā    
kassa cidaṃ āḷāhanaṃ         nānāgandhasameritaṃ    
kassāyaṃ kandati bhariyā       ito dūragataṃ patiṃ    
brahmadattaṃ apassantī        brahmadattāti kandati .

Te ca tattha viyākaṃsu        ye tattha su samāgatā  
brahmadattassa bhadante       brahmadattassa mārisa  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 203 -->
<a id="page-203"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 203]*

Tassa idaṃ āḷāhanaṃ         nānāgandhasameritaṃ    
tassāyaṃ kandati bhariyā       ito dūragataṃ patiṃ    
brahmadattaṃ apassantī        brahmadattāti kandatīti .

Chaḷāsītisahassāni           brahmadattassa nāmakā    
imasmiṃ āḷāhane daḍḍhā     tesaṃ kaṃ anusocasīti .

Yo rājā cūḷanīputto       pañcālānaṃ rathesabho    
taṃ bhante anusocāmi        bhattāraṃ sabbakāmadanti .

Sabbevahesuṃ rājāno       brahmadattassa nāmakā    
sabbeva cūḷanīputtā         pañcālānaṃ rathesabhā    
sabbesaṃ anupubbena         mahesittaṃ akārayi    
kasmā purimake hitvā       pacchimaṃ anusocasīti .

Ātume itthibhūtāya         dīgharattāya mārisa    
yassā me itthibhūtāya       saṃsāre bahu bhāsasīti .

Ahu itthī ahu puriso        pasuyoniṃpi [^203-1] āgamā    
evametaṃ atītānaṃ          pariyanto na dissatīti .

Ādittaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ        ghatasittaṃva pāvakaṃ    
vārinā viya osiñcaṃ        sabbaṃ nibbāpaye daraṃ    
abbūḷhi [^203-2] vata me sallaṃ    sokaṃ hadayanissitaṃ    
yo me sokaparetāya       patisokaṃ apānudi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 204 -->
<a id="page-204"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 204]*

Sāhaṃ abbūḷhasallāsmi       sītibhūtāsmi nibbutā    
na socāmi na rodāmi       tava sutvā mahāmunīti .

Tassa taṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        samaṇassa subhāsitaṃ    
pattacīvaramādāya           pabbaji anagāriyaṃ    
sā ca pabbajitā santā      agārasmā anagāriyaṃ    
mettaṃ cittaṃ abhāvesi       brahmalokupapattiyā    
gāmā gāmaṃ vicarantī        nigame rājadhāniyo .

Uruvelaṃ [^204-1] nāma so gāmo  yattha kālamakrubbatha    
mettacittaṃ ābhāvetvā [^204-2]  brahmalokupapattiyā    
itthīcittaṃ virājetvā       brahmalokupagā ahūti .

Ubbarīpetavatthu terasamaṃ .

Uddānaṃ .

Paṇḍumātā [^204-3] patiyā ca     nandi kuṇḍalinā ghato    
dveseṭṭhī tantavāyo [^204-4]    vihārasuttasovānaübbarīti [^204-5] .

Ubbarīvaggo dutiyo .

tatiyo cūḷavaggo    
[111] [^204-1] abhijjamāne vārimhi        gaṅgāya idha gacchasi    
naggo pubbaḍḍhapetova       mālādhārī alaṅkato    
kuhiṃ gamissasi peto [^204-6]      kattha vāso bhavissatīti .

#[^204-1] ma. Uruveḷā .  [^204-2] yu. Abhāvetvā .  [^204-3] ma. Mocakaṃ mātā mattā ca nandā .

#[^204-4] ma. Tunnavāyo ca uttarasuttakaṇṇaübbarīti .  [^204-5] idaṃ uddānaṃ na dissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 205 -->
<a id="page-205"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 205]*

Cundaṭṭhilaṃ [^205-1] gamissāmi      peto so iti bhāsati [^205-2]    
antare vāsabhagāmaṃ         bārāṇasiyā [^205-3] va santike .

Tañca disvā mahāmatto      koliyo iti vissuto    
sattubhattañca petassa        pītakañca yugaṃ adā .

Nāvāya tiṭṭhamānāya        kappakassa adāpayi    
kappakassa padinnamhi         ṭhāne petassa dissatha .

Tato suvatthavasano          mālādhārī alaṅkato    
ṭhāne ṭhitassa petassa       dakkhiṇā upakappatha    
tasmā dajjetha petānaṃ      anukampāya punappunanti .

Sāhunnavāsino [^205-4] eke    aññe kesanivāsino [^205-5]    
petā bhattāya gacchanti      pakkamanti diso disaṃ .

Dūrepeke [^205-6] padhāvitvā    aladdhā ca nivattare    
chātā pamucchitā gantvā [^205-7]  bhūmiyaṃ paṭisumbhitā    
keci tattha ca patitā [^205-8]     bhūmiyaṃ paṭisumbhitā    
pubbe akatakalyāṇā        aggidaḍḍhāva ātape .

Mayaṃ [^205-9] pubbe pāpadhammā    gharaṇī kulamātaro    
santesu deyyadhammesu       dīpaṃ nākamha attano .

#[^205-1] yu. Cundatthiyaṃ .  [^205-2] yu. Bhāsasi .  [^205-3] ma. Bārāṇasiṃ ca ... .  [^205-4] ma. Sātunna ... .

#[^205-5] ma. ... Vāsinā .  [^205-6] ma. Yu. Dūre eke .  [^205-7] ma. Yu. Bhantā .  [^205-8] ma. Te ca tattha  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 206 -->
<a id="page-206"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 206]*

Pahūtaṃ annapānaṃ hi          apissu [^206-1] avakirīyati    
samaggate [^206-2] pabbajite      na ca kiñci adamhase .

Akammakāmā alasā         sādhukāmā mahagghasā    
ālopapiṇḍadātāro        paṭiggahe paribhāsitā [^206-3] .

Te gharā tā va dāsiyo     tānevābharaṇāni no    
te aññe paricārenti [^206-4]   mayaṃ dukkhassa bhāgino    
veṇiṃ vā avaññā honti     rathakārī ca dubbhikā    
caṇḍālī kapaṇā honti       kappakā [^206-6] ca punappunaṃ .

Yāni yāni nihīnāni         kulāni kapaṇāni ca    
tesu tesveva jāyanti      esā maccharino gati .

Pubbe ca katakalyāṇā       dāyakā vītamaccharā    
saggante paripūrenti        obhāsenti ca nandanaṃ .

Vejayante ca pāsāde      ramitvā kāmakāmino    
uccākulesu jāyanti        sabhogesu tato cutā .

Kūṭāgāre ca pāsāde      pallaṅke goṇakatthate [^206-7]    
vījitaṅgā morahatthehi       kule jātā yasassino .

Aṅgato aṅgaṃ [^206-8] gacchanti    mālādhārī alaṅkatā    
jātiyo [^206-9] upatiṭṭhanti      sāyaṃ pātaṃ sukhesino .

#[^206-1] yu. Apisu .  [^206-2] yu. Samāgate .  [^206-3] ma. Yu. Paribhāsimhase .  [^206-4] yu. Parihārenti .

#[^206-5] ma. Veṇī .  [^206-6] yu. Nhāminī .  [^206-7] yu. Goṇasaṇṭhite .  [^206-8] yu. Aṅkato aṅkaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 207 -->
<a id="page-207"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 207]*

Nayidaṃ akatapuññānaṃ          katapuññānamevidaṃ    
asokaṃ nandanaṃ rammaṃ         tidasānaṃ mahāvanaṃ    
sukhaṃ akatapuññānaṃ           idha natthi parattha ca    
sukhañca katapuññānaṃ          idha ceva parattha ca .

Tesaṃ sahabyakāmānaṃ         kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ bahuṃ    
katapuññā hi modanti        sagge bhogasamaṅginoti .

Abhijjamānapetavatthu paṭhamaṃ .

[112] [^207-2] kuṇḍinagariyo thero         sānavāsinivāsiko [^207-1]    
poṭṭhapādoti nāmena       samaṇo bhāvitindriyo .

Tassa mātā pitā bhātā     duggatā yamalokikā    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatā .

Te duggatā sūcikaṭṭhā       kilantā naggino kisā    
ottappantā [^207-2] mahattāsā  na dessanti kurūrino .

Tassa bhātā vitaritvā       naggo ekapathekako    
catukuṇḍiko bhavitvāna        therassa dassayitumaṃ .

Thero cāmanasikatvā        tuṇhībhūto atikkami [^207-3]    
so ca viññāpayi theraṃ       bhātā petaṃ [^207-4] gato ahaṃ .

Mātā pitā ca te bhante    duggatā yamalokikā    
#[^207-1] yu. Sānuvāsinivāsino .  [^207-2] ma. Yu. Uttasantā mahātāsā .  [^207-3] yu. Apakkami .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 208 -->
<a id="page-208"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 208]*

Pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatā    
te duggatā sūcikaṭṭhā       kilantā naggino kisā    
ottappantā [^208-1] mahattāsā  na dessanti kurūrino .

Anukampassu kāruṇiko        datvā anvādisāhi no    
tava dinnena dānena        yāpessanti kurūrinoti .

Thero caritvā piṇḍāya      bhikkhū aññe ca dvādasa    
ekajjhaṃ sannipatiṃsu          bhattavissaggakāraṇā .

Thero sabbepi te āha     yathā laddhaṃ dadātha me    
saṅghabhattaṃ karissāmi         anukampāya ñātīnaṃ .

Niyyādayiṃsu [^208-2] therassa      thero saṅghaṃ nimantayi    
datvā anvādisi thero      pitu mātu ca bhātuno    
idaṃ me ñātīnaṃ hotu        sukhitā hontu ñātayo .

Samanantarānudiṭṭhe          bhojanaṃ upapajjatha    
suciṃ paṇītaṃ sampannaṃ          anekarasabyañjanaṃ    
tato uddissati [^208-3] bhātā    vaṇṇavā balavā sukhī .

Pahūtaṃ bhojanaṃ bhante         passa naggamhase mayaṃ    
tathā bhante parakkamma [^208-4]    yathā vatthaṃ labhāmhase .

Thero saṅkārakūṭato        uccinitvāna nantake    
#[^208-1] yu. Uttasantā mahātāsā .  [^208-2] yu. Niyyātayiṃsu .  [^208-3] ma. Uddassayī .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 209 -->
<a id="page-209"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 209]*

Pilotikaṃ paṭaṃ katvā         saṅghe cātuddise adā .

Datvā anvādisi thero      pitu mātu ca bhātuno    
idaṃ me ñātīnaṃ hotu        sukhitā hontu ñātayo .

Samanantarānudiṭṭhe          vatthāni upapajjiṃsu [^209-1]    
tato suvatthavasano          therassa dassayītumaṃ .

Vaṇṇavā balavā sukhī [^209-2]      yāvatā nandarājassa    
vijitasmiṃ paṭicchādā         tato bahuttarā bhante .

Vatthānicchādanāni no       koseyyakambalīyāni    
khomakappāsikāni ca         vipulā ca mahagghā ca    
te cākāsevalambare [^209-3]    te mayaṃ paridahāma    
yaṃ yaṃ hi manaso piyaṃ    
tathā bhante parakkāma       yathā gehaṃ labhāmase .

Thero paṇṇakuṭiṃ katvā       saṅghe cātuddise adā .

Datvā ca [^209-4] uddisi thero   pitu mātu ca bhātuno    
idaṃ me ñātīnaṃ hotu        sukhitā hontu ñātayo .

Samanantarānudiṭṭhe          gharāni upapajjiṃsu [^209-1]    
kūṭāgārā nivesanā        vibhattā bhāgaso mitā .

Na manussesu īdisā         yādisā no gharā idha    
api dibbesu yādisā        tādisā no gharā idha    
#[^209-1] ma. Udapajjisuṃ .  [^209-2] ma. Yu. Vaṇṇavā ... Sukhīti ime pāṭhā natthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 210 -->
<a id="page-210"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 210]*

Daddallamānā ābhenti      samantā caturo disā .

Tathā bhante parakkama        yathā pānaṃ labhāmase .

Thero karakaṃ [^210-1] pūretvā    saṅghe cātuddise adā .

Datvā anvādisi thero      pitu mātu ca bhātuno    
idaṃ me ñātīnaṃ hotu        sukhitā hontu ñātayo .

Samanantarānudiṭṭhe          pānīyaṃ upapajjatha [^210-2]    
gambhīrā caturassā ca        pokkharaññā [^210-3] sunimmitā    
sītudakā supatitthā ca        sītā appaṭigandhiyā    
padumuppalasañchannā          vārikiñjakkhapūritā .

Tattha nahātvā pivitvā      therassa paṭidassayuṃ    
pahūtaṃ pānīyaṃ bhante         pāpā [^210-4] dukkhaphalanti no    
āhiṇḍamānā khañjāma       sakkhare kusakaṇṭake    
tathā bhante parakkama        yathā yānaṃ labhāmase .

Thero sipāṭikaṃ laddhā       saṅghe cātuddise adā .

Datvā anvādisi thero      pitu mātu ca bhātuno    
idaṃ me ñātīnaṃ hotu        sukhitā hontu ñātayo .

Samanantarānudiṭṭhe          petā rathena māgamama    
anukampitamha bhadante        bhattena chādanena ca    
gharena pānīyadānena        yānadānena cūbhayaṃ    
muniṃ kāruṇikaṃ loke         taṃ [^210-5] bhante vanditumāgatāti .

Sānuvāsittherapetavatthu [^210-6] dutiyaṃ .

#[^210-1] ma. Karaṇaṃ .  [^210-2] ma. Udapajjatha .  [^210-3] pokkharañño .  [^210-4] ma. Pādā dukkhā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 211 -->
<a id="page-211"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 211]*

[113] [^211-3] veḷuriyatthambhaṃ ruciraṃ pabhassaraṃ    
vimānamāruyha anekacittaṃ    
tatthacchasi devi mahānubhāve    
pathaddhani paṇṇaraseva cando .

Vaṇṇo ca te kanakassa sannibho    
uttamarūpo [^211-1] bhusadassaneyyo    
pallaṅkaseṭṭhe atule nisinnā    
ekā tuvaṃ natthi tuyhaṃ sāmiko .

Imā ca te pokkharaññā samantato [^211-2]    
pahūtamāsā [^211-3] bahupuṇḍarīkā    
sovaṇṇacuṇṇehi samantamotatā [^211-4]    
na tattha paṅko paṇṇako [^211-5] ca vijjati .

Haṃsāpime dassanīyā manoramā    
udakasmiṃ anupariyanti sabbadā    
samayya vaggūpanadanti sabbe    
bindussarā [^211-6] dundubhīnaṃ va ghoso .

Daddallamānā yasasā yasassinī    
nāvāya ca tvaṃ avalamba tiṭṭhasi    
#[^211-1] yu. Uggatarūpo . Ma. Uttatta rūpo .  [^211-2] ma. Pokkharaṇī samantā . Yu. Samaṅgato .

#[^211-3] ma. Pahūtamalyā .  [^211-4] ma. Samantamotthatā . Yu. Suvaṇṇacuṇṇehi samaṅgamotakā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 212 -->
<a id="page-212"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 212]*

Āḷārapamhe hasite piyaṃvade    
sabbaṅgakalyāṇi bhusaṃ virocasi .

Idaṃ vimānaṃ virajaṃ sameṭṭhitaṃ    
uyyānavanaṃ [^212-1] ratinandavaḍḍhanaṃ    
icchāmi te [^212-2] nāri anomadassane    
tayā saha nandane idha moditunti .

Karohi kammaṃ idha vedanīyaṃ    
cittañca te idha nitañca [^212-3] hotu    
katvāna kammaṃ idha modanīyaṃ [^212-4]    
evaṃ mamaṃ lacchasi kāmakāmininti .

Sādhūti so tassā paṭisuṇitvā    
akāsi kammaṃ tahiṃ [^212-5] vedanīyaṃ    
katvāna kammaṃ tahiṃ vedanīyaṃ    
uppajji [^212-6] māṇavo tassā sahabyatanti .

Rathakārīpetavatthu tatiyaṃ .

Bhāṇavāraṃ dutiyaṃ .

[114] [^212-4] bhūsāni eko [^212-7] sāliṃ punāparo    
ayañca [^212-8] nārī sakamaṃsalohitaṃ    
#[^212-1] yu. Uyyānavantaṃ .  [^212-2] yu. Icchāmahaṃ .  [^212-3] ma. Natañca . Yu. Nītaṃ bhavatu .

#[^212-4] ma. Vedanīyaṃ .  [^212-5] yu. Sahavedanīyaṃ .  [^212-6] ma. Upapajji so ... .  [^212-7] yu. Eke ...


<!-- Siam Rath Page 213 -->
<a id="page-213"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 213]*

Tvañca gūthaṃ asuciṃ [^213-1] akantaṃ    
paribhuñjasi kissa ayaṃ vipākoti .

Ayaṃ pure mātaraṃ hiṃsati       ayaṃ pana kūṭavāṇijo    
ayaṃ maṃsāni khāditvā        musāvādena vañceti .

Ahaṃ manussesu manussabhūtā    
agārinī sabbakulassa issarā .

Santesu parigūyhāmi         mā ca kiñci ito adaṃ    
musāvādena chādemi        natthi etaṃ mama gehe    
sace santaṃ parigūyhāmi       gūthaṃ [^213-2] me hotu bhojanaṃ .

Tassa kammavipākena         musāvādassa cūbhayaṃ    
sugandhasālino bhattaṃ         gūthaṃ me parivattati .

Avajjāni [^213-3] ca kammāni     na hi kammaṃ vinassati    
duggandhaṃ kimīnaṃ mīḷhaṃ         bhuñjāmi ca pivāmi cāti .

Bhusapetavatthu catutthaṃ .

[115] [^213-5] accherarūpaṃ sugatassa ñāṇaṃ    
satthā yathā puggalaṃ byākāsi    
ussannapuññāpi bhavanti heke    
parittapuññāpi bhavanti heke .

Ayaṃ kumāro sīvathikāya chaḍḍito  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 214 -->
<a id="page-214"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 214]*

Aṅguṭṭhasnehena yāpesi rattiṃ .

Na yakkhabhūtā na sirisapā [^214-1] vā    
viheṭhayeyyuṃ katapuññakumāraṃ    
sunakhāpi imassa palihiṃsu pāde    
dhaṅkā siṅgālā parivattayanti .

Gabbhāsayaṃ pakkhigaṇā haranti    
kākā pana akkhimalaṃ haranti    
na imassa rakkhaṃ vidahiṃsu keci    
na osathaṃ sāsapadhūpanaṃ vā .

Nakkhattayogaṃ pana [^214-2] uggahesuṃ    
na sabbadhaññānipi ākiriṃsu    
etādisaṃ uttamakicchapattaṃ    
rattābhataṃ sīvathikāya chaḍḍitaṃ .

Navanītapiṇḍaṃ [^214-3] viya vedhamānaṃ    
sasaṃsayaṃ jīvitasāvasesaṃ    
tamaddasa devamanussapūjito    
disvā pana [^214-4] byākari bhūripañño    
ayaṃ kumāro nagarassimassa    
aggakuliko bhavissati bhogavā [^214-5] ca    
#[^214-1] yu. Siriṃsapā . Ma. Sarīsapā .  [^214-2] yu. Pi na .  [^214-3] ma. Nonītapiṇḍaṃva pavedhamānaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 215 -->
<a id="page-215"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 215]*

Kissa vataṃ kiṃ pana brahmacariyaṃ    
kissa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipāko    
etādisaṃ byasanaṃ pāpuṇitvā    
taṃ tādisaṃ paccanubhossatiddhinti .

Buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa    
pūjaṃ akāsi janatā uḷāraṃ    
tatrassa cittassa ahu aññathattaṃ    
vācaṃ abhāsi pharusaṃ asabbhaṃ    
so taṃ vitakkaṃ paṭivinodayitvā    
pītipasādaṃ paṭiladdhā pacchā    
tathāgataṃ jetavane vasantaṃ    
yāguyā upaṭṭhāsi so [^215-1] sattarattaṃ .

Tassa vataṃ taṃ pana brahmacariyaṃ    
tassa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipāko    
etādisaṃ byasanaṃ pāpuṇitvā    
taṃ tādisaṃ paccanubhossatiddhiṃ .

Ṭhatvāna so vassasataṃ idheva    
sabbehi kāmehi samaṅgibhūto    
kāyassa bhedā abhisamparāyaṃ    
sahabyataṃ gacchati vāsavassāti [^215-2] .

Kumārapetavatthu pañcamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 216 -->
<a id="page-216"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 216]*

[116] [^216-6] naggā dubbaṇṇarūpāsi        kīsā dhamanisanthatā [^216-1]    
upphāsulike kīsike         kā nu tvaṃ idha tiṭṭhasati .

Ahaṃ bhadante petīmhi        duggatā yamalokikā    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatā (ti) .

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         petalokaṃ ito gatā (ti) .

Anavajjesu titthesu         vicini aḍḍhamāsakaṃ    
santesu deyyadhammesu       dīpaṃ nākāsimattano .

Nadiṃ upemi tasitā          rittakā parivattati    
chāyaṃ upemi uṇhesu        ātapo parivattati .

Aggivaṇṇo ca me vāto     ḍahanto upavāyati    
etañca bhante arahāmi      aññañca pāpakaṃ tato .

Gantvāna hatthinīpuraṃ [^216-2]      vajjesi mayhaṃ mātaraṃ    
dhītā ca te mayā diṭṭhā     duggatā yamalokikā    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatā .

Atthi ca me ettha nikkhittaṃ   anakkhātañca taṃ mayā    
cattāri satasahassāni        pallaṅkassa ca heṭṭhato .

Tato me dānaṃ dadātu       tassā ca hotu jīvikā    
dānaṃ datvā ca me mātā    dakkhiṇaṃ anvādissatu [^216-3] me [^216-4]    
#[^216-1] yu. ...Saṇṭhitā .  [^216-2] ma. Hatthiniṃ puraṃ . Evamuparipi . Yu. Hastinīpuraṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 217 -->
<a id="page-217"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 217]*

Tadāhaṃ sukhitā hessaṃ         sabbakāmasamiddhinīti .

Sādhūti so tassā [^217-1] paṭisuṇitvā  gantvāna hatthinīpuraṃ [^217-2]    
tassā avoca mātaraṃ         dhītā ca te mayā diṭṭhā    
duggatā yamalokikā          pāpakammaṃ karitvāna    
petalokaṃ ito gatā         sā maṃ tattha samādapesi .

Gantvāna hatthinīpuraṃ [^217-2]       vajjesi mayhaṃ mātaraṃ    
dhītā ca te mayā diṭṭhā      duggatā yamalokikā    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna          petalokaṃ ito gatā .

Atthi ca me ettha nikkhittaṃ    anakkhātañca taṃ mayā    
cattāri satasahassāni         pallaṅkassa ca heṭṭhato .

Tato me dānaṃ dadātu        tassā ca hotu jīvikā    
dānaṃ datvā ca me mātā     dakkhiṇaṃ anvādissatu me    
tadāhaṃ sukhitā hessaṃ         sabbakāmasamiddhinīti .

Tato hi sā dānaṃ adāsi [^217-3]   datvā ca [^217-4] tassā dakkhiṇamādisi    
petī ca sukhitā āsi         tassā cāpi sujīvikāti .

Serinīpetavatthu chaṭṭhaṃ .

[117] [^217-7] naranārīpurakkhato yuvā      rajanīye kāmaguṇehi [^217-5] sobhasi    
divasaṃ anubhosi kāraṇaṃ       kiṃ akāsi purimāya jātiyāti .

Ahaṃ rājagahe ramme       ramaṇīye giribbaje    
#[^217-1] ma. Ayaṃ pāṭho natthi .  [^217-2] ma. Gantvāna hatthinīpuranti dve pāṭhā natthi .

#yu. Hastinīpuraṃ .  [^217-3] ma dānamadā .  [^217-4] ma. Datvā cāti ayaṃ pāṭho natthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 218 -->
<a id="page-218"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 218]*

Migaluddo pure āsiṃ        lohitapāṇī dāruṇo .

Avirodhakaresu pāṇīsu        puthusattesu [^218-1] paduṭṭhamānaso    
vicari [^218-2] atidāruṇo sadā    parahiṃsāya rato asaññato .

Tassa me sahāyo suhadayo    saddho āsi upāsako    
so hi [^218-3] maṃ anukampanto    nivāresi punappunaṃ    
mākāsi pāpakaṃ kammaṃ        mā tāta duggatiṃ agā    
sace icchasi pecca sukhaṃ      virama pāṇavadhaṃ asaṃyamaṃ [^218-4] .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        sukhakāmassa hitānukampino    
nākāsiṃ sakalānusāsaniṃ       cirapāpābhirato abuddhimā .

So maṃ puna bhūrisumedhaso      anukampāya saṃyame nivesayi    
sace divā hanasi pāṇino     atha te rattiṃ bhavatu saṃyamo .

Svāhaṃ divā hanitvāna pāṇino virato rattiṃ ahosi saṃyato    
rattāhaṃ parihāremi [^218-5]      divā khajjāmi duggato .

Tassa kammassa kusalassa       anubhomi rattiṃ amānusiṃ    
divā paṭihatāva kukkurā      upadhāvanti samantā khādituṃ    
ye ca te satatānuyogino    dhuvaṃ payuttā sugatassa sāsane    
maññāmi te amatameva       kevalaṃ adhigacchanti    
padaṃ asaṅkhatanti .

Migaluddapetavatthu sattamaṃ .

#[^218-1] yu. Puthusantesu .  [^218-2] ma. Vicariṃ .  [^218-3] ma. Pi . Yu. Ca .  [^218-4] ma. Pāṇavadhā asaṃyamā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 219 -->
<a id="page-219"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 219]*

[118] [^219-8] kūṭāgāre ca pāsāde      pallaṅke goṇasaṇṭhite [^219-1]    
pañcaṅgikena turiyena        ramasi suppavādite .

Tato ratyā vivasāne [^219-2]    suriyuggamanaṃ [^219-3] pati    
apaviṭṭho [^219-4] susānasmiṃ      bahuṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchasi .

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchasīti .

Ahaṃ rājagahe ramme        ramaṇīye giribbaje    
migaluddo pure āsiṃ        luddo āsiṃ asaññato .

Tassa me sahāyo suhadayo    saddho āsi upāsako    
tassa kulupako bhikkhu         āsi gotamasāvako .

So hi maṃ anukampanto       nivāresi punappunaṃ    
mākāsi pāpakaṃ kammaṃ        mā tāta duggatiṃ agā    
sace icchasi pecca sukhaṃ      virama pāṇavadhaṃ asaṃyamaṃ .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        sukhakāmassa hitānukampino    
nākāsiṃ sakalānusāsaniṃ       cirapāpābhirato abuddhimā .

So maṃ puna bhūrisumedhaso      anukampāya saṃyame nivesayi    
sace divā hanasi pāṇino     atha te rattiṃ bhavatu saṃyamo .

Svāhaṃ divā hanitvā pāṇino  virato rattiṃ ahosi saṃyato    
rattāhaṃ parihāremi         divā khajjāmi duggato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 220 -->
<a id="page-220"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 220]*

Tassa kammassa kusalassa       anubhomi rattiṃ amānusiṃ    
divā paṭihatāva kukkurā      upadhāvanti samantā khādituṃ    
ye ca te satatānuyogino    dhuvaṃ payuttā sugatassa sāsane    
maññāmi te amatameva       kevalaṃ adhigacchanti    
padaṃ asaṅkhatanti .

Dutiyamigaluddapetavatthu aṭṭhamaṃ .

[119] [^220-9] mālī kirīṭī [^220-1] kāyūrī       gattā te candanussadā    
pasannamukhavaṇṇosi           suriyavaṇṇova sobhasi .

Amānusā parisajjā         ye te me parivārikā    
dasa kaññāsahassāni         yā temā paricārikā .

Tā kambukāyūradharā         kañcanacelabhūsitā    
mahānubhāvosi tuvaṃ          lomahaṃsanarūpavā .

Piṭṭhimaṃsāni attano         sāmaṃ ukkacca [^220-2] khādasi    
kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         piṭṭhimaṃsāni attano    
sāmaṃ ukkacca [^220-2] khādasi .

Attanohaṃ anatthāya         jīvaloke acarissaṃ    
pesuññamusāvādena         nikativañcanāya ca .

Tatthāhaṃ parisaṃ gantvā       saccakāle upaṭṭhite  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 221 -->
<a id="page-221"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 221]*

Atthaṃ dhammaṃ nirakatvā [^221-1]     adhammamanuvattissaṃ [^221-2] .

Evaṃ so khādatattānaṃ       yo hoti piṭṭhimaṃsiko    
yathāhaṃ ajja khādāmi        piṭṭhimaṃsāni attano .

Tayidaṃ tayā nārada sāmaṃ diṭṭhaṃ    
anukampakā ye kusalā vadeyyuṃ    
mā pesuṇaṃ mā ca musā bhaṇi    
mā khosi piṭṭhimaṃsiko tuvanti .

Kūṭavinicchayakapetavatthu [^221-3] navamaṃ .

[120] 10 antalikkhasmiṃ tiṭṭhanto      duggandho pūti vāyasi    
mukhañca te kimiyo pūtigandhaṃ    khādanti kiṃ kammamakāsi pubbe .

Tato satthaṃ gahetvāna       okkantanti [^221-4] punappunaṃ    
khārena paripphositvā       okkantanti punappunaṃ .

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchasi .

Ahaṃ rājagahe ramme        ramaṇīye giribbaje    
issaro dhanadhaññassa         supahūtassa mārisa .

Tassāyaṃ me bhariyā ca       dhītā ca suṇisā ca me    
tamālaṃ uppalañcāpi         paccagghañca vilepanaṃ    
thūpaṃ harantiyo vāresiṃ       taṃ pāpaṃ pakataṃ mayā .

#[^221-1] ma. Nirākatvā . Yu. Niraṃkatvā .  [^221-2] yu. ...Tiyaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 222 -->
<a id="page-222"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 222]*

Chaḷāsītisahassāni           mayaṃ paccattavedanā    
thūpapūjaṃ vivaṇṇetvā         pacāma niraye bhusaṃ    
ye ca kho thūpapūjāya        vattante arahato mahe .

Ādīnavaṃ pakāsenti         vivecayetha no [^222-1] tato    
imā ca passa āyantiyo     māladhārī alaṅkatā .

Mālāvipākaṃ anubhontiyo     samiddhā tā yasassiyo [^222-2]    
tañca disvāna accheraṃ       abbhūtaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ .

Namo karonti sappaññā      vandanti taṃ mahāmuniṃ    
sohaṃ nūna [^222-3] ito gantvā   yoniṃ laddhāna mānusiṃ    
thūpapūjaṃ karissāmi           appamatto punappunanti .

Dhātuvivaṇṇapetavatthu dasamaṃ .

Uddānaṃ    
abhijjamāno koṇḍañño [^222-4]   rathakārī bhusena ca    
kumāro bhaṇikā [^222-5] ceva     dve luddā piṭṭhipūjayo    
vaggo tena vuccatīti [^222-6] .

Cūḷavaggo tatiyo .

#[^222-1] ma. Ne .  [^222-2] ma. Yu. Yasassiniyo .  [^222-3] yu. Dāni .  [^222-4] ma. Kuṇḍiyo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 223 -->
<a id="page-223"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 223]*

Catuttho mahāvaggo    
[121] [^223-1] vesāli nāma nagaratthi vajjīnaṃ    
tattha ahu licchavi ambasakkharo [^223-1]    
disvāna petaṃ nagarassa bāhiraṃ    
tattheva pucchittha taṃ kāraṇatthiko .[^223-5]    
seyyā [^223-2] nisajjā nayimassa atthi    
abhikkamo natthi paṭikkamo ca [^223-3]    
asītapītaṃ khāyitavatthabhogā    
paricārikā [^223-4] sāpi imassa natthi .

Ye ñātakā diṭṭhasutā suhajjā    
anukampakā yassa ahesuṃ pubbe    
daṭṭhaṭhapi dāni na labhanti [^223-5] taṃpi    
virājitatto hi janena tena .

Na duggatassa [^223-6] bhavanti mittā    
jahanti mittā vikalaṃ viditvā    
atthañca disvā parivārayanti    
bahū ca mittā uggatassa honti .

Nihīnattho [^223-7] sabbabhogehi kiccho [^223-8]    
#[^223-1] ma. Ammasakkaro .  [^223-2] yu. Seyyo .  [^223-3] yu. Vā .  [^223-4] ma. Paricāraṇā .

#[^223-5] ma. Diṭṭhumpi te dāni na taṃ labhanti .  [^223-6] ma. Oggatattassa .  [^223-7] ma. Nihīnatto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 224 -->
<a id="page-224"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 224]*

Sammakkhito saṃparibhinnagatto    
ussāvabinduva palimpamāno    
ajja suve jīvitassuparodho [^224-1] .

Etādisaṃ uttamakicchapattaṃl    
uttāsitaṃ picumandassa [^224-2] sūle    
atha tvaṃ kena vaṇṇena vadesi    
yakkha jīva bho jīvitameva seyyoti .

Sālohito eso ahosi mayhaṃ    
ahaṃ sarāmi purimāya jātiyā    
disvā ca me kāruññaṃ ahosi    
rāja mā pāpadhammo nirayaṃ patāyaṃ .

Ito cuto licchavi esa poso    
sattussadaṃ nīrayaṃ ghorarūpaṃ    
uppajjati dukkaṭakammakārī    
mahābhitāpaṃ kaṭukaṃ bhayānakaṃ .

Anekabhāgena guṇena seyyo    
ayameva sūlo nirayena tena    
mā [^224-3] ekantadukkhaṃ kaṭukaṃ bhayānakaṃ    
ekantatippaṃ nirayaṃ patāyaṃ .

#[^224-1] ma. Jīvitassūparodho . Yu. Jīvitassaparodho .  [^224-2] ma. Pucimandassa .  [^224-3] ma. Māsaddo  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 225 -->
<a id="page-225"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 225]*

Idañca sutvā vacanaṃ mameso    
dukkhūpanīto vijaheyya pāpaṃ [^225-1]    
tasmā ahaṃ santike na bhaṇāmi    
mā mekato jīvatassuparodhoti .

Aññāto eso purisassa attho    
aññaṃpi icchāmase pucchituṃ tuvaṃ    
okāsakammaṃ mama no sace karosi [^225-2]    
pucchāmihaṃ [^225-3] na ca no kujjhitabbaṃ .

Addhā paṭiññā me tadā ahu    
acikkhanā appasannassa hoti    
akāmā saddheyyavacoti [^225-4] katvā    
pucchassu maṃ kāmaṃ yathā visayhanti .

Yaṃ kiñcāhaṃ cakkhunā passissāmi    
sabbaṃpi tāhaṃ abhisaddaheyyaṃ    
disvāpi taṃ nopi ce saddaheyya    
kareyyāsi me yakkha niyassakammanti .

Saccappaṭiññā tava me sā hotu    
sutvāna dhammaṃ labhassu pasādaṃ    
#[^225-1] yu. Pāṇaṃ .  [^225-2] ma. Okāsakammaṃ sace no karosi .  [^225-3] pucchāma taṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 226 -->
<a id="page-226"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 226]*

Aññatthiko no ca paduṭṭhacitto    
yante sutaṃ asutaṃ vāpi dhammaṃ .

Sabbaṃ akkhissaṃ yathā pajānaṃ    
setena assena alaṅkatena    
upayāsi sūlāvutakassa santike    
yānaṃ idaṃ abbhūtaṃ dassaneyyaṃ    
kissetaṃ kammassa ayaṃ vipāko .

Vesāliyā tassa [^226-1] nagarassa majjhe    
cikkhallamagge nagaraṃ [^226-2] ahosi    
gosīsamekāhaṃ pasannacitto    
setuṃ [^226-3] gahetvā nagarasmiṃ [^226-4] nikkhipiṃ .

Etasmiṃ pādāni patiṭṭhapetvā    
mayañca aññe ca atikkamimha [^226-5]    
yānaṃ idaṃ abbhūtaṃ dassaneyyaṃ    
tasseva kammassa ayaṃ vipāko .

Vaṇṇo ca te sabbadisā pabhāsati    
gandho ca te sabbadisā pavāyati    
yakkhiddhipattosi mahānubhāvo    
naggo cāsi kissa ayaṃ vipāko .

#[^226-1] ma. Ayaṃ pāṭho na dissati .  [^226-2] yu. Cikkhalapabbe narakaṃ .  [^226-3] ma. Setaṃ .  [^226-4] yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 227 -->
<a id="page-227"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 227]*

akkodhano niccapasannacitto    
saṇhāhi vācāhi janaṃ upemi    
tasseva kammassa ayaṃ vipāko    
dibbo me vaṇṇo satataṃ pabhāsati .

Yasañca kittiñca dhamme ṭhitānaṃ    
disvāna mantemi pasannacitto    
tasseva kammassa ayaṃ vipāko    
dibbo me gandho satataṃ pavāyati .

Sahāyānaṃ titthasmiṃ nahāyantānaṃ    
thale gahetvā nidahissa dussaṃ    
khiḍḍatthiko no ca paduṭṭhitto    
tenamhi naggo kasirā pavutti .
