Yo kīḷamāno ca karoti pāpaṃ    
tassīdisaṃ kammavipākamāhu    
akīḷamāno pana yo karoti    
kiṃ tassa kammassa vipākamāhu .

Ye duṭṭhasaṅkappamanā manussā    
kāyena vācāya ca saṅkiliṭṭhā    
kāyassa bhedā abhisamparāyaṃ    
asaṃsayaṃ te nirayaṃ upenti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 228 -->
<a id="page-228"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 228]*

Apare pana sugatiṃ [^228-1] āsisamānā    
dāne ratā saṅgahitattabhāvā    
kāyassa bhedā abhisamparāyaṃ    
asaṃsayaṃ te sugatiṃ upentīti .

Taṃ kinti jāneyyaṃ ahaṃ avecca    
kalyāṇapāpassa ayaṃ vipāko    
kiṃ vāhaṃ disvā abhisaddaheyyaṃ    
ko vāpi maṃ saddahāpeyya etanti .

Disvā ca sutvā abhisaddahassu    
kalyāṇapāpassa ayaṃ vipāko    
kalyāṇapāpe ubhaye asante    
siyā nu sattā sugatā duggatā vā .

No cettha kammāni kareyyuṃ maccā    
kalyāṇapāpāni manussaloke    
nāhesuṃ sattā sugatā duggatā vā    
hīnā paṇītā ca manussaloke .

Yasmā ca kammāni karonti maccā  
kalyāṇapāpāni manussaloke  
tasmā [3] sattā sugatā duggatā vā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 229 -->
<a id="page-229"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 229]*

Hīnā paṇītā ca manussaloke .

Dvayañca [^229-1] kammānaṃ vipākamāhu    
sukhassa dukkhassa ca vedanīyaṃ    
tā devatā [^229-2] parivārayanti    
paccanti bālā dvayataṃ apassinoti .

Na matthi kammāni sayaṃ katāni    
datvāpi me natthi so [^229-3] ādiseyya    
acchādanaṃ sayanamathannapānaṃ    
tenamhi naggo kasirā pavuttīti .

Siyā nu kho kāraṇaṃ kiñci yakkha    
acchādanaṃ yena tuvaṃ labhetha    
ācikkha me tvaṃ yadatthi hetu    
saddhāyitaṃ hetuvaco suṇomāti .

Kappitako nāma idhatthi bhikkhu  
jhāyī susīlo arahā vimutto  
guttindriyo saṃvutapātimokkho  
sītibhūto uttamadiṭṭhipatto  
sakhilo vadaññū suvaco sumukho  
svāgamo suppaṭimuttako ca  
puññassa khettaṃ araṇavihārī  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 230 -->
<a id="page-230"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 230]*

Devamanussānañca dakkhiṇeyyo    
santo vidhūmo anīgho nirāso    
mutto visallo amamo avaṅko    
nirūpadhi sabbapapañcakhīṇo    
tisso vijjā anuppatto jutimā .

Appaññāto disvāpi na sujāno    
munīti naṃ [^230-1] vajjīsu voharanti    
jānanti taṃ yakkhabhūtā anejaṃ    
kalyāṇadhammaṃ vicarantaṃ [^230-2] loke .

Tassa tuvaṃ ekaṃ yugaṃ duve vā    
mamuddisitvāna sace dadetha    
paṭiggahitāni ca tāni assu [^230-3]    
mamañca passetha saṃnaddhadussanti .

Kasmiṃ padese samaṇaṃ vasantaṃ    
gantvāna passemu mayaṃ idāni    
sa [^230-4] majja kaṅkhaṃ vicikicchitañca    
diṭṭhivisūkāni ko vinodaye ceti [^230-5] .

Eso nisinno kapinaccanāyaṃ    
parivārito devatāhi bahūhi    
#[^230-1] yu. Muni naṃ .  [^230-2] yu. Vicaranti .  [^230-3] yu. Cassa .  [^230-4] ma. Yo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 231 -->
<a id="page-231"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 231]*

Dhammikathaṃ bhāsati saccanāmo    
sakasmi accherake [^231-1] appamattoti .

Tathāhaṃ kassāmi gantvā idāni    
acchādayissaṃ samaṇaṃ yugena    
paṭiggahitāni ca tāni passa [^231-2]    
tuvañca passemu saṃnaddhadussanti .

Mā akkhaṇe pabbajitaṃ upāgami    
sādhu vo licchavi nesa dhammo    
tato ca kāle upasaṅkamitvā    
tattheva passāmi raho nisinnanti .

Tathā hi vatvā agamāsi tattha    
parivārito dāsagaṇena licchavi    
so taṃ nagaraṃ upasaṅkamitvā    
vāsupagañchittha sake nivesane .

Tato ca kāle gihikiccāni katvā    
nhātvā pivitvā ca khaṇaṃ labhitvā    
viceyya peḷato ca yugāni aṭṭha    
gāhāpayi dāsagaṇena licchavi .

So taṃ padesaṃ upasaṅkamitvā  
taṃ addasa samaṇaṃ santacittaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 232 -->
<a id="page-232"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 232]*

Paṭikkantaṃ gocarato nivattaṃ    
sītibhūtaṃ rukkhamūle nisinnaṃ .

Tamenaṃ avoca upasaṅkamitvā    
appābādhaṃ phāsuvihārañca pucchi    
vesāliyaṃ licchavi ahaṃ bhadante    
jānanti maṃ licchavi ambasakkharo .

Imāni me aṭṭha yugāni bhante [^232-1]    
paṭiggaṇha bhante dadāmi tuyhaṃ    
teneva atthena idhāgatosmi    
yathā ahaṃ attamano bhaveyyaṃ .

Dūratova samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca    
nivesanante parivajjayanti    
pattāni bhijjanti tava nivesane    
saṅghāṭiyo cāpi [^232-2] vidālayanti [^232-3] .

Athāpare [^232-4] pādakudārikāhi [^232-5]    
avaṃsirā samaṇā pāṭiyanti    
etādisaṃ pabbajitaṃ [^232-6] vihesaṃ    
tayā kataṃ samaṇā pāpuṇanti .

Tiṇena telaṃpi [^232-7] na tvaṃ adāsi    
#[^232-1] ma. Yu. Subhāni .  [^232-2] yu. Pāpi .  [^232-3] vipātayantītipi dissati .  [^232-4] yu. Athā pure .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 233 -->
<a id="page-233"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 233]*

Mūḷhassa [^233-1] maggaṃpi na pāvadāsi    
andhassa daṇḍaṃ sayamādiyāsi    
etādiso kadariyo asaṃvuto [2] .

Atha tvaṃ kena vaṇṇena kimeva disvā    
amhehi saha saṃvibhāgaṃ karosi .

Paccemi bhante yaṃ tvaṃ vadesi .

Vimosayi [^233-3] samaṇabrāhmaṇe [^233-4] ca .

Khiḍḍatthiko no ca paduṭṭhacitto    
etaṃpi me dukkaṭameva bhante    
khiḍḍāya kho pasavitvāna pāpaṃ    
vedeti dukkhaṃ appamattabhogī [^233-5]    
daharo yuvā nagganiyassa bhāgī    
kiṃsu tato dukkhatarassa hoti .

Taṃ disvā saṃvegamalamatthaṃ bhante    
tappaccayā vāpi [^233-6] dadāmi dānaṃ    
paṭiggaṇha bhante vatthayugāni aṭṭha    
yakkhassimāgacchantu dakkhiṇāyo .

Addhā hi [^233-7] dānaṃ bahudhā pasaṭṭhaṃ    
dadato ca te akkhayadhammamatthu    
#[^233-1] ma. Muṭṭhassa .  [^233-2] ma. Tuvaṃ .  [^233-3] ma. Vihesayiṃ .  [^233-4] yu. ......Ṇetha .  [^233-5] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 234 -->
<a id="page-234"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 234]*

paṭiggaṇhāmi te vatthayugāni aṭṭha    
yakkhassimāgacchantu dakkhiṇāyo .

Tato hi so ācamayitvā licchavi    
therassa datvāna yugāni aṭṭha    
paṭiggahitāni ca tāni vāssuṃ    
yakkhañca passetha saṃnaddhadussaṃ .

Tamaddasa candanasāralittaṃ    
ājaññamāruyha uḷāravaṇṇaṃ    
alaṅkataṃ sādhunivatthadussaṃ    
parivāritaṃ yakkhamahiddhipattaṃ .

So taṃ disvā attamano udaggo    
pahaṭṭhacittova subhaggarūpo    
kammañca disvāna mahāvipākaṃ    
sandiṭṭhikaṃ cakkhunā sacchikatvā .

Tamenamavoca upasaṅkamitvā    
dassāmi dānaṃ samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ    
na cāpi me kiñci adeyyamatthi    
tuvañca me yakkha bahūpakāro .

Tuvañca me licchavi ekadesaṃ  
adāsi dānāni amoghametaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 235 -->
<a id="page-235"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 235]*

Svāhaṃ karissāmi tayā va sakkhiṃ    
amānuso mānusakena saddhiṃ .

Gati ca banadhū ca parāyanañca    
mitto mamāsi atha devatāsi [^235-1]    
yācāmi [^235-2] taṃ pañjaliko bhavitvā    
icchāmi taṃ yakkha punāpi daṭṭhaṭha .

Sace tuvaṃ assaddho bhavissasi    
kadariyarūpo vippaṭipannacitto    
teneva [^235-3] maṃ licchavi [^235-4] dassanāya    
disvā ca taṃ nopi [^235-5] ca ālapissaṃ .

Sace tuvaṃ bhavissasi dhammagāravo    
dāne rato saṅgahitattabhāvo    
opānabhūto samaṇabrāhmaṇānaṃ    
evaṃ mamaṃ licchavi [^235-4] dassanāya .

Disvā ca taṃ ālapissaṃ bhadante    
imañca sūlato lahuṃ pamuñca    
yatonidānaṃ akarimha sakkhiṃ    
maññāmi [^235-6] sūlāvutakassa kāraṇā    
te aññamaññaṃ akarimha sakkhiṃ .

#[^235-1] ma. Devatā me .  [^235-2] yu. Yathāmahaṃ .  [^235-3] ma. Tvaṃ neva .  [^235-4] ma. Lacchasi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 236 -->
<a id="page-236"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 236]*

Ayañca sūlāvuto [^236-1] lahuṃ pamutto    
sakkacca dhammāni samācaranto    
muñceyya so nirayāva tamhā    
kammaṃ siyā aññatra savedanīyaṃ [^236-2] .

Kappitakañca upasaṅkamitvā    
tena saha saṃvibhajitvāna kāle    
sayaṃ mukhena upanisajja puccha    
so te akkhissati etamatthaṃ .

Tameva bhikkhuṃ upasaṅkamitvā pucchassu    
puññatthiko [^236-3] no ca [^236-4] paduṭṭhacitto    
so te sutaṃ asutañcāpi dhammaṃ    
sabbaṃpi akkhissati yathā pajānaṃ    
suto ca dhammaṃ sugatiṃ akkhissa [^236-5] .

So tattha rahassaṃ samullapitvā    
sakkhiṃ karitvāna amānusena    
pakkāmi so licchavīnaṃ sakāsaṃ    
atha bravi parisaṃ sannisinnaṃ .

Suṇantu bhonto mama ekavākyaṃ    
varaṃ varissaṃ labhissāmi atthaṃ    
#[^236-1] ma. Sūlato .  2. Ma. Vedanīyaṃ .  [^236-3] ma. Aññatthiko .  [^236-4] yu. Neva .  [^236-5] suto ...


<!-- Siam Rath Page 237 -->
<a id="page-237"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 237]*

Sūlāvuto puriso luddakammo    
paṇītadaṇḍo anusattarūpo .

Ettāvatā vīsatirattimattā    
yato āvuto neva jīvati na mato    
tāhaṃ mocayissāmi dāni    
yathā matiṃ anujānātu saṅgho .

Etañca aññañca lahuṃ pamuñca    
ko taṃ vadetha ca tathā karontaṃ    
yathā pajānāsi tathā karohi    
yathā matiṃ anujānāti saṅgho .

So taṃ padesaṃ upasaṅkamitvā    
sūlāvutaṃ mocayi khippameva    
mā bhāyi sammāti [^237-1] ca taṃ avoca    
tikicchakānañca upaṭṭhapesi .

Kappitakañca upasaṅkamitvā    
tena saha saṃvibhajitvāna kāle    
sayaṃ mukheneva [^237-2] upanisajja licchavi    
tattheva pucchatthanaṃ [^237-3] kāraṇatthiko .

Sūlāvuto puriso luddakammo  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 238 -->
<a id="page-238"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 238]*

Paṇītadaṇḍo anusattarūpo    
ettāvatā vīsatirattimattā    
yato āvuto neva jīvati na mato .

So mocito gantvā mayā idāni    
etassa yakkhassa vacoti [^238-1] bhante    
siyā nu kho kāraṇaṃ kiñcideva    
yena so nirayaṃ no vajeyya .

Ācikkha bhante yadi atthi hetu    
saddhāyitaṃ hetu vacoti [^238-2] suṇoma    
na tesaṃ kammānaṃ vināsamatthi    
avedayitvā idha bayantibhāvo .

Sace so dhammāni [^238-3] samācareyya    
sakkaccaṃ rattindivaṃ appamatto    
muñceyya so nirayā ca [^238-4] tamhā    
kammaṃ siyā aññatra vedanīyaṃ .

Aññāto eso purisassa attho    
mamaṃpidāni anukampa bhante    
anusāsa maṃ ovada bhūripañña    
yathā ahaṃ no [^238-5] nirayaṃ vajeyyaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 239 -->
<a id="page-239"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 239]*

Ajjeva buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ upehi    
dhammañca saṅghañca pasannacitto    
tatheva sikkhāpadāni pañca    
akhaṇḍaphullāni samādiyassu .

Pāṇātipātā viramassu khippaṃ    
loke adinnaṃ parivajjayassu    
amajjapo mā ca musā abhāsi [^239-1]    
sakena dārena ca hohi tuṭṭho .

Imañca aṭṭhaṅgavaraṃ upetaṃ [^239-2]    
samādiyāhi kusalaṃ sukhindriyaṃ [^239-3] .

Cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañca          paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ    
annapānaṃ khādanīyaṃ          vatthaṃ senāsanāni ca .

Dadāhi ujubhūtesu           sadā [^239-4] puññaṃ pavaḍḍhati    
bhikkhū ca sīlasampanne        vītarāge bahussute    
tappehi [^239-5] annapānena     sadā puññaṃ pavaḍḍhati .

Evañca dhammāni [^239-6] samācaranto    
sakkaccaṃ rattindivaṃ appamatto    
muñceyya so [^239-7] nirayā ca tamhā    
#[^239-1] ma. Abhāṇī .  [^239-2] ma. Imañca ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgavarenupetaṃ .  [^239-3] ma. Sukhuddariyaṃ .

#[^239-4] ma. Yu. Vippasannena cetasā .  [^239-5] yu. ...Si .  [^239-6] ma. Kammāni .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 240 -->
<a id="page-240"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 240]*

Kammaṃ siyā aññatra vedanīyaṃ .

Ajjeva buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ upemi[^240-5]    
dhammañca saṅghañca pasannacitto    
tatheva sikkhāpadāni [^240-1] pañca    
akhaṇḍaphullāni samādiyāmi .

Pāṇātipātā viramāmi khippaṃ    
loke adinnaṃ parivajjayāmi    
amajjapo no ca musā bhaṇāmi    
sakena dārena ca homi tuṭṭho .

Imañca ariyaṃ aṭṭhaṅgavaraṃ upetaṃ    
samādiyāmi kusalaṃ sukhindriyaṃ .

Cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañca          paccayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ    
annapānaṃ khādanīyaṃ          vatthaṃ senāsanāni ca    
bhikkhū ca sīlasampanne        vītarāge bahussute    
dadāmi na vikkappāmi [^240-2]     buddhānaṃ sāsane rato .

Etādiso licchavi ambasakkharo    
vesāliyaṃ aññataro upāsako    
saddho mudu kārakaro ca bhikkhu    
saṅghañca sakkacca tadā upaṭṭhahi .

Sūlāvuto ca arogo hutvā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 241 -->
<a id="page-241"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 241]*

(1) serī sukhī pabbajjaṃ upāgami    
āgamma kappitakuttamaṃ ubhopi    
sāmaññaphalāni ajjhaguṃ .

Etādisā sappurisānaṃ sevanā    
mahapphalā hoti sataṃ vijānataṃ    
sūlāvuto aggaphalaṃ phussati [^241-2]    
phalaṃ kaniṭṭhaṃ pana ambasakkharoti .

Ambasakkharapetavatthu paṭhamaṃ .Jha    
[122] [^241-2] suṇotha yakkhassa ca vāṇijāna ca    
.Pe.

(vimānavatthusmiṃ sattamassa sunikkhittavaggassa dasamavatthusmiṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ)    
serissakapetavatthu dutiyaṃ .

[123] [^241-3] rājā piṅgalako nāma       suraṭṭhānaṃ adhipati ahu    
moriyānaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ         gantvā suraṭṭhaṃ punarāgamā .

Uṇhe majjhantike kāle     rājā paṅkaṃ upāgami  
addasa maggaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ         petānaṃ vaṇṇanāpathaṃ  
sārathiṃ āmantayi rājā      ayaṃ maggo ramaṇīyo  
khemo sovatthiko sivo  
imināva sārathi yāhi [^241-3]     suraṭṭhānaṃ santike ito  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 242 -->
<a id="page-242"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 242]*

Tena pāyāsi soraṭṭho      senāya caturaṅginiyā .

Ubbiggarūpo puriso         suraṭṭhaṃ etadabrūvi    
kumaggaṃ paṭipannamhā         bhiṃsanaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ .

Purato padissati maggo       pacchato ca na dissati    
kumaggaṃ paṭipannamhā         yamapurisānaṃ santike .

Amānuso vāyati gandho      ghoso sūyati dāruṇo    
saṃviggo rājā suraṭṭho      sārathiṃ etadabrūvi    
kumaggaṃ paṭipannamhā         bhiṃsanaṃ lomahaṃsanaṃ    
puratova dissati maggo       pacchato ca na dissati .

Kumaggaṃ paṭipannamhā         yamapurisānaṃ santike    
amānuso vāyati gandho      ghoso sūyati dāruṇo .

Hatthikkhandhañca āruyha       olokento catuddisā    
addasa rukkhaṃ nigrodhaṃ [^242-1]     pādapaṃ chāyasampannaṃ    
nīlabbhavaṇṇasadisaṃ            meghavaṇṇasirannibhaṃ    
sārathiṃ āmantayi rājā      kiṃ eso dissati brahā    
nalabbhavaṇṇasadiso           meghavaṇṇasirannibho .

So nigrodho so mahārāja   pādapo chāyasampanno    
nīlabbhavaṇṇasadiso           meghavaṇṇasirannibho .

Tena pāyāsi suraṭṭho       yena so dissati [^242-2] brahā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 243 -->
<a id="page-243"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 243]*

Nīlabbhavaṇṇasadiso           meghavaṇṇasirannibho .

Hatthikkhandhato oruyha       rājā rukkhaṃ upāgami    
nisīdi rukkhamūlasmiṃ           sāmacco saparijano    
pūraṃ pānīyakarakaṃ [^243-1]         pūve citte ca addasa .

Puriso ca devavaṇṇī         sabbābharaṇabhūsito    
upasaṅkamitvā rājānaṃ       soraṭṭhaṃ etadabrūvi    
svāgatante mahārāja       atho te adurāgataṃ    
pivatu devo pānīyaṃ         pūve khāda arindama .

Pivitvā rājā pānīyaṃ       sāmacco saparijano    
pūve khāditvā pivitvā ca    suraṭṭho etadabrūvi    
devatā nusi gandhabbo       ādu sakko purindado    
ajānanto [^243-2] taṃ pucchāma    kathaṃ jānemu taṃ mayaṃ .

Namhi devo na gandhabbo     nāpi sakko purindado    
peto ahaṃ mahārāja        suraṭṭhā idhamāgato .

Kiṃsīlo kiṃsamācāro         suraṭṭhasmiṃ pure tuvaṃ    
kena te brahmacariyena      ānubhāvo ayaṃ tava .

Taṃ suṇohi mahārāja         arindama raṭṭhavaḍḍhana    
amaccā pārisajjā ca       brāhmaṇo ca purohito .

Suraṭṭhasmiṃ ahaṃ deva         puriso pāpacetaso  
micchādiṭṭhi ca dussīlo       kadariyo paribhāsako  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 244 -->
<a id="page-244"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 244]*

Dadantānaṃ karontānaṃ        vārayissaṃ bahujanaṃ    
aññesaṃ dadamānānaṃ         antarāyaṃ karomahaṃ .

Vipāko natthi dānassa       saṃyamassa kuto phalaṃ    
natthi ācariyo nāma        adantaṃ ko damissati .

Samatulyāni bhūtāni          kule [^244-1] jeṭṭhāpacāyiko    
natthi balaṃ viriyaṃ vā         kuto uṭṭhānaporisaṃ .

Natthi dānaphalaṃ nāma         na visodheti verinaṃ    
laddheyyaṃ labhate macco      nīyati pariṇāmajaṃ .

Natthi mātā pitā bhātā     loko natthi ito paraṃ    
natthi dinnaṃ natthi hutaṃ        sunihitaṃpi na vijjati .

Yopi haneyya purisaṃ         purisassa [^244-2] chindate siraṃ    
na koci kiñci hanati         sattannaṃ vivaramantare .

Acchejjo abhejjo [^244-3] jīvo   aṭṭhaṃso guḷaparimaṇḍalo    
yojanāni satā [^244-4] pañca     ko jīvaṃ chetumarahati .

Yathā suttaguḷe khitte       nibbeṭhentaṃ palāyati    
evamevaṃpi so jīvo        nibbeṭhento palāyati .

Yathā gāmato nikkhamma       aññaṃ gāmaṃ pavisati    
evamevaṃpi so jīvo        aññaṃ kāyaṃ [^244-5] pavisati .

#[^244-1] ma. Kuto .  [^244-2] ma. Yu. Parassa .  [^244-3] yu. Acchejjabhejujo .  [^244-4] ma. Yojanānaṃ sataṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 245 -->
<a id="page-245"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 245]*

Yathā gehato nikkhamma       aññaṃ gehaṃ pavisati    
evamevaṃpi so jīvo        aññaṃ bondiṃ nivīsati [^245-1] .

Cūḷāsīti mahākappino satasahassāni   ye bālā ye ca paṇḍitā    
saṃsāraṃ khepayitvāna         dukkhassantaṃ karissare .

Mitāni sukhadukkhāni          doṇehi piṭakehi ca    
jino sabbaṃ pajānāti        samūḷhā itarā pajā .

Evaṃdiṭṭhi pure āsiṃ        samūḷho mohapāruto    
micchādiṭṭhi ca dussīlo       kadariyo paribhāsako .

Oraṃ me chahi māsehi       kālakiriyā bhavissati .

Ekantaṃ kaṭukaṃ ghoraṃ         niriyaṃ papatissāhaṃ    
catukkaṇṇaṃ catudvāraṃ         vibhattaṃ bhāgaso mitaṃ    
ayopākārapariyantaṃ         ayasā paṭikujjitaṃ .

Tassa ayomayā bhūmi         jalitā tejasā yutā    
samantā yojanasataṃ          pharitvā tiṭṭhati sabbadā .

Vassasatasahassāni [^245-2]        ghoso sūyati tāvade .

Lakkho eso mahārāja      satabhāgā vassakoṭiyo    
koṭisatasahassāni           niraye paccare janā    
micchādiṭṭhī ca dussīlā       ye ca ariyūpavādino .

Tatthāhaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ         dukkhaṃ vedissaṃ vedanaṃ    
phalaṃ pāpassa kammassa        tasmā socāmahaṃ bhusaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 246 -->
<a id="page-246"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 246]*

Taṃ suṇohi mahārāja         arindama raṭṭhavaḍḍhana    
dhītā mayhaṃ mahārāja        uttarā bhaddamatthu te .

Karoti bhaddakaṃ kammaṃ         sīlesūposathe ratā    
dānaratā [^246-1] saṃvibhāgī ca     vadaññū [^246-2] vītamaccharā    
akhaṇḍakārī sikkhāyaṃ         suṇhā parakulesu ca    
upāsikā sakyamunino        sambuddhassa sirīmato .

Bhikkhu ca sīlasampanno        gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi    
okkhittacakkhu satimā        guttadvāro susaṃvuto    
sapadānaṃ caramāno          agamā taṃ nivesanaṃ .

Tamaddasa mahārāja          uttarā bhaddamatthu te    
pūraṃ pānīyakarakaṃ            pūve citte ca sā adā    
pitā me kālakato bhante    tassetaṃ upakappatu .

Samanantarānudiṭṭhe          vipāko upapajjatha    
bhuñjāmi kāmakāmī [^246-3]       rājā vessavaṇo yathā .

Taṃ suṇohi mahārāja         arindama raṭṭhavaḍḍhana    
sadevakassa lokassa         buddho aggo pavuccati    
taṃ buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ gaccha         saputtadāro arindama .

Aṭṭhaṅgikena maggena        phusanti amataṃ padaṃ    
taṃ dhammaṃ saraṇaṃ gaccha         saputtadāro arindama .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 247 -->
<a id="page-247"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 247]*

Cattāro ca paṭipannā       cattāro ca phale ṭhitā    
esa saṅgho ujubhūto        paññāsīlasamāhito    
taṃ saṅghaṃ saraṇaṃ gaccha         saputtadāro arindama .

Pāṇātipātā viramassu khippaṃ    
loke adinnaṃ parivajjayassu    
amajjapo no [^247-1] ca musā abhāṇi    
sakena dārena ca hohi tuṭṭho .

Atthakāmosi me yakkha       hitakāmosi devate    
karomi tuyhaṃ vacanaṃ          tvamasi ācariyo mama .

Upemi saraṇaṃ buddhaṃ          dhammañcāpi anuttaraṃ    
saṅghañca naradevassa         gacchāmi saraṇaṃ ahaṃ .

Pāṇātipātā viramāmi khippaṃ    
loke adinnaṃ parivajjayāmi    
amajjapo no ca musā bhaṇāmi    
sakena dārena [2] homi tuṭṭho .

Ophunāmi [^247-3] mahāvāte     nadiyā vā [^247-4] sīghagāmiyā [^247-5]    
vamāmi pāpakaṃ diṭṭhiṃ         buddhānaṃ sāsane rato .

Idaṃ vatvāna suraṭṭho        viramitvā pāpadassanaṃ [^247-6]    
#[^247-1] ma. Mā .  [^247-2] ma. Ca .  [^247-3] yu. Odhunāmi .  [^247-4] ma. Vāsaddo natthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 248 -->
<a id="page-248"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 248]*

Namo bhagavato katvā        pāmokkho rathamāruyhīti .

Nandikāpetavatthu tatiyaṃ .

[124] [^248-4] uṭṭhehi revate supāpadhamme    
.Pe.

(vimānavatthusmiṃ pañcamassa mahārathavaggassa dutiyavatthusmiṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ)    
revatipetavatthu catutthaṃ .

[125] [^248-5] idaṃ mama ucchuvanaṃ mahantaṃ    
nibbattati puññaphalaṃ anappakaṃ    
taṃ dāni me na paribhogaṃ upeti [^248-1]    
ācikkha bhante kissa ayaṃ vipāko .

Vihaññāmi [^248-2] khajjāmi ca vāyamāmi ca    
parisakkāmi paribhuñjituṃ kiñci    
svāhaṃ chinnātumo [^248-3] kapaṇo lālapāmi    
kissa kammassa ayaṃ vipāko .

Vighāto cāhaṃ paripatāmi chamāyaṃ    
parivattāmi vāricarova ghamme    
dūrato [^248-4] ca me assukāni galanti    
ācikkha bhante kissa ayaṃ vipāko .

Chāto kilanto ca pipāsito ca  
#[^248-1] ma. Paribhogameti .  [^248-2] ma. Haññāmi .  [^248-3] ma. Chinnathāmo .  [^248-4] yu. Rudato ca  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 249 -->
<a id="page-249"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 249]*

Saṃtasito sātasukhaṃ na vinde    
pucchāmi taṃ etamatthaṃ bhadante    
kathannu ucchuparibhogaṃ labheyyaṃ .

Pure tuvaṃ kammamakāsi attanā .

Manussabhūto purimāya jātiyā    
ahañca taṃ etamatthaṃ vadāmi    
sutvāna tvaṃ etamatthaṃ vijānaṃ .

Ucchū tuvaṃ khādamāno payāto    
puriso ca te piṭṭhito anugañchi    
so ca taṃ paccāsanto kathesi    
tassa tuvaṃ na kiñci ālapittha .

So ca taṃ [^249-1] abhaṇantaṃ ayāci    
deheyya ucchunti ca taṃ avoca    
tassa tuvaṃ piṭṭhito ucchacha adāsi    
tassetaṃ kammassa ayaṃ vipāko .

Iṅgha tvaṃ [^249-2] gantvāna piṭṭhito gaṇheyyāsi    
gahetvāna khādassu yāvadatthaṃ    
teneva tvaṃ attamano bhavissasi    
haṭaṭho udaggo [^249-3] ca pamodito ca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 250 -->
<a id="page-250"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 250]*

Gantvāna so piṭṭhito aggahesi    
gahetvāna taṃ khādi yāvadatthaṃ    
teneva so attamano ahosi    
haṭṭho udaggo ca pamoditoti [^250-1] .

Ucchupetavatthu pañcamaṃ .

[126] [^250-6] sāvatthī nāma nagaraṃ         himavantassa passato    
tatthāsuṃ dve kumārā       rājaputtāti me sutaṃ .

Pamattā [^250-2] rajanīyesu       kāmassādābhinandino    
paccuppanne sukhe giddhā     na te passiṃsu nāgataṃ .

Te puttā [^250-3] ca manussattā     paralokaṃ ito gatā    
te ca [^250-4] ghosenti [^250-5] na dissanto  pubbe dukkaṭamattano .

Bahūsu vata santesu          deyyadhamme upaṭṭhite    
nāsakkhimhā ca attānaṃ      parittasotthiṃ [^250-6] kātuṃ sukhāvahaṃ .

Kiṃ tato pāpakaṃ assa        yanno rājakulā cutā    
upapannā petavisayaṃ         khuppipāsāsamappitā .

Sāmino idha hutvāna        honti assāmino tahiṃ    
maranti [^250-7] khuppipāsāya      manussā unnatonatā .

Evamādīnavaṃ [^250-8] ñatvā      issaramānasambhavaṃ    
#[^250-1] yu. Ime pāṭhā na dissanti .  [^250-2] ma. Sammattā .  [^250-3] yu. Cutā .

#[^250-4] yu. Tedha . [^250-5] ma. Ghosentayadissantā .  [^250-6] ma. Parittaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 251 -->
<a id="page-251"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 251]*

Pahāya issaramadaṃ           bhave saggagato naro    
kāyassa bhedā sappañño     saggaṃ so upapajjatīti .

Kumārapetavatthu chaṭṭhaṃ .

[127] [^251-7] pubbe katānaṃ kammānaṃ       vipāko mathaye manaṃ    
rūpe sadde rase gandhe     phoṭṭhabbe ca manorame .

Naccaṃ gītaṃ ratiṃ khiḍḍaṃ         anubhutvā anappakaṃ    
uyyāne paricāritvā [^251-1]    pavisanto giribbajaṃ .

Isiṃ sunettamaddakkhi         attadantaṃ samāhitaṃ    
appicchaṃ hirīsampannaṃ         uñche pattagate rataṃ .

Hatthikkhandhato oruyha       laddhā bhanteti ca brūvi    
tassa pattaṃ gahetvāna       uccaṃ paggayha khattiyo .

Thaṇḍile pattaṃ bhinditvā      hasamāno apakkami    
rañño kitavassāhaṃ putto     kiṃ maṃ bhikkhu karissasi .

Tassa kammassa pharusassa       vipāko kaṭuko ahu    
yaṃ rājaputto vedesi       nirayamhi samappito .

Chaḷeva caturāsīti           vassāni nahutāni ca    
bhusaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchiṭṭho        niraye katakibbiso .

Uttānopi ca paccittha       nikujjo vāmadakkhiṇo    
uddhaṃpādo ṭhito ceva       ciraṃ bālo apaccitha .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 252 -->
<a id="page-252"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 252]*

Bahūni vassasahassāni         pūgāni nahutāni ca    
bhusaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchiṭṭho        niraye katakibbiso .

Etādisaṃ kho kaṭukaṃ         appaduṭṭhapadosinaṃ    
paccanti pāpakammantā       isiṃ āsajja subbataṃ .

So tattha bahuvassāni        vedayitvā bahudukkhaṃ    
khuppipāsahato nāma         peto āsi tato cuto .

Evamādīnavaṃ ñatvā         issaramadasambhavaṃ    
pahāya issaramadaṃ           nivātamanuvattaye .

Diṭṭheva dhamme pāsaṃso      yo buddhesu sagāravo    
kāyassa bhedā sappañño     saggaṃ so upapajjatīti .

Rājaputtapetavatthu sattamaṃ .

[128] [^252-8] gūthakūpato uggantvā        ko nu diṭṭhova [^252-1] tiṭṭhasi    
nisaṃsayaṃ pāpakammanto        kinnu saddayase tuvanti .

Ahaṃ bhadante [^252-2] petomhi    duggato yamalokiko    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatoti .

Kiṃ nu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchasīti .

Ahu āvāsiko mayhaṃ        issukī kulamaccharī    
ajjhosito [^252-3] mayhaṃ ghare    kadariyo paribhāsako .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 253 -->
<a id="page-253"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 253]*

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        bhikkhavo paribhāsissaṃ    
tassa kammavipākena         petalokaṃ ito gatoti .

Amitto mittavaṇṇena        yo te āsi kulūpako    
kāyassa bhedā duppañño     kinnu pecca gatiṃ gatoti .Ña    
tassevāhaṃ pāpakammassa      sīse tiṭṭhāmi matthake    
so ca paravisayaṃ patto       mameva parivārako .

Yaṃ bhadantehanantaññe        etaṃ me hotu bhojanaṃ    
ahañca kho yaṃhanāmi         etaṃ so upajīvatīti .

Gūthakhādakapetavatthu aṭṭhamaṃ .

[129] [^253-9] gūthakūpato uggantvā        kā nu diṭṭhāva tiṭṭhasi    
nisaṃsayaṃ pāpakammantā        kinnu saddayase tuvanti .

Ahaṃ bhadante [^253-1] petīmhi     duggatā yamalokikā    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatāti .

Kiṃ nu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchasīti .

Ahu āvāsiko mayhaṃ        issukī kulamaccharī    
ajjhosito [^253-2] mayhaṃ ghare    kadariyo paribhāsako .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        bhikkhavo paribhāsissaṃ    
tassa kammavipākena         petalokaṃ ito gatāti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 254 -->
<a id="page-254"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 254]*

Amitto mittavaṇṇena        yo te āsi kulūpako    
kāyassa bhedā duppañño     kinnu pecca gatiṃ gatoti .

Tassevāhaṃ pāpakammassa      sīse tiṭṭhāmi matthake    
so ca paravisayaṃ patto       mameva parivārako .

Yaṃ bhadantehanantaññe        etaṃ me hotu bhojanaṃ    
ahañca kho yaṃhanāmi         etaṃ so upajīvatīti .

Gūthakhādakapetavatthu navamaṃ .

[130] 10 naggā dubbaṇṇarūpātha       kīsā dhamanisaṇṭhitā    
upphāsulikā kīsakā         ke nu tumhetha mārisāti .

Mayaṃ bhadante petamhā       duggatā yamalokikā    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gatāti .

Kiṃ nu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         petalokaṃ ito gatāti .

Anavajjesu [^254-1] titthesu      vicinimhaḍḍhamāsakaṃ    
santesu deyyadhammesu       dīpaṃ nākamhamattano .

Nadiṃ upema tasitā          rittakā parivattati    
chāyaṃ upema uṇhesu        ātapo parivattati .

Aggivaṇṇova no vāto      ḍahanto upavāyati    
etañca bhante arahāma      aññañca pāpakaṃ tato .

Api yojanāni gacchāma       chātā āhāragiddhino  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 255 -->
<a id="page-255"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 255]*

Aladdhā yeva nivattāma      aho no appapuññatā .

Chātā pamucchitā bhante      bhūmiyaṃ paṭisumbhitā    
uttānā patikirāma         avakujjā patāmase .

Te ca tattheva patitā       bhūmiyaṃ paṭisumbhitā    
uraṃ sīsañca ghaṭṭema         aho no appapuññatā .

Etañca bhante arahāma      aññañca pāpakaṃ tato    
santesu deyyadhammesu       dīpaṃ nākamhamattano .

Te hi nūna ito gantvā     yoniṃ laddhāna mānusiṃ    
vadaññū sīlasampannā         kāhāma kusalaṃ bahunti .

Gaṇapetavatthu dasamaṃ .

[131] 1[^255-1] diṭṭhā tayā nirayā tiracchānayonī    
petā asurā vāpi [^255-1] manussadevā    
sayamaddasa kammavipākamattano    
nessāmi taṃ pāṭaliputtamakkhataṃ    
tattha gantvā kusalaṃ karohi kammanti .

Atthakā mosi me yakkha      hitakāmosi devate    
karomi tuyhaṃ vacanaṃ          tvamasi ācariyo me .

Diṭṭhā mayā nirayā tiracchānayonī  
petā asurā vāpi manussadevā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 256 -->
<a id="page-256"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 256]*

Sayamaddasa kammavipākamattano    
kāhāmi puññāni anappakānīti .ด    
pāṭaliputtapetavatthu ekādasamaṃ .

[132] 1[^256-2] ayañca te pokkharaṇī surammā    
samā suppatitthā [^256-1] ca mahodakā ca    
supupphitā bhamaragaṇānukiṇṇā    
kathaṃ tayā laddhā ayaṃ manuññā .

Idañca te ambavanaṃ surammaṃ    
sabbotukaṃ dhārayati [^256-2] phalāni    
supupphitaṃ bhamaragaṇānukiṇṇaṃ    
kathaṃ tayā laddhamidaṃ vimānanti .

Ambapakkodakaṃ yāguṃ         sītacchāyaṃ [^256-3] manoramaṃ    
dhītāya dinnadānena         tena me idha labbhatīti .

Sandiṭṭhikaṃ eva passatha dānassa    
damassa saṃyamassa vipākaṃ    
dāsī ahaṃ ayyakulesu hutvā    
suṇisā homi agārassa issarāti .

Asātaṃ sātarūpena          piyarūpena appiyaṃ    
dukkhaṃ sukhassa rūpena         pamattaṃ ativattatīti [^256-4] .ๆ    
ambavanapetavatthu dvādasamaṃ .

#[^256-1] sutitthātipi .  [^256-2] ma. Dhārayate .  [^256-3] ma. Yu. Sītacchāyā manoramā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 257 -->
<a id="page-257"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 257]*

[133] 1[^257-3] yaṃ dadāti na taṃ hoti       detheva dānaṃ datvāna    
ubhayaṃ tarati [^257-1] ubhayaṃ        tena dānena [^257-2] gacchati    
jāgaratha mā pamajjathāti .

Akkharukkhapetavatthu terasamaṃ .

[134] 1[^257-4] mayaṃ bhoge saṃharimha        samena visamena ca    
te aññe paribhuñjanti       mayaṃ dukkhassa bhāginīti .

Bhogasaṃharapetavatthu catuddasamaṃ .

[135] 1[^257-5] saṭṭhī vassasahassāni        paripuṇṇāni sabbaso    
niraye paccamānānaṃ         kadā anto bhavissati .

Natthi anto kuto anto     na anto paṭidissati    
tathā hi pakataṃ pāpaṃ         mama tuyhañca mārisa .

Dujjīvitamajīvamha            ye sante [^257-3] na dadamhase    
santesu deyyadhammesu       dīpaṃ nākamhamattano .

So hi nūna ito gantvā     yoniṃ laddhāna mānusiṃ    
vadaññū sīlasampanno         kāhāmi kusalaṃ bahunti .

Seṭṭhiputtapetavatthu paṇṇarasamaṃ .

[136] 1[^257-6] kinnu ummattarūpova        migo bhantova dhāvasi    
nisaṃsayaṃ pāpakammaṃ [^257-4]        kinnu saddayase tuvanti .

#[^257-1] ma. Dhārati dānaṃ .  [^257-2] ma. Ayaṃ pāṭho natthi .  [^257-3] yesaṃ notipi dissati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 258 -->
<a id="page-258"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 258]*

Ahaṃ bhante petomhi        duggato yamalokiko    
pāpakammaṃ karitvāna         petalokaṃ ito gato .

Saṭṭhī kūṭasahassāni          paripuṇṇāni sabbaso    
sīse mayhaṃ nipatanti         te bhindanti ca matthakanti .

Kinnu kāyena vācāya       manasā dukkaṭaṃ kataṃ    
kissa kammavipākena         petalokaṃ ito gatoti [^258-1] .

Saṭṭhī kūṭasahassāni          paripuṇṇāni sabbaso    
sīse tuyhaṃ nipatanti         te bhindanti ca matthakanti .

Athaddusāsiṃ sambuddhaṃ         sunettaṃ bhāvitindriyaṃ    
nisinnaṃ rukkhamūlasmiṃ          jhāyantaṃ akutobhayaṃ .

Sālittakappahārena         bhindissaṃ tassa matthakaṃ    
tassa kammavipākena         idaṃ dukkhaṃ nigacchissaṃ .

Saṭṭhī kūṭasahassāni          paripuṇṇāni sabbaso    
sīse mayhaṃ nipatanti         te bhindanti ca matthakanti .

Dhammena te kāpurisa    
saṭṭhī kūṭasahassāni          paripuṇṇāni sabbaso    
sīse tuyhaṃ [^258-2] nipatanti      te bhindanti ca matthakanti .

Saṭṭhikūṭasahassapetavatthu soḷasamaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 259 -->
<a id="page-259"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 259]*

Tassuddānaṃ    
ambasakkharo serissako ca    piṅgalo revatī ucchukhādako [^259-1]    
dve kumārā dve gūthabhojanā [^259-2]  pāṭalipokkharaṇī ca [^259-3]    
akkhato [^259-4] bhogasaṃharā      seṭṭhiputtā saṭṭhikūṭasahassāni    
(5)      vaggo tena pavuccatīti [^259-6] .

(7)    
mahāvaggo catuttho .

Petavatthu samattaṃ .

#[^259-1] ma. Ucchu .  [^259-2] ma. Gūthā .  [^259-3] ma. Gaṇapāṭali ambavanaṃ .  [^259-4] ma. Akkharukkha .

#(5) ma. Iti soḷasavatthūni .  [^259-6] yu. Idaṃ udānaṃ na dissati . (7) ma. Vagguddānaṃ    
#         urago uparivaggo          cūḷamahāticatudhā    
#         vatthūni ekapaññāsaṃ         catudhā bhāṇavārato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 260 -->
<a id="page-260"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 260]*

Suttantapiṭake khuddakanikāyassa theragāthā    
namo tassa bhagavato arahato sammāsambuddhassa .

[137] sīhānaṃva nadantānaṃ          dāṭhīnaṃ girigabbhare    
suṇātha bhāvitattānaṃ         gāthā atthupanāyikā [^260-1]    
yathānāmā yathāgottā      yathādhammavihārino    
yathādhimuttā sappaññā       vihariṃsu atanditā    
tattha tattha vipassitvā       phusitvā accutaṃ padaṃ    
katantaṃ paccavekkhantā       imamatthaṃ abhāsiṃsu [^260-2] .

ekanipāto    
theragāthāya ekanipātassa paṭhamavaggo    
[138] [^260-1] channā me kuṭikā sukhā nivātā  vassa deva yathāsukhaṃ    
cittaṃ me susamāhitaṃ vimuttaṃ  ātāpī viharāmi vassadevāti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā subhūti thero gāthaṃ abhāsitthāti .

[139] [^260-2] upasanto uparato          mantabhāṇī anuddhato    
dhunāti pāpake dhamme       dumapattaṃva mālutoti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā mahākoṭṭhito [^260-3] thero gāthaṃ abhāsittha [^260-4] .

#[^260-1] yu. Attupanāyikā .  [^260-2] ma. Yu. Abhāsisuṃ .  [^260-3] ma. Mahākoṭuṭhiko .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 261 -->
<a id="page-261"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 261]*

[140] [^261-3] paññaṃ imaṃ passa tathāgatānaṃ    
aggi yathā pajjalito nisīve [^261-1]    
ālokadā cakkhudadā bhavanti    
ye āgatānaṃ vinayanti kaṅkhanti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā kaṅkhārevato [^261-2] thero gāthaṃ abhāsittha .

[141] [^261-4] sabbhireva samāsetha         paṇḍitehatthadassibhi    
atthaṃ mahantaṃ gambhīraṃ         duddasaṃ nipuṇaṃ aṇaṇa    
dhīrā samadhigacchanti          appamattā vicakkhaṇāti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā puṇṇo mantāniputto thero gāthaṃ abhāsittha .

[142] [^261-5] yo duddamayo damena danto    
dabbo santusito vitiṇṇakaṅkho    
vijitāvi apetabheravo hi`    
dabbo so parinibbuto ṭhitattoti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā dabbo thero gāthaṃ abhāsittha .

[143] [^261-6] yo sītavanaṃ upāgā bhikkhu    
eko santusito samāhitatto    
vijitāvi apetalomahaṃso    
rakkhaṃ kāyagatāsatiṃ dhītimāti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā sītavaniyo thero [^261-3] .

#[^261-1] ma. Yu. Nisīthe . Nissivetipi .  [^261-2] po. Revato .  [^261-3] ma. Ito paraṃ gāthaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 262 -->
<a id="page-262"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 262]*

[144] [^262-7] yo pānudi maccurājassa senaṃ    
naḷasetatava sudubbalaṃ mahogho    
vijitāvi apetabheravo hi    
danto so parinibbuto ṭhitattoti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā bhalliyo thero .

[145] [^262-8] yo duddamayo damena danto    
dhīro [^262-1] santusito vitiṇṇakaṅkho    
vijitāvi apetalomahaṃso    
vītarāgo [^262-2] parinibbuto ṭhitattoti .

Vīro thero [^262-3] .

[146] [^262-9] svāgataṃ nāpagataṃ [^262-4]        nayidaṃ dummantitaṃ mama    
pavibhattesu [^262-5] dhammesu      yaṃ seṭṭhaṃ tadupāgaminti .

Pilindavaccho thero .

[147] 10 vihari apekkhaṃ idha vā huraṃ vā    
yo vedagū santusito [^262-6] yatatto    
sabbesu dhammesu anūpalitto    
lokassa jaññā udayabbayañcāti .

Puṇṇamāso thero .

#[^262-1] ma. Yu. Vīro .  [^262-2] ma. Yu. Vīro so .  [^262-3] po. Theroti .  [^262-4] ma. Na durāgataṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 263 -->
<a id="page-263"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 263]*

Uddānaṃ    
subhūti koṭṭhito [^263-1] thero    kaṅkhārevatapuṇṇako [^263-2]    
mantāniputto dabbo ca      sītavaniyo ca bhalliyo    
vīro pilindavaccho ca        puṇṇamāso tamonudoti .

<p align="center">Vaggo paṭhamo  .</p>

theragāthāya ekanipātassa dutiyavaggo    
[148] [^263-1] pāmujjabahulo bhikkhu         dhamme buddhappavedite    
adhigacche padaṃ santaṃ         saṅkhārūpasamaṃ sukhanti .

Cūḷavaccho [^263-3] thero .

[149] [^263-2] paññābalī sīlavatūpapanno    
samāhito jhānarato satīmā    
yadatthiyaṃ bhojanaṃ bhuñjamāno    
kaṅkhetha [^263-4] kālaṃ idha vītarāgoti .

Mahāvaccho [^263-5] thero .

[150] [^263-3] nīlabbhavaṇṇā rucirā         sītavārī sucindharā    
indagopakasañchannā         te selā ramayanti manti .

Vanavaccho thero .

[151] [^263-4] upajjhāyo maṃ avacāsi [^263-6]    ito gacchāmi sīvaka    
#[^263-1] ma. Yu. Koṭṭhiko .  [^263-2] ma. Kaṅkhārevatasammato . Yu. Kaṅkhārevatasubbato .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 264 -->
<a id="page-264"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 264]*

Gāme me vasati kāyo      araññaṃ me gato mano[^264-5]    
semānakopi gacchāmi        natthi saṅgo vijānatanti .

Vanavacchassa therassa sāmaṇero .

[152] [^264-5] pañca chinde pañca jahe      pañca cuttari bhāvaye    
pañcasaṅgātigo bhikkhu        oghatiṇṇoti vuccatīti .

Kuṇḍadhāno thero .

[153] [^264-6] yathāpi bhaddo ājañño      naṅgalāvattanī sikhī    
gacchati appakasirena         evaṃ rattindivā mama    
gacchanti appakasirena        sukhe laddhe nirāmiseti .

Belaṭṭhasīso thero .

[154] [^264-7] middhī yadā hoti mahagghaso ca    
niddāyitā samparivattasāyī    
mahāvarāhova nivāpapuṭṭho    
punappunaṃ gabbhamupeti mandoti .

Dāsako thero .

[155] [^264-8] ahu [^264-1] buddhassa dāyādo    bhikkhu bhesakaḷāvane    
kevalaṃ aṭṭhisaññāya         aphari paṭhaviṃ imaṃ    
maññehaṃ kāmarāgaṃ so       khippameva pahīyatīti .

Siṅgālapitā thero .

[156] [^264-9] udakaṃ hi nayanti nettikā     usukārā namayanti tejanaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 265 -->
<a id="page-265"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 265]*

Dāruṃ namayanti tacchakā       attānaṃ damayanti subbatāti .

Kuḷo thero .

[157] 10 maraṇe me bhayaṃ natthi        nikanti natthi jīvite    
sandehaṃ nikkhipissāmi        sampajāno patissatoti .

Ajito thero .

Uddānaṃ    
cūḷavaccho mahāvaccho        vanavaccho ca sīvako    
kuṇḍadhāno ca belaṭṭhi       dāsako ca tato paraṃ    
siṅgālapitiko thero        kuḷo ca ajito dasāti .

<p align="center">Vaggo dutiyo .</p>

theragāthāya ekanipātassa tatiyavaggo    
[158]  [^265-1] nāhaṃ bhayassa bhāyāmi        satthā no amatassa kovido    
yattha bhayaṃ nāvatiṭṭhati        tena maggena vajanti bhikkhavoti .

Nigrodho thero .

[159]  [^265-2] nīlā sugīvā sikhino         morā kāraviyaṃ [^265-1] abhinadanti    
te sītavātakalitā [^265-2]       suttaṃ jhānaṃ [^265-3] nibodhentīti    
cittako thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 266 -->
<a id="page-266"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 266]*

[160] [^266-3] ahaṃ kho veḷugumbasmiṃ        bhutvāna madhupāyāsaṃ    
padakkhiṇaṃ sammasanto         khandhānaṃ udayabbayaṃ    
sānana paṭiggamissāmi         vivekamanubrūhayanti .

Gosālo thero .

[161] [^266-4] anuvassiko pabbajito        passa dhammasudhammataṃ    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Sugandho thero .

[162] [^266-5] obhāsajātaṃ phalagaṃ          cittaṃ yassa abhiṇhaso    
tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsajja         kaṇha dukkhaṃ nigacchasīti .

Nandiyo thero .

[163] [^266-6] sutvā subhāsitaṃ vācaṃ        buddhassādiccabandhuno    
paccabyādhiṃ hi nipuṇaṃ         vālaggaṃ usunā yathāti .

Abhayo thero .

[164] [^266-7] dabbaṃ kusaṃ poṭakilaṃ          usīraṃ muñjapabbajaṃ    
urasā panudahissāmi         vivekamanubrūhayanti .

Lomasakaṅgiyo thero .

[165] [^266-8] kacci no vatthapasuto        kacci no bhūsanārato    
kacci sīlamayaṃ gandhaṃ          tvaṃ vāsi [^266-1] netarā pajāti .

Jambugāmikaputto thero .

[166] [^266-9] samunnamayamattānaṃ           usukārova tejanaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 267 -->
<a id="page-267"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 267]*

Cittaṃ ujuṃ karitvāna         avijjaṃ bhinda [^267-1] hāritāti .

Hārito thero .

[167] 10 ābādhe me samuppanne     sati me upapajjatha    
ābādho me samuppanno     kālo me nappamajjitunti .

Uttiyo thero .

Uddānaṃ    
nigrodho cittako thero     gosālatthero sugandho    
nandiyo abhayo thero       thero lomasakaṅgiyo    
jambugāmikaputto ca         hārito uttiyo isīti .

<p align="center">Vaggo tatiyo .</p>

theragāthāya ekanipātassa catutthavaggo    
[168] [^267-1] phuṭṭho ḍaṃsehi makasehi       araññasmiṃ brahāvane    
nāgo saṅgāmasīseva        sato tatrādhivāsayeti .

Gahuratīriyo thero [^267-2] .

[169] [^267-2] ajaraṃ jīramānena           tappamānena nibbutiṃ    
nirāmisaṃ [^267-3] paramaṃ santiṃ      yogakkhemaṃ anuttaranti .

Suppiyo thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 268 -->
<a id="page-268"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 268]*

[170] [^268-3] yathāpi ekaputtasmiṃ         piyasmiṃ kusalī siyā    
evaṃ sabbesu pāṇesu       sabbattha kusalo siyāti .

Sopāko thero    
[171] [^268-4] anāsannavarā etā        niccameva vijānatā    
gāmā araññamāgamma        tato gehaṃ upāvisiṃ    
tato uṭṭhāya pakkāmiṃ       anāmantetvā posiyoti .

Posiyo thero .

[172] [^268-5] sukhaṃ sukhattho labhate tadācaraṃ    
kittiñca pappoti yasassa vaḍḍhati    
yo ariyamaṭṭhaṅgikamañjasaṃ ujuṃ    
bhāveti maggaṃ amatassa pattiyāti .

Sāmaññakāni thero .

[173] [^268-6] sādhu sutaṃ sādhu caritakaṃ    
sādhu sadā aniketavihāro    
atthapucchanaṃ padakkhiṇakammaṃ    
etaṃ sāmaññaṃ akiñcanassāti .

Kumāputto thero .

[174] [^268-7] nānājanapadaṃ yanti          vicarantā asaññatā    
samādhiñca virādhenti        kiṃsu raṭṭhacariyā karissati .

Tasmā vineyya sārambhaṃ      jhāyeyya apurakkhatoti .

Kumāputtattherassa sahāyako thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 269 -->
<a id="page-269"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 269]*

[175] [^269-8] yo iddhiyā sarabhuṃ aṭṭhapesi    
gavampati so asito anejo    
taṃ sabbasaṅgātigataṃ mahāmuniṃ    
devā namassanti bhavassa pāragunti .

Gavampati thero .

[176] [^269-9] sattiyā viya omaṭṭho       ḍayhamāneva [^269-1] matthake    
kāmarāgappahānāya         sato bhikkhu paribbajeti .

Tisso thero .

[177] 10 sattiyā viya omaṭṭho       ḍayhamāneva [^269-1] matthake    
bhavarāgappahānāya          sato bhikkhu paribbajeti .

Vaḍḍhamāno thero .

Uddānaṃ    
gahuratīriyo [^269-2] suppiyo      sopāko ca [^269-3] posiyo ca [^269-4]    
sāmaññakāni kumāputto      kumāputtasahāyako    
gavampati tissatthero        vaḍḍhamāno mahāyasoti .

<p align="center">Vaggo catuttho .</p>


<!-- Siam Rath Page 270 -->
<a id="page-270"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 270]*

Theragāthāya ekanipātassa pañcamavaggo    
[178] [^270-1] vivaramanupatanti vijjutā    
vebhārassa ca paṇḍavassa ca    
nagavivaragato ca jhāyati    
putto appaṭimassa tādinoti .

Sirivaḍḍho thero .

[179] [^270-2] hale [^270-1] upahale sīsupahale   halā [^270-2] upahalā sīsupahalā    
patissatikā [^270-3] nu kho viharatha  āgato vo vālaṃ viya vedhiyatīti . [^270-4]    
khadiravaniyo thero .

[180] [^270-3] sumuttiko sumuttiko sāhu     samuttikomhi tīhi khujjakehi    
asitāsu mayā naṅgalāsu      mayā khuddakuddālāsu mayā .

Yadipi idhameva idhameva       atha vāpi alameva alameva    
jhāya sumaṅgala jhāya sumaṅgala   appamatto vihara sumaṅgalāti .

Sumaṅgalo thero .

[181] [^270-4] mataṃ vā amma rodanti       yo vā jīvaṃ na dissati    
jīvantaṃ maṃ amma dissanti [^270-5]   kasmā maṃ amma rodasīti .

Sānu thero .

[182] [^270-5] yathāpi bhaddo ājañño      khalitvā paṭitiṭṭhati    
#[^270-1] ma. Yu. Cāle upacāle sīsupacāle .  [^270-2] ma. Yu. Ime pāṭhā na dissanti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 271 -->
<a id="page-271"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 271]*

Evaṃ dassanasampannaṃ         sammāsambuddhasāvakanti .

Ramaṇīyavihārī thero .

[183] [^271-6] saddhāyāhaṃ pabbajito        agārasmā anagāriyaṃ    
sati paññā ca me vuḍḍhā     cittañca susamāhitaṃ .

Kāmaṃ karassu rūpāni         neva maṃ byādhayissasīti .

Samiddhi thero .

[184] [^271-7] namo te buddhavīratthu        vippamuttosi sabbadhi    
tuyhāpadāne viharaṃ         viharāmi anāsavoti .

Ujjayo thero .

[185] [^271-8] yato ahaṃ pabbajito         agārasmā anagāriyaṃ    
nābhijānāmi saṅkappaṃ        anariyaṃ dosasaṃhitanti .

Sañjayo thero .

[186] [^271-9] vihavihābhinadite [^271-1]         sippikābhirutehi ca    
na me taṃ phandati cittaṃ       ekattaṃ nirataṃ [^271-2] hi meti .

Rāmaṇeyyako thero .

[187] 10 dharaṇīva [^271-3] siccati [^271-4] vāti   māluto vijjutā carati nabhe    
upasammanti vitakkā         cittaṃ susamāhitaṃ mamanti [^271-5] .

Vimalo thero .

Uddānaṃ    
sirivaḍḍho revato thero     khadiravaniyo sumaṅgalo [^271-6]    
#[^271-1] ma. Cihacihābhinadite .  [^271-2] yu. Ekattanirattaṃ .  [^271-3] yu. Dharaṇī ca .  [^271-4] ma. Siñcati .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 272 -->
<a id="page-272"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 272]*

Sānu [^272-1] ramaṇīyavihārī ca     samiddhi ujjayasañjayo [^272-2]    
rāmaṇeyyako so thero     vimalo ca raṇañjahoti [^272-3] .

<p align="center">Vaggo pañcamo .</p>

theragāthāya ekanipātassa chaṭṭhavaggo    
[188] [^272-1] vassati devo yathā sugītaṃ     channā me kuṭikā sukhā nivātā    
cittaṃ susamāhitañca mayhaṃ      atha ce patthayasi pavassa devāti .

Godhiko thero .

[189] [^272-2] vassati devo yathā sugītaṃ     channā me kuṭikā sukhā nivātā    
cittaṃ susamāhitañca mayhaṃ [^272-4]   atha ce patthayasi pavassa devāti .

Subāhu thero .

[190] [^272-3] vassati devo yathā sugītaṃ     channā me kuṭikā sukhā nivātā    
tassaṃ viharāmi appamatto     atha ce patthayasi pavassa devāti .

Valliyo thero .

[191] [^272-4] vassati devo yathā sugītaṃ     channā me kuṭikā sukhā nivātā    
tassaṃ viharāmi adutiyo       atha ce patthayasi pavassa devāti .

Uttiyo thero .

#[^272-1] ma. Yu. Ayaṃ pāṭho natthi .  [^272-2] ma. Sañjayā .  [^272-3] yu. Raṇañjayoti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 273 -->
<a id="page-273"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 273]*

[192] [^273-5] āsandikuṭikaṃ katvā         ogayha añjanāvanaṃ    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Añjanāvaniyo thero .

[193] [^273-6] ko kuṭikāyaṃ bhikkhu kuṭikāyaṃ    vītarāgo susamāhitacitto .

Evaṃ jānāhi āvuso       amoghā te kuṭikā katāti .

Kuṭivihārī thero .

[194] [^273-7] ayamāhu purāṇiyā kuṭikā [^273-1]  aññaṃ patthayase navaṃ kuṭiṃ    
āsaṃ kuṭiyā virājaya        dukkhā bhikkhu puna navā kuṭīti .

Kuṭivihārī [^273-2] thero .

[195] [^273-8] ramaṇīyā me kuṭikā         saddhādeyyā manoramā    
na me attho kumārīhi       yesaṃ attho tahiṃ gacchatha nāriyoti .

Ramaṇīyakuṭiko thero .

[196] [^273-9] saddhāyāhaṃ pabbajito        araññe me kuṭikā katā    
appamatto ca ātāpī       sampajāno patissatoti .

Kosallavihārī thero .

[197] 10 te me ijjhiṃsu saṅkappā    yadattho pāvisiṃ kuṭiṃ    
vijjāvimuttiṃ paccessaṃ       mānānusayamujjahanti .

Sīvalī thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 274 -->
<a id="page-274"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 274]*

Uddānaṃ    
godhiko ca subāhu ca        valliyo uttiyo isi    
añjanāvaniyo thero        duve kuṭivihārino    
ramaṇīyakuṭiko thero         kosallavhayasīvalīti .

<p align="center">Vaggo chaṭṭho .</p>

theragāthāya ekanipātassa sattamavaggo    
[198] [^274-1] passati passo passantaṃ       apassantañca passati    
apassanto apassantaṃ        passantañca na passatīti .

Vappo thero .

[199] [^274-2] ekakā mayaṃ araññe viharāma    
apaviddhaṃva vanasmiṃ dārukaṃ    
tassa me bahukā pihayanti    
nerayikā viya saggagāminanti .

Vajjiputtako [^274-1] thero .

[200] [^274-3] cutā patanti patitā         giddhā ca punarāgatā    
katakiccaṃ ratarammaṃ           sukhenanvāgataṃ sukhanti .

Pakkho thero .

[201] [^274-4] dumavhayāya uppanno        jāto paṇḍaraketunā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 275 -->
<a id="page-275"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 275]*

Ketuhā ketunā yeva       mahāketuṃ padhaṃsayīti .

Vimalakoṇḍañño thero .

[202] [^275-5] ukkhepakaṭavacchassa          saṅkalitaṃ bahūhi vassehi    
taṃ bhāsati gahaṭṭhānaṃ         sunisinno uḷārapāmojjoti .

Ukkhepakaṭavaccho thero .

[203] [^275-6] anusāsi mahāvīro          sabbadhammāna pāragū    
tassāhaṃ dhammaṃ sutvāna       vihāsiṃ santike rato [^275-1]    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Meghiyo thero .

[204] [^275-7] kilesā jhāpitā mayhaṃ       bhavā sabbe samūhatā    
vikkhīṇo jātisaṃsāro        natthidāni punabbhavoti .

Ekadhammasavaniyo thero .

[205] [^275-8] adhicetaso appamajjato      munino monapathesu sikkhato    
sobhā na bhavanti tādino     upasantassa sadā satīmatoti .

Ekudāniyo thero .

[206] [^275-9] sutvāna dhammaṃ mahato mahārasaṃ    
sabbaññutaññāṇavarena desitaṃ    
maggaṃ papajjiṃ amatassa pattiyā    
so yogakkhemassa pathassa kovidoti .

Channo thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 276 -->
<a id="page-276"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 276]*

[207] 10 sīlameva idha aggaṃ         paññavā pana uttamo    
manussesu ca devesu        sīlapaññāṇato jayanti .

Puṇṇo thero .

Uddānaṃ    
vappo  ca vajjiputto ca     pakkho vimalakoṇḍañño    
ukkhepakaṭavaccho ca         meghiyo ekadhammiko    
ekudāniyachanno [^276-1] ca      puṇṇatthero mahabbaloti .

<p align="center">Vaggo sattamo .</p>

theragāthāya ekanipātassa aṭṭhamavaggo    
[208] [^276-1] susukhumanipuṇatthadassinā    
matikusalena nivātavuttinā    
saṃsevitabuddhasīlinā    
nibbānaṃ na hi tena dullabhanti .

Vacchapālo thero .

[209] [^276-2] yathā kalīro susu vaḍḍhitaggo  
dunnikkhaso [^276-2] hoti pasākhajāto  
evaṃ ahaṃ bhariyāyānītāya  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 277 -->
<a id="page-277"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 277]*

Anumañña maṃ pabbajitomhidānīti .

Ātumo thero .

[210] [^277-3] jiṇṇañca disvā dukkhitañca byādhitaṃ    
matañca disvā gatamāyusaṅkhayaṃ    
tato ahaṃ nikkhamitūna pabbajiṃ    
pahāya kāmāni manoramānīti .

Māṇavo thero .

[211] [^277-4] kāmachando ca byāpādo     thīnamiddhañca bhikkhuno    
uddhaccaṃ vicikicchā ca        sabbasova na vijjatīti .

Suyāmano thero .

[212] [^277-5] sādhu suvahitānaṃ [^277-1] dassanaṃ    kaṅkhā chijjati buddhi vaḍḍhati    
bālampi karonti paṇḍitaṃ      tasmā sādhu sataṃ samāgamoti .

Susārado thero .

[213] [^277-6] uppatantesu nipate         nipatantesu uppate    
vase avasamānesu          ramamānesu no rameti .

Piyañjaho thero .

[214] [^277-7] idaṃ pure cittamacāri cārikaṃ    
yenicchakaṃ yatthakāmaṃ yathāsukhaṃ    
tadajjahaṃ niggahissāmi yoniso    
hatthiṃ pabhinnaṃ viya aṅkusaggāhoti .

Hatthārohaputto thero  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 278 -->
<a id="page-278"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 278]*

[215] [^278-8] anekajātisaṃsāraṃ           sandhāvissaṃ anibbisaṃ    
tassa me dukkhajātassa       dukkhakkhandho paraddhatoti [^278-1] .

Meṇḍasiro thero .

[216] [^278-9] sabbo rāgo pahīno me     sabbo doso samūhato    
sabbo me vigato moho     sītibhūtosmi nibbutoti [^278-1] .

Rakkhito thero .

[217] 10 yaṃ mayā pakataṃ kammaṃ        appaṃ vā yadi vā bahuṃ    
sabbametaṃ parikkhīṇaṃ          natthidāni punabbhavoti .

Uggo thero .

Uddānaṃ    
vacchapālo ca yo thero     ātumo māṇavo isi    
suyāmano susārado         thero yo ca piyañjaho    
ārohaputto meṇḍasiro     rakkhito uggasavhayoti .

<p align="center">Vaggo aṭṭhamo .</p>

theragāthāya ekanipātassa navamavaggo    
[218] [^278-1] yaṃ mayā pakataṃ pāpaṃ         pubbe aññāsu jātisu    
idheva taṃ vedaniyaṃ          vatthu aññaṃ na vijjatīti .

Samitigutto thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 279 -->
<a id="page-279"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 279]*

[219] [^279-2] yena yena subhikkhāni        sivāni abhayāni ca    
tena puttaka gacchassu        mā sokā pahato bhavāti .

Kassapo thero .

[220] [^279-3] sīhappamatto vihara          rattindivamatandito    
bhāvehi kusalaṃ dhammaṃ         jaha sīghaṃ samussayanti .

Sīho thero .

[221] [^279-4] sabbarattiṃ supitvāna         divā saṅgaṇike rato    
kudāssu nāma dummedho      dukkhassantaṃ karissatīti .

Nīto thero .

[222] [^279-5] cittanimittassa kovido       pavivekarasaṃ vijāniya    
jhāyaṃ nipako patissato       adhigaccheyya sukhaṃ nirāmisanti .

Sunāgo thero .

[223] [^279-6] ito bahiddhā puthuaññavādinaṃ    
maggo na nibbānagamo yathā ayaṃ    
itissu saṅghaṃ bhagavānusāsati    
satthā sayaṃ pāṇitaleva dassayanti .

Nāgito thero .

[224] [^279-7] khandhā diṭṭhā yathābhūtaṃ       bhavā sabbe padālitā    
vikkhīṇo jātisaṃsāro        natthidāni punabbhavoti .

Paviṭṭho [^279-1] thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 280 -->
<a id="page-280"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 280]*

[225] [^280-8] asakkhiṃ vata attānaṃ         uddhātuṃ udakā thalaṃ    
vuyhamāno mahoghova [^280-1]     saccāni paṭivijjhahanti    
ajjuno thero .

[226] [^280-9] uttiṇṇā paṅkā palipā      pātālā parivajjitā    
mutto oghā ca ganthā ca    sabbe mānā visaṃhatāti .

Devasabho thero .

[227] 10 pañcakkhandhā pariññātā     tiṭṭhanti chinnamūlakā    
vikkhīṇo jātisaṃsāro        natthidāni punabbhavoti .

Sāmidatto thero .

Uddānaṃ    
thero samitigutto ca        kassapo sīhasavhayo    
nīto sunāgo nāgito       paviṭṭho ajjuno isi    
devasabho ca yo thero      sāmidatto mahabbaloti .

<p align="center">Vaggo navamo .</p>

theragāthāya ekanipātassa dasamavaggo    
[228] [^280-1] na tathāmataṃ satarasaṃ sudhannaṃ     yaṃ mayajja paribhuttaṃ    
aparimitadassinā gotamena     buddhena desito dhammoti .

Paripuṇṇako thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 281 -->
<a id="page-281"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 281]*

[229] [^281-2] yassāsavā parikkhīṇā        āhāre ca anissito    
suññato animitto ca        vimokkho yassa gocaro    
ākāseva sakuntānaṃ        padantassa duranvayanti .

Vijayo thero .

[230] [^281-3] dukkhā kāmā eraka        na sukhā kāmā eraka    
yo kāme kāmayati         dukkhaṃ so kāmayati eraka    
yo kāme na kāmayati       dukkhaṃ so na kāmayati erakāti .

Erako thero .

[231] [^281-4] namo hi tassa bhagavato       sakyaputtassa sirīmato    
tenāyaṃ aggaputtena [^281-1]     aggadhammo sudesitoti .

Mettaji thero .

[232] [^281-5] andhohaṃ hatanettosmi       kantāraddhāna pakkhanto [^281-2]    
sayamānopi gamissaṃ [^281-3]       na sahāyena pāpenāti .

Cakkhupālo thero .

[233] [^281-6] ekaṃ pupphaṃ cajitvāna        asītivassakoṭiyo [^281-4]    
saggesu paricāretvā       sesakenamhi nibbutoti .

Khaṇḍasumano thero .

[234] [^281-7] hitvā satapalaṃ kaṃsaṃ          sovaṇṇaṃ satarājikaṃ    
aggahiṃ mattikāpattaṃ         idaṃ dutiyābhisecananti .

Tisso thero .

#[^281-1] ma. Yu. Aggapattena .  [^281-2] sī. Yu. Pakkhanno . Ma. Pakkhando .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 282 -->
<a id="page-282"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 282]*

[235] [^282-8] rūpaṃ disvā sati muṭṭhā       piyanimittaṃ manasikaroto    
sārattacitto vedeti       tañca ajjhosa tiṭṭhati    
tassa vaḍḍhanti āsavā       bhavamūlopagāminoti .

Abhayo thero .

[236] [^282-9] saddaṃ sutvā sati muṭṭhā      piyanimittaṃ manasikaroto    
sārattacitto vedeti       tañca ajjhosa tiṭṭhati    
tassa vaḍḍhanti āsavā       saṃsāraṃ upagāminoti .

Uttiyo thero .

[237] 10 sammappadhānasampanno        satipaṭṭhānagocaro    
vimuttikusumasañchanno         parinibbāyissatyanāsavoti [^282-1] .

Devasabho thero .

Uddānaṃ    
paripuṇṇako ca vijayo        erako mettajī muni    
cakkhupālo khaṇḍasumano       tisso abhayo ca [^282-2]    
uttiyo mahāpañño         thero devasabhopi cāti .

<p align="center">Vaggo dasamo .</p>

theragāthāya ekanipātassa ekādasamavaggo  
[238] [^282-1] hitvā gihitvaṃ [^282-3] anavositatto  
mukhanaṅgalī odariko kusīto  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 283 -->
<a id="page-283"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 283]*

Mahāvarāhova nivāpapuṭṭho    
punappunaṃ gabbhamupeti mandoti .

Belaṭṭhakāni [^283-1] thero .

[239] [^283-2] mānena vañcitāse        saṅkhāresu saṅkilissamānāse    
lābhālābhena mathitā       samādhiṃ nādhigacchantīti .

Setuccho thero .

[240] [^283-3] nāhaṃ etena atthiko      sukhito dhammarasena tappito    
pitvāna rasaggamuttamaṃ       na ca kāhāmi rasena [^283-2] santhavanti .

Bandhuro thero .

[241] [^283-4] lahuko vata me kāyo      phuṭṭho ca pītisukhena vipulena    
tūlamiva eritaṃ mālutena     pilavativa me kāyoti .

Khitako thero .

[242] [^283-5] ukkaṇṭhitopi na vase       ramamānopi pakkame    
na tvevānatthasahitaṃ        vase vāsaṃ vicakkhaṇoti .

Malitavambho thero .

[243] [^283-6] sataliṅgassa atthassa        satalakkhaṇadhārino    
ekaṅgadassī dummedho      satadassī ca paṇḍitoti .

Suhemanto thero .

[244] [^283-7] pabbajiṃ tulayitvāna         agārasmā anagāriyaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 284 -->
<a id="page-284"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 284]*

Tisso vijjā anuppattā    kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Dhammasaṃvaro [^284-1] thero .

[245] [^284-8] sa vīsaṃvassasatiko          pabbajiṃ anagāriyaṃ    
tisso vijjā anuppattā    kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Dhammasaṭapituthero [^284-2] .

[246] [^284-9] na nūnāyaṃ paramahitānukampino    
rahogato anuvigaṇeti sāsanaṃ    
tathā hayaṃ vīharati pākatindriyo    
migī yathā taruṇajātikā vaneti .

Saṅgharakkhito thero .

[247] 10 nagā nagaggesu susaṃvirūḷhā    
udaggameghena navena sittā    
vivekakāmassa araññasaññino    
janeti bhiyyo usabhassa kalyatanti .

Usabho thero .

Uddānaṃ    
belaṭṭhakāni [^284-3] setuccho   bandhuro khitako isi    
malitavambho suhemanto      dhammasaṃvaro [^284-4] dhammasaṭapitā    
#[^284-1] ma. Yu. Dhammasavo .  [^284-2] dhammasavapitutherotipi .  [^284-3] ma. Belaṭṭhāniko .  [^284-4] ma. Yu.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 285 -->
<a id="page-285"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 285]*

Saṅgharakkhitatthero ca       usabho ca mahāmunīti .

<p align="center">Vaggo ekādasamo .</p>

theragāthāya ekanipātassa dvādasamavaggo    
[248] [^285-1] duppabbajjaṃ ve duradhivāsā gehā    
dhammo gambhīro duradhigamā bhogā    
kicchā vutti no itarītareneva    
yuttaṃ cintetuṃ satatamaniccatanti .

Jento thero .

[249] [^285-2] tevijjohaṃ mahājhāyī       cetosamathakovido    
sadattho me anuppatto     kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Vacchagotto thero .

[250] [^285-3] acchodikā puthusilā        gonaṅgulamigāyutā    
ambusevālasañchannā       te selā ramayanti manti .

Vanavaccho thero .L    
[251] [^285-4] kāyaduṭṭhullagaruno         hiyyamānamhi jīvite    
sarīrasukhagiddhassa           kuto samaṇasādhutāti .

Adhimutto thero .

[252] [^285-5] esāvahiyyase pabbatena    bahukuṭajasallarikena 1  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 286 -->
<a id="page-286"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 286]*

Nesādakena girinā        yasassinā paricchadenāti .

Mahānāmo thero .

[253] [^286-6] cha phassāyatane hitvā      guttadvāro susaṃvuto    
aghamūlaṃ vametvāna [^286-1]      patto me āsavakkhayoti .

Pārāpariyo [^286-2] thero .

[254] [^286-7] suvilitto suvasano         sabbābharaṇabhūsito    
tisso vijjā ajjhagamiṃ      kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Yaso thero .

[255] [^286-8] abhisatthova [^286-3] nipatti vayo    
rūpaṃ aññamiva tatheva santaṃ    
tasseva sato avippavasato    
aññasseva sarāmi attānanti .

Kimbilo thero .

[256] [^286-9] rukkhamūlagahanaṃ pasakkhiya [^286-4]    
nibbānaṃ hadayasmiṃ opiya [^286-5]    
jhāya gotama mā ca pamādo    
kinte biḷibiḷikā karissatīti .

Vajjiputto thero .

[257] 10 pañcakkhandhā pariññātā    tiṭṭhanti chinnamūlakā    
#[^286-1] ma. Yu. Vamitvāna .  [^286-2] po. Pārāsiriyo . Ma. Pārāsariyo . [^286-3] po. Ma. Abhisatto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 287 -->
<a id="page-287"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 287]*

Dukkhakkhayo anuppatto      patto me āsavakkhayoti .

Isidatto thero .

Uddānaṃ    
jento ca vacchagotto ca   vaccho ca nāgasavhayo [^287-1]    
adhimutto mahānāmo       pārāpariyo yasopi ca    
kimbilo vajjiputto ca      isidatto mahāyasoti .

Dvādasamo vaggo .

Tatruddānaṃ bhavati .

Vīsuttarasataṃ therā         katakiccā anāsavā    
ekakeva nipātamhi        susaṅgītā mahesibhīti .

Ekanipāto niṭṭhito    
dukanipāto    
theragāthāya dukanipātassa paṭhamavaggo    
[258] [^287-1] natthi koci bhavo nicco     saṅkhārā vāpi sassatā    
uppajjanti ca te khandhā    cavanti aparāparaṃ .

Etamādīnavaṃ ñatvā        bhavenamhi anatthiko    
nissaṭo sabbakāmehi       patto me āsavakkhayoti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā uttaro thero gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 288 -->
<a id="page-288"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 288]*

[259] [^288-2] nayidaṃ anayena jīvitaṃ        nāhāro hadayassa santiko    
āhāraṭṭhitiko samussayo    iti disvāna carāmi esanaṃ .

Paṅkoti hi naṃ avedayuṃ [^288-1]   yāyaṃ vandanapūjanā kulesu    
sukhumaṃ sallaṃ durubbahaṃ        sakkāro kāpurisena dujjahoti .

Itthaṃ sudaṃ āyasmā piṇḍolabhāradvājo thero gāthāyo abhāsitthāti .

[260] [^288-3] makkaṭo pañcadvārāyaṃ      kuṭikāyaṃ pasakkhiya [^288-2]    
dvārena anupariyeti       ghaṭṭayanto muhuṃ muhuṃ .

Tiṭṭha makkaṭa mā dhāvi      na hi te taṃ yathā pure    
niggahitosi paññāya        neva [^288-3] dūraṃ gamissasīti .

Valliyo thero .

[261] [^288-4] tiṇṇaṃ me tālapattānaṃ      gaṅgātīre kuṭī katā    
chavasittova me patto      paṃsukūlañca cīvaraṃ .

Dvinnaṃ antaravassānaṃ       ekavācā me bhāsitā    
tatiye antaravassamhi       tamokkhandho padālitoti .

Gaṅgātīriyo thero .

[262] [^288-5] api ce hoti tevijjo     maccuhāyī anāsavo    
appaññātoti naṃ bālā     avajānanti ajānakā [^288-4] .

Yo ca kho annapānassa     lābhī hotīdha puggalo    
pāpadhammopi ce hoti      so nesaṃ hoti sakkatoti .

Ajino thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 289 -->
<a id="page-289"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 289]*

[263] [^289-6] yadāhaṃ dhammamassosiṃ        bhāsamānassa satthuno    
na kaṅkhamabhijānāmi         sabbaññū aparājite    
satthavāhe mahāvīre       sārathīnaṃ varuttame    
magge paṭipadāyaṃ vā       kaṅkhā mayhaṃ na vijjatīti .

Meḷajino thero .

[264] [^289-7] yathā agāraṃ ducchannaṃ       vuṭṭhi samativijjhati    
evaṃ abhāvitaṃ cittaṃ        rāgo samativijjhati .

Yathā agāraṃ succhannaṃ       vuṭṭhi na samativijjhati .

Evaṃ subhāvitaṃ cittaṃ        rāgo na samativijjhatīti .

Rādho thero .

[265] [^289-8] khīṇā hi mayhaṃ jāti        vusitaṃ jinasāsanaṃ    
pahīno jālasaṅkhāto       bhavanetti samūhatā .

Yassatthāya pabbajito       agārasmā anagāriyaṃ    
so me attho anuppatto   sabbasaṃyojanakkhayoti .

Surādho thero .

[266] [^289-9] sukhaṃ supanti munayo         ye itthīsu na bajjhare    
sadā ve rakkhitabbāsu      yāsu saccaṃ sudullabhaṃ .

Vadhaṃ carimha te kāma       anaṇādāni te mayaṃ    
gacchāmadāni nibbānaṃ       yattha gantvā na socatīti .

Gotamo thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 290 -->
<a id="page-290"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 290]*

[267] 10 pubbe hanati attānaṃ       pacchā hanati so pare    
suhataṃ hanti attānaṃ        vītaṃseneva pakkhimā .

Na brāhmaṇo bahivaṇṇo     antovaṇṇo hi brāhmaṇo    
yasmiṃ pāpāni kammāni      sa ve kaṇho sujampatīti .

Vasabho thero .

Uddānaṃ    
uttaro ceva piṇḍolo     valliyo tīriyo isi    
ajino ca meḷajino rādho   surādho gotamopi ca    
vasabhena ime honti       dasa therā mahiddhikāti .

<p align="center">Vaggo paṭhamo .</p>

theragāthāya dukanipātassa dutiyavaggo    
[268] [^290-1] sussūsā sutavaḍḍhanī         sutaṃ paññāya vaḍḍhanaṃ    
paññāya atthaṃ jānāti      ñāto attho sukhāvaho .

Sevetha pantāni senāsanāni    
careyya saṃyojanavippamokkhaṃ    
sace ratiṃ nādhigaccheyya tattha    
saṅghe vase rakkhitatto satīmāti .

Mahācundo thero .

[269] [^290-2] ye [1] kho te veghamissena [^290-2]  nānatthena ca kammunā  
manusse uparundhanti              pharusupakkamā janā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 291 -->
<a id="page-291"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 291]*

Tepi tatheva kīranti        na hi kammaṃ panassati .

Yaṃ karoti naro kammaṃ       kalyāṇaṃ yadi pāpakaṃ    
tassa tasseva dāyādo     yaṃ yaṃ kammaṃ pakubbatīti .

Jotidāso thero .

[270] [^291-3]  accayanti ahorattā       jīvitaṃ uparujjhati    
āyu khīyati maccānaṃ        kunnadīnaṃva odakaṃ .

Atha pāpāni kammāni       karaṃ bālo na bujjhati    
pacchāssa kaṭukaṃ hoti       vipāko hissa pāpakoti .

Heraññakāni thero .

[271] [^291-4]  parittaṃ dārumāruyha        yathā sīde mahaṇṇave    
evaṃ kusītamāgamma         sādhujīvīpi sīdati .

Tasmā taṃ parivajjeyya      kusītaṃ hīnavīriyaṃ    
vivittehi ariyehi         pahitattehi jhāyihi    
niccaṃ āraddhaviriyehi       paṇḍitehi sahāvaseti .

Somamitto thero .

[272] [^291-5]  jano janasmiṃ sambandho      janamevassito jano    
jano janena heṭhiyati       heṭheti ca jano janaṃ .

Ko hi tassa janenattho     janena janitena vā    
janaṃ ohāya gaccheyya [^291-1]   heṭhayitvā bahuṃ jananti .

Sabbamitto thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 292 -->
<a id="page-292"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 292]*

[273] [^292-6] kāḷī itthī brahatī dhaṅkarūpā    
satthiñca bhetvā aparañca satthiṃ    
bāhañca bhetvā aparañca bāhuṃ [^292-1]    
sīsañca bhetvā dadhithālakaṃva    
esā nisinnā abhisandahitvā [^292-2] .Ta    
yo ve avidvā upadhiṃ karoti    
punappunaṃ dukkhamupeti mando    
tasmā pajānaṃ upadhiṃ na kayirā    
māhaṃ puna bhinnasiro sayissanti .

Mahākālo thero .

[274] [^292-7] bahū sapatte labhati         muṇḍo saṅghāṭipāruto    
lābhī annassa pānassa      vatthassa sayanassa ca .

Etamādīnavaṃ ñatvā        sakkāresu mahabbhayaṃ    
appalābho anavassuto      sato bhikkhu paribbajeti .

Tisso thero .

[275] [^292-8]  pācīnavaṃsadāyamhi          sakyaputtā sahāyakā    
pahāyanappake bhoge       uñchāpattāgate ratā    
āraddhaviriyā pahitattā     niccaṃ daḷhaparakkamā    
ramanti dhammaratiyā         hitvāna lokiyaṃ ratinti .

Kimbilo thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 293 -->
<a id="page-293"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 293]*

[276] [^293-9] ayonisomanasīkārā        maṇḍanaṃ anuyuñjisaṃ    
uddhato capalo cāsiṃ       kāmarāgena aṭṭito .

Upāyakusalenāhaṃ          buddhenādiccabandhunā    
yoniso paṭipajjitvā       bhave cittaṃ udabbahinti .

Nando thero .

[277] 10 pare ca naṃ pasaṃsanti       attā ce asamāhito    
moghaṃ pare pasaṃsanti        attā hi asamāhito .

Pare ca naṃ garahanti        attā ce susamāhito    
moghaṃ pare garahanti        attā hi susamāhitoti .

Sirimā thero .

Uddānaṃ    
cundo ca jotidāso ca     thero heraññakāni yo [^293-1]    
somamitto sabbamitto      kālo tisso ca kimbilo    
nando ca sirimā ceva      dasa therā mahiddhikāti .

<p align="center">Vaggo dutiyo .</p>

theragāthāya dukanipātassa tatiyavaggo    
[278] [^293-1] khandhā mayā pariññātā     taṇhā me susamūhatā    
bhāvitā mama bojjhaṅgā     patto me āsavakkhayo .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 294 -->
<a id="page-294"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 294]*

Sohaṃ khandhe pariññāya      abbhahitvāna [^294-1] jāliniṃ    
bhāvayitvāna bojjhaṅge     nibbāyissaṃ anāsavoti .

Uttaro thero .

[279] [^294-2] panādo nāma so rājā    yassa yūpo suvaṇṇiyo [^294-2]    
tiriyaṃ soḷasubbedho [^294-3]     uccamāhu [^294-4] sahassadhā .

Sahassakaṇḍo satageṇḍu [^294-5]   dhajālu haritāmayo    
anaccuṃ tattha gandhabbā      chasahassāni sattadhāti .

Bhaddaji thero .

[280] [^294-3] satimā paññavā bhikkhu       āraddhabalavīriyo    
pañca kappasatānāhaṃ        ekarattiṃ anussariṃ .

Cattāro satipaṭṭhāne      satta aṭṭha ca bhāvayaṃ    
pañca kappasatānāhaṃ        ekarattiṃ anussarinti .

Sobhito thero .

[281] [^294-4] yaṃ kiccaṃ daḷhaviriyena       yaṃ kiccaṃ bodhumicchatā    
karissaṃ nāvarajjhissaṃ        passa viriyaṃ parakkamaṃ .

Tvañca me maggamakkhāhi     añjasaṃ amatogadhaṃ    
ahaṃ monena monissaṃ       gaṅgāsotova sāgaranti .

Valliyo thero .

[282] [^294-5] kese me olikhissanti     kappako upasaṅkami    
#[^294-1] ma. Yu. Abbahitvāna . [^294-2] po. Suvaṇṇamayo .  Ma. Yu. Suvaṇṇako .  [^294-3] sī.


<!-- Siam Rath Page 295 -->
<a id="page-295"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 295]*

Tato ādāsamādāya       sarīraṃ paccavekkhisaṃ .

Tuccho kāyo adissittha     andhakāre [^295-1] tamo byagā [^295-2]    
sabbe coḷā samucchinnā    natthidāni punabbhavoti .

Vītasoko thero .

[283] [^295-6] pañca nīvaraṇe hitvā       yogakkhemassa pattiyā    
dhammādāsaṃ gahetvāna      ñāṇadassanamattano    
paccavekkhiṃ imaṃ kāyaṃ       sabbaṃ santarabāhiraṃ    
ajjhattañca bahiddhā ca      tuccho kāyo adissathāti .

Puṇṇamāso thero .

[284] [^295-7] yathāpi bhaddo ājañño     khalitvā patitiṭṭhati    
bhiyyo laddhāna saṃvegaṃ      adīno vahate dhuraṃ    
evaṃ dassanasampannaṃ        sammāsambuddhasāvakaṃ    
ājāniyaṃ maṃ dhāretha       puttaṃ buddhassa orasanti .

Nandako thero .

[285] [^295-8] ehi nandaka gacchāma       upajjhāyassa santikaṃ    
sīhanādaṃ nadissāma         buddhaseṭṭhassa sammukhā .

Yāya no anukampāya       amhe pabbājayī muni    
so no attho anuppatto   sabbasaṃyojanakkhayoti .

Bhārato [^295-3] thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 296 -->
<a id="page-296"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 296]*

[286] [^296-9] nadanti evaṃ sappaññā      sīhāva girigabbhare    
dhīrā [^296-1] vijitasaṅgāmā     jetvā māraṃ savāhanaṃ [^296-2] .

Satthā ca pariciṇṇo me     dhammo saṅgho ca pūjito    
ahañca citto [^296-3] sumano    puttaṃ disvā anāsavanti .

Bhāradvājo thero .

[287] 10 upāsitā sappurisā       sutā dhammā abhiṇhaso    
sutvāna paṭipajjissaṃ        añjasaṃ amatogadhaṃ .

Bhavarāgahatassa me sato     bhavarāgo puna me na vijjati    
na cāhu na ca me bhavissati   na ca me etarahipi [^296-4] vijjatīti .

Kaṇhadinno thero .

Uddānaṃ    
uttaro bhaddaji thero      sobhito valliyo isi    
vītasoko ca so thero     puṇṇamāso ca nandako    
bhārato [^296-5] bhāradvājo ca  kaṇhadinno mahāmunīti .

<p align="center">Vaggo tatiyo .</p>

theragāthāya dukanipātassa catutthavaggo    
[288] [^296-1] yato ahaṃ pabbajitvā [^296-6]    sammāsambuddhasāsane    
vimuccamāno uggacchiṃ       kāmadhātuṃ upaccagaṃ .

#[^296-1] ma. Yu. Vīrā .   [^296-2] ma. Savāhaniṃ .   [^296-3] ma. Yu. Vitto .   [^296-4] ma. Pisaddo natthi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 297 -->
<a id="page-297"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 297]*

Brahmuno pekkhamānassa     tato cittaṃ vimucci me    
akuppā me vimuttīti       sabbasaṃyojanakkhayāti .

Migasiro thero .

[289] [^297-2] aniccāni gahakāni         tattha tattha punappunaṃ    
gahakāraṃ gavesanto        dukkhā jāti punappunaṃ .

Gahakāraka diṭṭhosi         puna gehaṃ na kāhasi    
sabbā te phāsukā bhaggā [^297-1]  thūṇirā ca padālitā    
vimariyādikataṃ cittaṃ         idheva vidhamissatīti .

Sivako thero .

[290] [^297-3] arahaṃ sugato loke        vātehābādhito muni    
sace uṇhodakaṃ atthi       munino dehi brāhmaṇa .

Pūjito pūjaneyyānaṃ        sakkareyyāna sakkato    
apacito apacineyyānaṃ [^297-1]   tassa icchāmi hātaveti .

Upavāṇo thero .

[291] [^297-4] diṭṭhā mayā dhammadharā upāsakā    
kāmā aniccā iti bhāsamānā    
sā rattarattā maṇikuṇḍalesu    
puttesu dāresu ca te apekkhā .

Addhāna jānanti yato [^297-2] ca dhammaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 298 -->
<a id="page-298"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 298]*

Kāmā aniccā iti cāpi āhu    
rāgañca tesaṃ na balatthi chettuṃ    
tasmā sitā puttadāraṃ dhanañcāti .

Isidinno thero .

[292] [^298-5] devo ca vassati devo ca gaḷagaḷāyati    
ekako cāhaṃ bherave bile viharāmi    
tassa mayhaṃ ekakassa bherave bile viharato    
natthi bhayaṃ vā chambhitattaṃ vā lomahaṃso vā .

Dhammatā mamesā yassa me   ekakassa bherave bile    
viharato natthi bhayaṃ vā      chambhitattaṃ vā lomahaṃso vāti .

Sambulakaccāno thero .

[293] [^298-6] kassa selūpamaṃ cittaṃ        ṭhitaṃ nānupakampati    
virattaṃ rajanīyesu          kuppanīye na kuppati .

Yassevaṃ bhāvitaṃ cittaṃ       kuto taṃ dukkhamessati .

Mama selūpamaṃ cittaṃ         ṭhitaṃ nānupakampati    
virattaṃ rajanīyesu          kuppanīye na kuppati .

Mamevaṃ bhāvitaṃ cittaṃ        kuto maṃ dukkhamessatīti .

Khitako [^298-1] thero .

[294] [^298-7] na tāva supituṃ hoti        ratti nakkhattamālinī    
paṭijaggitumevesā         ratti hoti vijānatā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 299 -->
<a id="page-299"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 299]*

Hatthikkhandhāvapatitaṃ         kuñjaro ce anukkame    
saṅgāme me mataṃ seyyo   yañce jīve parājitoti .

Soṇo poṭiriyaputto [^299-1] thero .

[295] [^299-8] pañca kāmaguṇe hitvā      piyarūpe manorame    
saddhāya gharā nikkhamma [^299-2]   dukkhassantakaro bhave .

Nābhinandāmi maraṇaṃ         nābhinandāmi jīvitaṃ    
kālañca paṭikaṅkhāmi        sampajāno patissatoti .

Nisabho thero .

[296] [^299-9] ambapallavasaṅkāsaṃ         aṃse katvāna cīvaraṃ    
nisinno hatthigīvāyaṃ        gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ .

Hatthikkhandhato oruyha      saṃvegaṃ  alabhiṃ tadā    
sohaṃ ditto tadā santo    patto me āsavakkhayoti .

Usabho thero .

[297] 10 ayamiti kappaṭo kappaṭakuro    
acchāya accaṃbharāya [^299-3]    
amataghaṭikāyaṃ dhammakaṭapatto [^299-4]    
kaṭapadaṃ jhānāni ocetuṃ .

Mā kho tvaṃ kappaṭa pacālesi    
mā taṃ [^299-5] upakaṇṇakamhi [^299-6] tāḷessaṃ    
#[^299-1] po. Giriputto .  [^299-2] yu. Saddhāya abhinikkhamma .   [^299-3] ma. Yu. Atibharitāya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 300 -->
<a id="page-300"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 300]*

Na hi tvaṃ kappaṭa mattamaññāsi    
saṅghamajjhamhi pacalāyamānoti .

Kappaṭakuro thero .

Uddānaṃ    
migasiro sivako ca         upavāṇo ca paṇḍito    
isidinno ca kaccāno      khitako ca mahāvasī    
poṭiriyaputto nisabho       usabho kappaṭakuroti .

<p align="center">Vaggo catuttho .</p>

theragāthāya dukanipātassa pañcamavaggo    
[298] [^300-1] aho buddhā aho dhammā    aho no satthu sampadā    
yattha etādisaṃ dhammaṃ       sāvako sacchikāhiti .

Asaṅkheyyesu kappesu      sakkāyādhigatā ahuṃ    
tesamayaṃ pacchimako         carimoyaṃ samussayo    
jātimaraṇasaṃsāro          natthidāni punabbhavoti .

Kumārakassapo thero .

[299] [^300-2] yo have daharo bhikkhu      yuñjati buddhasāsane    
jāgaro [^300-1] sa hi suttesu   amoghantassa jīvitaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 301 -->
<a id="page-301"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 301]*

Tasmā saddhañca sīlañca      pasādaṃ dhammadassanaṃ    
anuyuñjetha medhāvī        saraṃ buddhāna sāsananti .

Dhammapālo thero .

[300] [^301-3] kassindriyāni [^301-1] samathaṃ gatāni    
assā yathā sārakinā sudantā    
pahīnamānassa anāsavassa    
devāpi tassa pihayanti tādino .

Mayhindriyāni samathaṃ gatāni    
assā yathā sārathinā sudantā    
pahīnamānassa anāsavassa    
devāpi mayhaṃ pihayanti tādinoti .

Brahmāli thero  .

[301] [^301-4] chavipāpaka cittabhaddaka       mogharāja satataṃ samāhito    
hemantikasītakālarattiyo     bhikkhu tvaṃsi kathaṃ karissasi .

Sampannasassā magadhā       kevalā iti me sutaṃ    
palālacchannako seyyaṃ      yathaññe sukhajīvinoti .

Mogharājā thero .

[302] [^301-5] na ukkhipe no ca parikkhipe pare  
na okkhipe pāragataṃ na eraye  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 302 -->
<a id="page-302"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 302]*

Na cattavaṇṇaṃ parisāsu byāhare    
anuddhato sammitabhāṇi subbato .

Susukhumanipuṇatthadassinā       matikusalena nivātavuttinā    
saṃsevitabuddhasīlinā         nibbānaṃ na hi tena dullabhanti .

Visākho pañcālīputto thero .

[303] [^302-6] nadanti morā susikhā supekhuṇā    
sunīlagīvā sumukhā sugajjino    
susaddalā cāpi mahāmahī ayaṃ    
subyāpitambu suvalāhakaṃ nabhaṃ .

Sukallarūpo sumanassa jhāyitaṃ    
sunikkamo [^302-1] sādhu subuddhasāsane    
susukkasukkaṃ nipuṇaṃ sududdasaṃ    
phusāhi taṃ uttamamaccutaṃ padanti .

Cūḷako thero .

[304] [^302-7] nandamānāgataṃ cittaṃ        sūlamāthopamānakaṃ    
tena teneva vajasi        yena sūlaṃ kaliṅgaraṃ .

Tāhaṃ citta kaliṃ brūmi       taṃ brūmi cittadubbhakaṃ    
satthā te dullabho laddho   mānatthe maṃ niyojayīti .

Anūpamo thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 303 -->
<a id="page-303"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 303]*

[305] [^303-8] saṃsaraṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ          gatīsu parivattisaṃ    
apassaṃ ariyasaccāni        andhabhūto puthujjano .

Tassa me appamattassa      saṃsārā vinaḷīkatā    
sabbā gatī samucchinnā      natthidāni punabbhavoti .

Vajjito thero .

[306] [^303-9] assatthe haritobhāse      saṃvirūḷhamhi pādape    
ekaṃ buddhagataṃ saññaṃ        alabhitthaṃ patissato .

Ekattiṃse ito kappe     yaṃ saññaṃ alabhiṃ tadā    
tassā saññāya vāhasā     patto me āsavakkhayoti .

Sandhito thero .

Uddānaṃ    
kumārakassapo thero       dhammapālo ca brahmāli    
mogharājā visākho ca      cūḷako ca anūpamo    
vajjito sandhito thero     kilesarajavāhanoti .

<p align="center">Vaggo pañcamo .</p>

Tathā dukanipātamhi         navuti ceva aṭṭha ca    
therā ekūnapaññāsaṃ       bhāsitā nayakovidā .

Dukanipāto niṭṭhito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 304 -->
<a id="page-304"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 304]*

Theragāthāya tikanipāto    
[307] [^304-1] ayonisuddhimanvesaṃ         aggiṃ paricariṃ vane    
suddhimaggaṃ ajānanto       akāsiṃ aparaṃ [^304-1] tapaṃ .

Taṃ sukhena sukhaṃ laddhaṃ        passa dhammasudhammataṃ    
tisso vijjā anuppattā    kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Brahmabandhu pure āsiṃ      idāni khomhi brāhmaṇo    
tevijjo nhātako camhi    sotthiyo camhi vedagūti .

Aṅgaṇikabhāradvājo thero .

[308] [^304-2] pañcāhāhaṃ pabbajito       sekkho [^304-2] appattamānaso    
vihāraṃ me paviṭṭhassa       cetaso paṇidhī ahū    
nāsissaṃ na pivissāmi       vihārato na nikkhame    
na pi passaṃ nipātessaṃ      taṇhāsalle anūhate .

Tassa mevaṃ viharato        passa viriyaparakkamaṃ    
tisso vijjā anuppattā    kataṃ buddhassa sāsananti .

Paccayo thero .

[309] [^304-3] yo pubbe karaṇīyāni       pacchā so kātumicchati    
sukhā so dhaṃsate ṭhānā     pacchā camanutappati .

Yañhi kayirā tañhi vade     yaṃ na kayirā na taṃ vade  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 305 -->
<a id="page-305"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 305]*

Akarontaṃ bhāsamānaṃ        parijānanti paṇḍitā .

Susukhaṃ vata nibbānaṃ         sammāsambuddhadesitaṃ    
asokaṃ virajaṃ khemaṃ         yattha dukkhaṃ nirujjhatīti .

Bākulo thero .Ṭa    
[310] [^305-4] sukhañca jīvituṃ icche        sāmaññasmiṃ apekkhavā    
saṅghikaṃ nātimaññeyya       cīvaraṃ pānabhojanaṃ .

Sukhañce jīvituṃ icche       sāmaññasmiṃ apekkhavā    
ahi musikasobbhaṃva          sevetha sayanāsanaṃ .

Sukhañce jīvituṃ icche       sāmaññasmiṃ apekkhavā    
itarītarena tusseyya       ekadhammañca bhāvayeti .

Dhaniyo thero .

[311] [^305-5] atisītaṃ atiuṇhaṃ           atisāyamidaṃ ahu    
iti visaṭṭhakammante        khaṇā accenti māṇave .

Yo ca sītañca uṇhañca      tiṇā bhiyyo na maññati    
karaṃ purisakiccāni          so sukhā na vihāyati .

Dabbaṃ kusaṃ poṭakilaṃ         usīraṃ muñjapabbajaṃ    
urasā panudahissāmi [^305-1]     vivekamanubrūhayanti .

Mātaṅgaputto thero .

[312] [^305-6] ye cittakathī bahussutā      samaṇā pātaliputtavāsino    
tesaññataroyamāyuvā       dvāre tiṭṭhati khujjasobhito .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 306 -->
<a id="page-306"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 306]*

Ye cittakathī bahussutā      samaṇā pātaliputtavāsino    
tesaññataroyamāyuvā       dvāre tiṭṭhati māluterito .

Suyuddhena suyiṭṭhena        saṅgāmavijayena ca    
brahmacariyānuciṇṇena       evāyaṃ sukhamedhatīti .

Khujjasobhito thero .K    
[313] [^306-7] yodha koci manussesu       parapāṇāni hiṃsati    
asmā lokā paramhā ca    ubhayā dhaṃsate naro .

Yo ca mettena cittena    sabbapāṇānukampati    
bahuṃ hi so pasavati         puññaṃ tādisako naro .

Subhāsitassa sikkhetha        samaṇupāsanassa ca    
ekāsanassa ca raho       cittavūpasamassa cāti .

Vāraṇo thero .

[314] [^306-8] ekopi saddho medhāvī     assaddhānaṃ ca [^306-1] ñātinaṃ    
dhammaṭṭho sīlasampanno      hoti atthāya bandhunaṃ .

Niggayha anukampāya        coditā ñātayo mayā    
ñātibandhavapemena         kāraṃ katvāna bhikkhusu .

Te abbhatītā kālakatā     pattā te tidivaṃ sukhaṃ    
bhātaro mayhaṃ mātā ca     modanti kāmakāminoti .

Passiko thero .

[315] [^306-9] kālāpabbaṅgasaṅkāso      kīso [^306-2] dhamanisaṇṭhito  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 307 -->
<a id="page-307"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 307]*

Mattaññū annapānasmiṃ       alīnamanaso [^307-1] naro .

Phuṭṭho ḍaṃsehi makasehi      araññasmiṃ brahāvane    
nāgo saṅgāmasīseva       sato tatrādhivāsaye .

Yathā brahmā tathā eko   yathā devo tathā duve    
yathā gāmo tathā tayo     kolāhalaṃ tatuttarinti .

Yasojo thero .

[316] 10 ahu tuyhaṃ pure saddhā     sā te ajja na vijjati    
yaṃ tuyhaṃ tuyhamevetaṃ       natthi duccaritaṃ mama .

Aniccā hi calā saddhā     evaṃ diṭṭhā hi sā mayā    
rajjantipi virajjanti        tattha kiṃ jiyyate muni .

Paccati munino bhattaṃ        thokaṃ thokaṃ kule kule    
piṇḍikāya carissāmi        atthi jaṅghabalaṃ mamāti .

Sāṭimattiyo thero .(    
[317] 1[^307-1] saddhāya abhinikkhamma       navapabbajito navo    
mitte bhajeyya kalyāṇe    suddhājīve atandite .

Saddhāya abhinikkhamma        navapabbajito navo    
saṅghasmiṃ viharaṃ bhikkhu        sikkhetha vinayaṃ budho .

Saddhāya abhinikkhamma        navapabbajito navo    
kappākappesu kusalo       careyya apurakkhatoti .

Upāli thero .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 308 -->
<a id="page-308"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 308]*

[318] 1[^308-2] paṇḍitaṃ vata maṃ santaṃ       alamatthavicintakaṃ    
pañca kāmaguṇā loke      sammohā pātayiṃsu maṃ .

Pakkhanno māravisaye       daḷhasallasamappito    
asakkhiṃ maccurājassa        ahaṃ pāsā pamuccituṃ .

Sabbe kāmā pahīnā me    bhavā sabbe padālitā    
vikkhīṇo jātisaṃsāro       natthidāni punabbhavoti .

Uttarapālo thero .

[319] 1[^308-3] suṇātha ñātayo sabbe     yāvantettha samāgatā    
dhammaṃ vo desayissāmi      dukkhā jāti punappunaṃ .

Ārabhatha [^308-1] nikkhamatha       yuñjatha buddhasāsane    
dhunātha maccuno senaṃ       naḷāgāraṃva kuñjaro .

Yo imasmiṃ dhammavinaye      appamatto vihessati    
pahāya jātisaṃsāraṃ         dukkhassantaṃ karissatīti .

Abhibhūto thero .

[320] 1[^308-4] saṃsaraṃ hi nirayaṃ agacchisaṃ    
petalokamagamaṃ punappunaṃ    
dukkhamamhipi tiracchānayoniyā    
nekadhā hi vusitaṃ ciraṃ mayā .

Mānusopi ca bhavobhirādhito  
saggakāyamagamaṃ sakiṃ sakiṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 309 -->
<a id="page-309"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 309]*

Rūpadhātūsu arūpadhātūsu    
nevasaññīsu asaññīsuṭṭhitaṃ .

Sambhavā suviditā asārakā    
saṅkhatā pacalitā saderitā    
taṃ viditvā mahamattasambhavaṃ    
santimeva satimā samajjhaganti .

Gotamo thero .

[321] 1[^309-5] yo pubbe karaṇīyāni      pacchā so kātumicchati    
sukhā so dhaṃsate ṭhānā     pacchā camanutappati .

Yañhi kayirā tañhi vade     yaṃ na kayirā na taṃ vade    
akarontaṃ bhāsamānaṃ        parijānanti paṇḍitā .

Susukhaṃ vata nibbānaṃ         sammāsambuddhadesitaṃ    
asokaṃ virajaṃ khemaṃ         yattha dukkhaṃ nirujjhatīti .

Hārito  thero .

[322] 1[^309-6] pāpamitte vivajjetvā    bhajeyyuttamapuggale [^309-1]    
ovāde cassa tiṭṭheyya    patthento acalaṃ sukhaṃ .
