pītisukhena ca kāyaṃ          pharitvā vihariṃ tadā    
sattamiyā pāde pasāresiṃ [^465-1]   tamokkhandhaṃ padāliya .

Vijayā .

Chakkanipāto samatto .

therīgāthāya sattakanipāto    
[459] [^465-1] musalāni gahetvāna         dhaññaṃ koṭṭenti māṇavā    
puttadārāni posentā      dhanaṃ vindanti māṇavā .

Ghaṭetha buddhasāsane         yaṃ katvā nānutappati    
khippaṃ pādāni dhovitvā      ekamantaṃ nisīdatha .

Cittaṃ upaṭṭhapetvāna        ekaggaṃ susamāhitaṃ    
paccavekkhatha saṅkhāre       parato no ca attato .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        paṭācārānusāsaniṃ    
pāde pakkhālayitvāna       ekamante upāvisiṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 466 -->
<a id="page-466"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 466]*

Rattiyā purime yāme       pubbajātimanussariṃ    
rattiyā majjhime yāme      dibbacakkhuṃ visodhayiṃ .

Rattiyā pacchime yāme      tamokkhandhaṃ padālayiṃ    
tevijjā atha vuṭṭhāmi [^466-1]    katā te anusāsanī .

Sakkaṃva devā tidasā        saṅgāme aparājitaṃ    
purakkhitvā viharāmi [^466-2]      tevijjamhi anāsavā .

Uttarā .

[460] [^466-2] satiṃ upaṭṭhapetvāna         bhikkhunī bhāvitindriyā    
paṭivijjhiṃ padaṃ santaṃ          saṅkhārūpasamaṃ sukhaṃ .

Kiṃ [^466-3] nu uddissa muṇḍāsi    samaṇī viya dissasi    
na ca rocesi pāsaṇḍe      kimidaṃ carasi momuhā .

Ito bahiddhā pāsaṇḍā      diṭṭhiyo upanissitā    
na te dhammaṃ vijānanti       na te dhammassa kovidā .

Atthi sakyakule jāto       buddho appaṭipuggalo    
so me dhammamadesesi       diṭṭhīnaṃ samatikkamaṃ    
dukkhaṃ dukkhasamuppādaṃ         dukkhassa ca atikkamaṃ    
ariyaṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ          dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        vihariṃ sāsane ratā    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 467 -->
<a id="page-467"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 467]*

Sabbattha vihatā nandi        tamokkhandho padālito    
evaṃ jānāhi pāpima        nihato tvamasi antaka .

Cālā .

[461] [^467-3] satīmatī cakkhumatī            bhikkhunī bhāvitindriyā    
paṭivijjhiṃ padaṃ santaṃ          akāpurisasevitaṃ .

Kiṃ nu jātiṃ na rocesi       jāto kāmāni bhuñjati    
bhuñjāhi kāmaratiyo         māhu pacchānutāpinī .Ū    
jātassa maraṇaṃ hoti         hatthapādāna chedanaṃ    
vadhabandhaparikkhepaṃ [^467-1]        jāto dukkhaṃ nigacchati .

Atthi sakyakule jāto       sambuddho aparājito    
so me dhammamadesesi       jātiyā samatikkamaṃ    
dukkhaṃ dukkhasamuppādaṃ         dukkhassa ca atikkamaṃ    
ariyaṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ          dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        vihariṃ sāsane ratā    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Sabbattha vihatā nandi        tamokkhandho padālito    
evaṃ jānāhi pāpima        nihato tvamasi antaka .

Upacālā .

Sattakanipāto samatto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 468 -->
<a id="page-468"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 468]*

Therīgāthāya aṭṭhakanipāto    
[462] [^468-1] bhikkhunī sīlasampannā         indriyesu susaṃvutā    
adhigacche padaṃ santaṃ         asecanakamojjavaṃ [^468-1] .

Tāvatiṃsā ca yāmā ca       tusitā cāpi devatā    
nimmānaratino devā        ye devā vasavattino    
tattha cittaṃ paṇidhehi         yattha te vusitaṃ pure .

Tāvatiṃsā ca yāmā ca       tusitā cāpi devatā    
nimmānaratino devā        ye devā vasavattino    
kālaṃ kālaṃ bhavā bhavaṃ        sakkāyasmiṃ purakkhatā    
avītivattā sakkāyaṃ         jātimaraṇasārino .

Sabbo ādīpito loko      sabbo loko paridīpito [^468-2]    
sabbo pajjalito loko      sabbo loko pakampito .

Akampitaṃ atuliyaṃ            aputhujjanasevitaṃ    
buddho dhammaṃ me desesi     tattha me nirato mano .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        vihariṃ sāsane ratā    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Sabbattha vihatā nandi        tamokkhandho padālito    
evaṃ jānāhi pāpima        nihato tvamasi antaka .

Sīsūpacālā .

Aṭṭhakanipāto samatto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 469 -->
<a id="page-469"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 469]*

[463] [^469-1] mā su te vaḍḍha lokamhi     vanatho ahu kudācanaṃ    
mā puttaka punappunaṃ         ahu dukkhassa bhāgimā .

Sukhaṃ hi vaḍḍha munayo         anejā chinnasaṃsayā    
sītibhūtā damappattā         viharanti anāsavā .

Tehānuciṇṇaṃ isībhi          maggaṃ dassanapattiyā    
dukkhassantakiriyāya          tvaṃ vaḍḍha anubrūhaya .

Visāradāva bhaṇasi           etamatthaṃ janetti me    
maññāmi nūna māmike        vanatho te na vijjati .

Ye keci vaḍḍha saṅkhārā     hīnaükkaṭṭhamajjhimā    
aṇupi aṇumattopi           vanatho me na vijjati .

Sabbe me āsavā khīṇā     appamattassa jhāyato    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Uḷāraṃ vata me mātā       patodaṃ samavassari    
paramatthasañhitā gāthā       yathāpi anukampikā .

Tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ sutvā        anusiṭṭhiṃ janettiyā    
dhammasaṃvegamāpādiṃ          yogakkhemassa pattiyā .

Sohaṃ padhānapahitatto        rattindivamatandito    
mātarā codito santo      aphusiṃ santimuttamaṃ .

Vaḍḍhamātā .

Navakanipāto samatto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 470 -->
<a id="page-470"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 470]*

Therīgāthāya ekādasakanipāto    
[464] [^470-1] kalyāṇamittatā muninā       lokaṃ ādissa vaṇṇitā    
kalyāṇamitte bhajamāno      api bālo paṇḍito assa .

Bhajitabbā sappurisā         paññā tathāpi [^470-1] vaḍḍhati    
bhajantānaṃ bhajamāno         yo [^470-2] koci khattiyādiko    
sappurise sabbehipi         dukkhehi ceva mocaye    
dukkhañceva vijāneyya       dukkhassa ca samudayaṃ    
nirodhaṭṭhaṅgikamaggaṃ          cattāri ariyasaccāni ca [^470-3] .

Dukkho itthibhāvo akkhāto   purisadammasārathinā .

Sapattikampi hi dukkhaṃ         appekaccā sakiṃ vijātāyo    
galake [^470-4] apakantanti [^470-5]    sukhumāliniyo visāni khādanti    
janamārakamajjhagatā          ubhopi byasanāni anubhonti    
upavijaññā gacchantī         addasāhaṃ patiṃ mataṃ    
panthe vijāyitvāna         appattāhaṃ sakaṃ gehaṃ .

Dve puttā kālakatā       pati ca panthe mato kapaṇikāya    
mātā pitā ca bhātā ca     ḍayhanti ekacitakāyaṃ .

Khīṇakulamhi [^470-6] kapaṇe        anubhūtaṃ te dukkhaṃ aparimāṇaṃ    
assu ca te pavattaṃ hi [^470-7]    bahūni jātisahassāni .

Passiṃ taṃ susānamajjhe [^470-8]     athopi khāditāni puttamaṃsāni  
#[^470-1] yu. Tathā pavaḍḍhati . [^470-2] ma. Ayaṃ gāthāya pādo na dissati . [^470-3] ma. Yu. Casaddo  
#natthi . [^470-4] yu. Gale . [^470-5] ma. Api kantanti . [^470-6] khīṇakulīne . [^470-7] ma. Yu. Hisaddo  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 471 -->
<a id="page-471"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 471]*

Hatakulikā sabbagarahitā       matapatikā amataṃ adhigacchiṃ .

Bhāvito me ayaṃ maggo      ariyoṭṭhaṅgiko amatagāmī    
nibbānaṃpi sacchikataṃ          dhammādāsaṃ avekkhihaṃ [^471-1] .

Ahamhi kantasallā          ohitabhārā kataṃ [^471-2] me karaṇīyaṃ    
kīsāgotamī therī           suvimuttacittā imaṃ bha6ṇīti .

Kīsāgotamī .

Ekādasakanipāto samatto .

therīgāthāya dvādasakanipāto    
[465] [^471-1] ubho mātā ca dhītā ca      mayaṃ āsuṃ sapattiyo    
tassā me ahu saṃvego      abbhūto [^471-3] lomahaṃsano .

Dhiratthu kāmā asucī         duggandhā bahukaṇṭakā    
yattha mātā ca dhītā ca      sabhariyā mayaṃ ahuṃ .

Kāmesvādīnavaṃ disvā       nekkhammaṃ daṭṭhu khemato    
sā pabbajiṃ rājagahe        agārasmā anagāriyaṃ .

Pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi        dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
cetopariyañāṇañca [^471-4]       sotadhātu visodhitā .

Iddhipi me sacchikatā        patto me āsavakkhayo    
cha me [^471-5] abhiññā sacchikatā  kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

#[^471-1] yu. Apekkhihaṃ . [^471-2] ma. Kataṃ hi . [^471-3] ma. Yu. Abbhuto . [^471-4] ma. Ceto pariccañāṇañca .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 472 -->
<a id="page-472"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 472]*

Iddhiyā abhinimmitvā        caturassaṃ rathaṃ ahaṃ    
buddhassa pāde vanditvā     lokanāthassa sirīmato [^472-1] .

Supupphitaggaṃ upagamma pādapaṃ    
ekā tuvaṃ tiṭṭhasi rukkhamūle    
na cāpi te dutiyo atthi koci    
na tvaṃ bāle bhāyasi dhuttakānaṃ .

Sataṃ sahassānaṃpi [^472-2] dhuttakānaṃ    
samāgatā edisakā bhaveyyuṃ .

Lomaṃ na iñje napi sampavedhe    
kiṃ me tuvaṃ māra karissaseko .

Esā antaradhāyāmi        kucchiṃ vā pavisāmi te    
bhamukantarepi tiṭṭhāmi        tiṭṭhantiṃ maṃ na dakkhasi .

Cittamhi vasībhūtāhaṃ          iddhipādā subhāvitā    
cha mebhiññā [^472-3] sacchikatā    kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Sattisūlūpamā kāmā         khandhāsaṃ adhikuṭṭanā    
yaṃ tvaṃ kāmaratiṃ brūsi        arati dāni sā mama .

Sabbattha vihatā nandi        tamokkhandho padālito    
evaṃ jānāhi pāpima        nihato tvamasi antakāti .

Uppalavaṇṇā .

Dvādasakanipāto samatto .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 473 -->
<a id="page-473"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 473]*

Therīgāthāya soḷasakanipāto    
[466] [^473-1] udakahārī [^473-1] ahaṃ sīte      sadā udakamotariṃ    
ayyānaṃ daṇḍabhayabhītā        vācādosabhayaṭṭitā .

Kassa brāhmaṇa tvaṃ bhīto     sadā udakamotari    
vedhamānehi gattehi        sītaṃ vedayase bhusaṃ .

Jānantī ca tuvaṃ bhoti        puṇṇike paripucchasi    
karontaṃ kusalaṃ kammaṃ         rundhantaṃ kammapāpakaṃ .

Yo ca vuḍḍho [2] daharo vā   pāpakammaṃ pakubbati    
udakābhisecanā [^473-3] sopi     pāpakammā pamuccati .

Ko nu te idamakkhāsi       ajānantassa ajānako    
udakābhisecanā [^473-3] nāma     pāpakammā pamuccati    
saggaṃ nūna gamissanti         sabbe maṇḍūkakacchapā    
nāgā ca sasasumārā ca       ye caññe udake carā .

Orabbhikā sūkarikā         macchikā migabandhakā    
corā ca vajjhaghātā ca      ye caññe pāpakammino    
udakābhisecanā [^473-3] tepi     pāpakammā pamuccare .

Sace imā nadiyo te       pāpaṃ pubbe kataṃ vaheyyuṃ 4  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 474 -->
<a id="page-474"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 474]*

Puññaṃpimā vaheyyuṃ te [^474-1]    tena tvaṃ paribāhiro assa [^474-2] .

Yassa brāhmaṇa tvaṃ bhīto     sadā udakamotari    
tameva brahme mākāsi      mā te sītaṃ chaviṃ hane .

Kumaggapaṭipannaṃ maṃ           ariyamaggaṃ samānayi    
udakābhisecanaṃ [^474-3] bhoti      imaṃ sāṭakaṃ dadāmi te .

Tuyheva sāṭako hotu       nāhamicchāmi sāṭakaṃ .

Sace bhāyasi dukkhassa        sace te dukkhamappiyaṃ    
mākāsi pāpakaṃ kammaṃ        āvi vā yadi vā raho .

Sace ca pāpakaṃ kammaṃ        karissasi karosi vā    
na te dukkhā pamutyatthi      upeccāpi palāyato .

Sace bhāyasi dukkhassa        sace te dukkhamappiyaṃ    
upehi buddhaṃ saraṇaṃ          dhammaṃ saṅghañca tādinaṃ    
samādiyāhi sīlāni          tante mokkhāya [^474-4] hessati .

Brahmabandhu pure āsiṃ       ajjamhi sacca brāhmaṇo    
tevijjo vedasampanno      sotthiyo camhi nhātako .

Puṇṇikā [^474-5] .

Soḷasakanipāto samatto .

#[^474-1] yu. Ayaṃ pāṭho natthi . [^474-2] ma. Ayaṃ pāṭho natthi . [^474-3] ma. Dakābhisecanā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 475 -->
<a id="page-475"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 475]*

Therīgāthāya vīsatinipāto    
[467] [^475-1] kāḷakā bhamaravaṇṇasadisā    
vellitaggā mama muddhajā ahuṃ    
te jarāya sāṇavākasadisā    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Vāsitova surabhikaraṇḍako    
pupphapūraṃ mama uttamaṅgabhūto [^475-1]    
taṃ jarāyatha salomagandhikaṃ    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Kānanaṃva sahitaṃ suropitaṃ    
kocchasūcivicittaggasobhitaṃ    
taṃ jarāya viraḷaṃ tahiṃ tahiṃ    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Saṇhagandhakasuvaṇṇamaṇḍitaṃ [^475-2]    
sobhate su veṇīhi alaṅkataṃ    
taṃ jarāya khalitaṃ siraṃ kataṃ    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Cittakārasukatāva lekhitā  
sobhate [^475-3] su bhamukā pure mama  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 476 -->
<a id="page-476"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 476]*

Tā jarāya valīhi palambitā    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Bhassarā surucirā yathā maṇi    
nettāhesuṃ abhinīlamāyatā    
te jarāyabhihatā na sobhate [^476-1]    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Saṇhatuṅgasadisī ca nāsikā    
sobhate su abhiyobbanaṃ pati    
sā jarāya upakūlitā viya    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Kaṅkaṇaṃva sukataṃ suniṭṭhitaṃ    
sobhate [^476-1] su mama kaṇṇapāḷiyo pure [^476-2]    
tā jarāya valīhi palambitā    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Pattalimakulavaṇṇasadisā    
sobhate su dantā pure mama    
te jarāya khaṇḍā yavapītakā    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Kānanasmiṃ vanasaṇḍacāriṇī  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 477 -->
<a id="page-477"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 477]*

Kokilāva madhuraṃ nikūjitaṃ [^477-1]    
taṃ jarāya khalitaṃ tahiṃ tahiṃ    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Saṇṭhakammudīva [^477-2] suppamajjitā    
sobhate su gīvā pure mama    
sā jarāya bhaggā vināmitā [^477-3]    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Vaṭṭapalighasadisopamā ubho    
sobhate su bāhā pure mama    
tā jarāya yathā pāṭalippalitā [^477-4]    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Saṇhamuddikāsuvaṇṇamaṇḍitā    
sobhate su hatthā pure mama    
te jarāya yathā mūlamūlikā    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Pīnavaṭṭasahituggatā ubhopi [^477-5]    
sobhate su thanakā pure mama    
therīti va [^477-6] lambantenodakā    
#[^477-1] ma. Nikūjihaṃ . [^477-2] ma. Saṇhakamburiva . [^477-3] yu. Vināsitā . [^477-4] ma. Pāṭalibbalitā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 478 -->
<a id="page-478"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 478]*

Saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Kañcanassa phalakaṃva sumaṭṭhaṃ [^478-1]    
sobhate su kāyo pure mama    
so valīhi sukhumāhi otato    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Nāgabhogasadisopamā ubho    
sobhate su ūrū pure mama    
tā [^478-2] jarāya yathā veḷunāḷiyo    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Saṇhanūpurasuvaṇṇamaṇḍitā    
sobhate su jaṅghā pure mama    
tā jarāya tiladaṇḍakāriva    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Tūlapuṇṇasadisopamā ubho    
sobhate su pādā pure mama    
te jarāya phuṭikā valīmatā    
saccavādivacanaṃ anaññathā .

Ediso ahu ayaṃ samussayo  
jajjaro bahudukkhānamālayo  
sopalepapatito jarāgharo  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 479 -->
<a id="page-479"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 479]*

Saccavādivacanaṃ anaññātha .

Ambapālī .

[468] [^479-2] samaṇāti bhoti maṃ [^479-1] vipassi   samaṇāti paṭibujjhasi    
samaṇānameva kittesi        samaṇī nūna bhavissasi .

Vipulaṃ annañca pānañca       samaṇānaṃ pavecchasi    
rohiṇi dāni pucchāmi        kena te samaṇā piyā .

Akammakāmā alasā         paradattūpajīvino [^479-2]    
āsaṃsukā sādhukāmā        kena te samaṇā piyā .

Cirassaṃ vata maṃ tāta         samaṇānaṃ paripucchasi    
tesante kittayissāmi       paññāsīlaparakkamaṃ .

Kammakāmā analasā         kammaseṭṭhassa kārakā    
rāgadosaṃ pajahanti          tena me samaṇā piyā .

Tīṇi pāpassa mūlāni         dhunanti sucikārino    
sabbapāpaṃ pahīnesaṃ          tena me samaṇā piyā .

Kāyakammaṃ suci nesaṃ         vacīkammañca tādisaṃ    
manokammaṃ suci nesaṃ         tena me samaṇā piyā .

Vimalā saṅkhamuttāva         suddhā santarabāhirā    
puṇṇā sukkehi dhammehi [^479-3]   tena me samaṇā piyā .

Bahussutā dhammadharā         ariyā dhammajīvino    
atthaṃ dhammañca desenti      tena me samaṇā piyā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 480 -->
<a id="page-480"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 480]*

Bahussutā dhammadharā         ariyā dhammajīvino    
ekaggacittā satimanto      tena me samaṇā piyā .

Dūraṅgamā satimanto         mantabhāṇī anuddhatā่    
dukkhassantaṃ pajānanti        tena me samaṇā piyā .

Yamhā gāmā pakkamanti      na vilokenti kiñcanaṃ    
anapekkhāva gacchanti        tena me samaṇā piyā .

Na te saṃ koṭṭhe osenti [^480-1]   na kumbhiṃ na kaḷopiyaṃ    
pariniṭṭhitamesānā          tena me samaṇā piyā .

Na te hiraññaṃ gaṇhanti       na suvaṇṇaṃ na rūpiyaṃ    
paccuppannena yāpenti      tena me samaṇā piyā .

Nānākulā pabbajitā        nānājanapadehi ca    
aññamaññaṃ piyāyanti         tena me samaṇā piyā .

Atthāya vata no bhoti       kule jātāsi rohiṇi 2-    
saddhā buddhe ca dhamme ca    saṅghe ca tibbagāravā .

Tuvaṃ hetaṃ pajānāsi         puññakkhettaṃ anuttaraṃ    
amhaṃpi ete samaṇā        paṭiggaṇhanti dakkhiṇaṃ    
patiṭṭhito hettha yañño      vipulo no bhavissati .

Sace bhāyasi dukkhassa        sace te dukkhamappiyaṃ    
upehi buddhaṃ [^480-3] saraṇaṃ       dhammaṃ saṅghañca tādinaṃ    
samādiyāhi sīlāni          tante atthāya hehiti .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 481 -->
<a id="page-481"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 481]*

Upemi buddhaṃ [^481-1] saraṇaṃ       dhammaṃ saṅghañca tādinaṃ    
samādiyāmi sīlāni          taṃ me atthāya hehiti .

Brahmabandhu pure āsiṃ       so idānimhi brāhmaṇo    
tevijjo sotthiyo camhi     vedagū camhi nhātako .

Rohiṇī .

[469] [^481-3] laṭṭhihattho pure āsiṃ       so dāni migaluddako    
āsāya palipā ghorā       nāsakkhiṃ pārametase [^481-2] .

Sumattaṃ maṃ maññamānā        cāpā puttamatosayi    
cāpāya bandhanaṃ chetvā      pabbajissaṃ pure [^481-3] ahaṃ .

Mā me kujjha mahāvīra       mā me kujjha mahāmuni    
na hi kodhaparetassa         suddhi atthi kuto tapo .

Pakkamissañca nālāto       kodha nālāya vacchati    
bandhantī itthirūpena         samaṇe dhammajīvino .

Ehi kāḷa nivattassu        bhuñja kāme yathā pure    
ahaṃ ca te vasīkatā         ye ca me santi ñātakā .

Etto ceva catubbhāgaṃ      yathā bhāsasi taṃ cāpe [^481-4]    
tayi rattassa posassa        uḷāraṃ vata taṃ siyā .

Kāḷaṅginiṃva takkāriṃ         pupphitaṃ girimuddhani  
phullaṃ dālimalaṭṭhiṃva          antodīpeva pāṭaliṃ  
haricandanalittaṅgiṃ           kāsikuttamadhāriniṃ  
#[^481-1] ma. Upemi saraṇaṃ buddhaṃ .  [^481-2] ma. Pārametave .  [^481-3] ma. Yu. Puno mahaṃ .  [^481-4] ma. Etto  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 482 -->
<a id="page-482"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 482]*

Taṃ maṃ rūpavatiṃ santiṃ          kassa ohāya gacchasi .

Sākuṇikova sakuṇaṃ [^482-1]        yathā bandhitumicchati    
āharimena rūpena          na maṃ tvaṃ bādhayissasi .

Imañca me puttaphalaṃ         kāḷa uppāditaṃ tayā    
taṃ maṃ puttavatiṃ santiṃ         kassa ohāya gacchasi .

Jahanti putte sappaññā      tato ñātī tato dhanaṃ    
pabbajanti mahāvīrā         nāgo chetvāva bandhanaṃ .

Idāni te imaṃ puttaṃ        daṇḍena churikāya vā    
bhūmiyaṃ va nisumbheyyaṃ [^482-2]      puttasokā na gacchasi .

Sace puttaṃpi sigālānaṃ       kukkurānaṃ padāhisi    
na maṃ puttakate jammi        punarāvattayissasi .

Handa kho dāni bhadante      kuhiṃ kāḷa gamissasi    
katamaṃ gāmaṃ nigamaṃ           nagaraṃ rājadhāniyo .

Ahumha pubbe gaṇino        asamaṇā samaṇamānino    
gāmena gāmaṃ vicarimha       nagare rājadhāniyo .

Eso hi bhagavā buddho      nadiṃ nerañjaraṃ pati    
sabbadukkhappahānāya         dhammaṃ desesi pāṇinaṃ    
tassāhaṃ santike [^482-3] gacchaṃ    so me satthā bhavissati .

Vandanaṃ dāni vajjāsi [^482-4]     lokanāthaṃ anuttaraṃ    
#[^482-1] ma. Yu. Sākuntikova sakuṇiṃ . [^482-2] ma. Bhūmiyaṃ vā nisumbhissaṃ .  [^482-3] ma. Santikaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 483 -->
<a id="page-483"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 483]*

Padakkhiṇañca katvāna         ādiseyyāsi dakkhiṇaṃ .

Etaṃ kho labbhamamhehi       yathā bhāsasi taṃ cāpe [^483-1]    
vandanaṃ dāni te vajjaṃ       lokanāthaṃ anuttaraṃ    
padakkhiṇañca katvāna         ādisissāmi dakkhiṇaṃ .

Tato ca kāḷo pakkāmi      nadiṃ nerañjaraṃ pati    
so addasāsi sambuddhaṃ       desentaṃ amataṃpadaṃ    
dukkhaṃ dukkhasamuppādaṃ         dukkhassa ca atikkamaṃ    
ariyaṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ          dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ .

Tassa pādāni vanditvā      katvāna naṃ padakkhiṇaṃ    
cāpāya ādisitvāna        pabbaji anagāriyaṃ .

Tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Cāpā .

[470] [^483-4] petāni bhoti puttāni       khādamānā tuvaṃ pure    
tuvaṃ divā ca ratto ca       atīva paritappasi .

Sājja sabbāni khāditvā     satta puttāni brāhmaṇi    
vāsiṭṭhi [^483-2] kena vaṇṇena    na bāḷhaṃ paritappasi    
bahūni me [^483-3] puttasatāni     ñātisaṅghasatāni ca    
khāditāni atītaṃse          mama tuyhañca brāhmaṇa .

Sāhaṃ nissaraṇaṃ ñatvā        jātiyā maraṇassa ca  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 484 -->
<a id="page-484"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 484]*

Na socāmi na rodāmi       na cāhaṃ paritappāmi [^484-1] .

Abbhūtaṃ vata vāsiṭṭhi [^484-2]      vācaṃ bhāsasi edisiṃ    
kassa tvaṃ dhammamaññāya       giraṃ bhāsasi edisiṃ .

Esa brāhmaṇa sambuddho     nagaraṃ mithilaṃ pati    
sabbadukkhappahānāya         dhammaṃ desesi pāṇinaṃ .

Tassāhaṃ brāhmaṇa arahato    dhammaṃ sutvā nirūpadhiṃ    
tattha viññātasaddhammā       puttasokaṃ byapānudiṃ .

So ahaṃpi gamissāmi         nagaraṃ  mithilaṃ pati    
appeva maṃ so bhagavā       sabbadukkhā pamocaye .

Addasa brāhmaṇo buddhaṃ      vippamuttaṃ nirūpadhiṃ    
tassa dhammamadesesi         muni dukkhassa pāragū    
dukkhaṃ dukkhasamuppādaṃ         dukkhassa ca atikkamaṃ    
ariyaṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ          dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ .

Tattha viññātasaddhammo       pabbajjaṃ samarocayi    
sujāto tīhi rattīhi         tisso vijjā aphassayi .

Ehi sārathi gacchāhi        rathaṃ nīyādayāhimaṃ    
ārogyaṃ brāhmaṇiṃ vajja     pabbajito [^484-3] dāni brāhmaṇo    
sujāto tīhi rattīhi         tisso vijjā aphassayi .

(tato ca rathamādāya        sahassañcāpi sārathi  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 485 -->
<a id="page-485"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 485]*

Ārogyaṃ brāhmaṇiṃ avoca    pabbajito dāni brāhmaṇo    
sujāto tīhi rattīhi         tisso vijjā aphassayi .) [^485-1]    
etañcāhaṃ assarathaṃ         sahassañcāpi sārathiza    
tevijjaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ sutvā     puṇṇapattaṃ dadāmi te .

Tumheva hotu assaratho      sahassañcāpi brāhmaṇi    
ahaṃpi pabbajissāmi          varapaññassa santike .

Hatthigavassaṃ maṇikuṇḍalañca    
phītañcimaṃ gehavigataṃ pahāya    
pitā pabbajito tuyhaṃ        bhuñja bhogāni sundari    
tuvaṃ dāyādikā kule .

Hatthigavassaṃ maṇikuṇḍalañca    
rammañcimaṃ gehavigataṃ pahāya    
pitā pabbajito mayhaṃ        puttasokena addhito [^485-2]    
ahaṃpi pabbajissāmi          bhātusokena addhitā [^485-3]    
so te ijjhatu saṅkappo     yaṃ tvaṃ patthesi sundari    
uttiṭṭhapiṇḍo uñcho ca      paṃsukūlañca cīvaraṃ    
etāni abhisambhontī        paraloke anāsavā .

Sikkhamānāya me ayye      dibbacakkhuṃ visodhitaṃ    
pubbenivāsaṃ jānāmi        yattha me vusitaṃ pure .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 486 -->
<a id="page-486"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 486]*

Tuvaṃ nissāya kalyāṇi [^486-1]     therī saṅghassa sobhaṇe    
tisso vijjā anuppattā     kataṃ buddhassa sāsanaṃ .

Anujānāhi me ayye       icche sāvatthiṃ gantave    
sīhanādaṃ nadissāmi          buddhaseṭṭhassa santike .

Passa sundari satthāraṃ        hemavaṇṇaṃ  harittacaṃ    
adantānaṃ dametāraṃ         sambuddhamakutobhayaṃ .

Passa sundarimāyantiṃ         vippamuttaṃ nirūpadhiṃ    
vītarāgaṃ visaṃyuttaṃ           katakiccamanāsavaṃ .

Bārāṇasīto nikkhamma        tava santikamāgatā    
sāvikā te mahāvīra        pāde vandati sundarī .

Tuvaṃ buddho tuvaṃ satthā       tuyhaṃ dhītamhi brāhmaṇa    
orasā mukhato jātā       katakiccā anāsavā .

Tassā te svāgataṃ bhadde    tato te adurāgataṃ    
evaṃ hi dantā āyanti      satthu pādāni vandikā    
vītarāgā visaṃyuttā         katakiccā anāsavā .

Sundarī .

[471] [^486-5] daharāhaṃ suddhavasanā         yaṃ pure dhammamassuṇiṃ    
tassā me appamattāya      saccābhisamayo ahu .

Tatohaṃ sabbakāmesu         bhusaṃ aratimajjhagaṃ    
sakkāyasmiṃ bhayaṃ disvā       nekkhammasseva [^486-2] pihaye .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 487 -->
<a id="page-487"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 487]*

Hitvānāhaṃ ñātigaṇaṃ         dāsakammakarāni ca    
gāmakhettāni phītāni        ramaṇīye pamodite    
pahāyāhaṃ pabbajitā         sāpateyyaṃ anappakaṃ .

Evaṃ saddhāya nikkhamma       saddhamme suppavedite    
na me [^487-1] taṃ assa paṭirūpaṃ    ākiñcaññaṃ hi patthaye    
yā [^487-2] jātarūparajataṃ        ṭhapetvā [^487-3] punarāgame .

Rajataṃ jātarūpaṃ vā          natthetassapi santiyā [^487-4]    
na etaṃ samaṇasāruppaṃ        na etaṃ ariyadhanaṃ .

Lobhanaṃ madanaṃ cetaṃ          samohanaṃ [^487-5] rajabandhanaṃ [^487-6]    
sāsaṅkaṃ bahuāyāsaṃ         natthi cettha dhuvaṃ ṭhiti .

Ettāvatā [^487-7] pamattā ca   saṅkiliṭṭhamanā narā    
aññamaññena byāruddhā      puthu kubbanti medhagaṃ .

Vadho bandho parikleso      jāni sokapariddavo    
kāmesu adhipannānaṃ         dissate byasanaṃ bahuṃ .

Taṃ maṃñātī amittāva         kiṃ maṃ [^487-8] kāmesu yuñjatha    
jānātha maṃ pabbajitaṃ         kāmesu bhayadassiniṃ .

Na hiraññasuvaṇṇena          parikkhīyanti āsavā    
amittā vadhakā kāmā       sapattā sallabandhanā .

#[^487-1] ma. Netaṃ assa .  [^487-2] ma. Yo . [^487-3] ma. Chaḍḍetvā .  [^487-4] ma. Yu. Na bodhāya    
#na santaye .  [^487-5] yu. Mohanaṃ .  [^487-6] yu. Rajavaḍḍhanaṃ .  [^487-7] yu. Ettha rattā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 488 -->
<a id="page-488"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 488]*

Taṃ maṃ ñātī amittāva        kiṃ maṃ kāmesu yuñjatha    
jānātha maṃ pabbajitaṃ         muṇḍaṃ saṅghāṭipārutaṃ .

Uttiṭṭhapiṇḍo uñcho ca      paṃsukūlañca cīvaraṃ    
etaṃ kho mama sāruppaṃ       anagārūpanissayo .

Vantā mahesīhi [^488-1] kāmā    ye dibbā ye ca mānusā[^488-3]    
khemaṭṭhāne vimuttā te     pattā te acalaṃ sukhaṃ .

Māhaṃ kāmehi saṅgacchiṃ       yesu tāṇaṃ na vijjati    
amittā vadhakā kāmā       aggikhandhūpamā dukhā .

Aparisuddho [^488-2] eso sabhayo  savighāto sakaṇṭako    
gedho suvisamo ceso       mahanto mohanāmukho .

Upasaggo bhīmarūpo ca        kāmā sappasirūpamā    
ye bālā abhinandanti       andhabhūtā puthujjanā .

Kāmasaṃsaggasattā [^488-3] hi janā  bahū  loke avindiṃsu [^488-4]    
pariyantaṃ nābhijānanti        jātiyā maraṇassa ca .

Duggatigamanaṃ maggaṃ           manussā kāmahetukaṃ    
bahuṃ ve paṭipajjanti         attano rogamāvahaṃ .

Evaṃ amittajananā          tāpanā saṅkilesikā    
lokāmisā bandhanīyā        kāmā maraṇabandhanā .

#[^488-1] yu. Mahesinā . [^488-2] yu. Paripantho .  [^488-3] ma. Yu. Kāmapaṅkena sattā .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 489 -->
<a id="page-489"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 489]*

Ummādanā ullapanā        kāmā cittappamādino [^489-1]    
sattānaṃ saṅkilesāya        khippaṃ mārena oḍḍitaṃ .

Anantādīnavā kāmā        bahudukkhā mahāvisā    
appassādā raṇakarā        sukkapakkhavisosanā .

Sāhaṃ etādisaṃ hitvā [^489-2]    byasanaṃ kāmahetukaṃ    
na taṃ paccāgamissāmi        nibbānābhiratā sadā .

Raṇaṃ karitvā kāmānaṃ        sītibhāvābhikaṅkhinī    
appamattā viharissāmi [^489-3]    ratā [^489-4] saṃyojanakkhaye .

Asokaṃ virajaṃ khemaṃ          ariyaṭṭhaṅgikaṃ ujuṃ    
taṃ maggaṃ anugacchāmi         yena tiṇṇā mahesino .

Imaṃ passatha dhammaṭṭhaṃ         subhaṃ kammāradhītaraṃ    
anejaṃ upasampajja          rukkhamūlamhi jhāyati .

Ajjaṭṭhamī pabbajitā         saddhā saddhammasobhaṇā    
vinītā uppalavaṇṇāya        tevijjā maccuhāyinī .

Sāyaṃ bhujissā anaṇā        bhikkhunī bhāvitindriyā    
sabbayogavisaṃyuttā          katakiccā anāsavā .

Taṃ sakko devasaṅghena       upasaṅgamma iddhiyā    
namassati bhūtapati            subhaṃ kammāradhītaranti .

Subhā kammāradhītā .

Vīsatinipāto samatto .

#[^489-1] ma. Pamaddino . Yu. Pamāthino . [^489-2] ma. Yu. Katvā . [^489-3] ma. Vihassāmi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 490 -->
<a id="page-490"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 490]*

Therīgāthāya tiṃsanipāto    
[472] [^490-1] jīvakambavanaṃ rammaṃ           gacchantiṃ bhikkhuniṃ subhaṃ    
dhuttako sannivāresi        tamenaṃ abravī subhā .

Kiṃ te aparādhitaṃ mayā       yaṃ maṃ ovadiyānutiṭṭhasi [^490-1]    
na hi pabbajitāya āvuso     puriso samphusanāya kappati .

Garuke mama satthu sāsane     yā sikkhā sugatena desitā    
parisuddhapadaṃ anaṅgaṇaṃ         kiṃ maṃ ovadiyānutiṭṭhasi [^490-1] .

Āvilacitto anāvilaṃ        sarajo vītarajaṃ anaṅgaṇaṃ    
sabbattha vimuttamānasaṃ        kiṃ maṃ ovadiyānutiṭṭhasi [^490-1] .

Daharā ca apāpikā cāsi     kinte pabbajjā karissati    
nikkhipa kāsāyacīvaraṃ ehi     ramāmase [^490-2] supupphite vane .

Madhurañca pavanti sabbaso      kusumarajena samuṭṭhitā dumā    
paṭhamavasanto sukho utu ehi   ramāmase [^490-2] supupphite vane .

Kusumitasikharā ca pādapā      abhigajjantiva māluteritā    
kā tuyhaṃ rati bhavissati       yadi ekā vanamogāhissasi .

Vāḷamigasaṅghasevitaṃ          kuñjaramattakareṇuloḷitaṃ    
asahāyikā gantumicchasi       rahitaṃ bhīsanakaṃ mahāvanaṃ .

Tapanīyakatāva dhītikā         vicarasi cittaratheva [^490-3] accharā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 491 -->
<a id="page-491"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 491]*

Kāsikasukhumehi vagguhi        sobhasi nivasanehinopame .

Ahaṃ tava vasānugo siyaṃ       yadi viharemase kānanantare    
na hi matthi tayā piyataro     pāṇo kinnarimandalocane .

Yadi me vacanaṃ karissasi       sukhitā ehi agāramāvasa    
pāsādanivātavāsinī         parikammante karontu nāriyo .

Kāsikasukhumāni dhārassu [^491-1]    abhiropehi ca mālavaṇṇakaṃ    
kañcanamaṇimuttakaṃ bahuṃ         vividhaṃ ābharaṇaṃ karomi te .

Sudhotarajapacchadaṃ subhaṃ         goṇakatūlikasanthataṃ navaṃ    
abhirūhi sayanaṃ mahārahaṃ        candanamaṇḍitaṃ sāragandhikaṃ .

Uppalaṃ ca udakato [^491-2] ubbhataṃ  yathā yaṃ amanussasevitaṃ    
evaṃ tuvaṃ brahmacārini       sakesu aṅgesu jaraṃ gamissasi .

Kinte idha sārasammataṃ       kuṇapapūramhi susānavaḍḍhanetha    
bhedanadhamme kaḷevare       yaṃ disvā vimano udikkhasi .

Akkhīni ca turiyāriva         kinnariyāriva pabbatantare    
tava me nayanāni dakkhiya      bhiyyo kāmarati pavaḍḍhati .

Uppalasikharopamāni te       vimale hāṭakasannibhe mukhe    
tava me nayanāni dakkhiya      bhiyyo kāmaguṇo pavaḍḍhati .

Api dūragatā saremhase      āyatapamhe visuddhadassane  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 492 -->
<a id="page-492"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 492]*

Na hi matthi tayā piyatarā     nayanā kinnarimandalocane .

Apathena payātumicchasi        candaṃ kīḷanakaṃ gavesasi    
sineruṃ [^492-1] laṅghetumicchasi     yo tvaṃ buddhasutaṃ patthesi [^492-2] .

Natthi hi loke sadevake     rāgo yatthapi dāni me siyā    
napi naṃ jānāmi kīriso       atha maggena hato samūlako .

Iṅghāḷakhuyāva ujjhito       visapattoriva aggato [^492-3] kato    
napi naṃ passāmi kīriso       atha maggena hato samūlako .

Yassā siyā apaccavekkhitaṃ    satthā vā anusāsito siyā    
tvaṃ tādisikaṃ palobhaya        jānantiṃ so imaṃ vihaññasi .

Mayhaṃ hi akkuṭṭhavandite      sukhadukkhe ca sati upaṭṭhitā    
saṅkhatamasubhanti jāniya        sabbattheva mano na limpati .

Sāhaṃ sugatassa sāvikā       maggaṭṭhaṅgikayānayāyinī    
uddhaṭasallā anāsavā       suññāgāragatā ramāmihaṃ .

Diṭṭhā hi mayā sucittitā     sombhā dārukacillakāni vā    
tantīhi ca khīlakehi ca        vinibaddhā vividhaṃ panaccitā .

Tamhuddhaṭe tantikhīlake       vissaṭṭhe vikale paripakkate    
avinde khaṇḍaso kate       kimhi tattha manaṃ nivesaye .

Tathūpamaṃ dehakāni maṃ tehi     dhammehi vinā na vattanti  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 493 -->
<a id="page-493"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 493]*

Dhammehi vinā na vattanti     kimhi tattha manaṃ nivesaye .

Yathā haritālena makkhitaṃ      addasa cittikaṃ bhittiyā kataṃ    
tamhi te viparītadassanaṃ       paññā [^493-1] mānusikā niratthikā .

Māyaṃ viya aggato kataṃ       supinanteva suvaṇṇapādapaṃ    
upadhāvasi andha rittakaṃ       janamajjheriva rupparūpakaṃ .

Vaṭṭaniriva koṭarohitā       majjhe bubbuḷakā saassukā    
pīḷakoḷikā cettha jāyati     vividhā cakkhuvidhā va [^493-2] maṇḍitā .

Upādiya [^493-3] cārudassanā     na ca pajjittha alaggamānasā [^493-4]    
handa te cakkhuṃ harassu taṃ     tassa narassa adāsi tāvade .

Tassa ca viramāsi tāvade     rāgo tattha khamāpayi  ca naṃ    
sotthi siyā brahmacārini     na puno edisakaṃ bhavissata .

Āhaniya [^493-5] edisaṃ janaṃ      aggiṃ pajjalitaṃva liṅgiya    
gaṇhissaṃ āsīvisaṃ [^493-6] viya     api nu sotthi siyā khamehi no .

Muttā ca tato sā bhikkhunī    agami buddhavarassa santikaṃ    
passiya varapuññalakkhaṇaṃ        cakkhu āsi yathāpurāṇakanti .

Subhā jīvakambavanikā .

Tiṃsanipāto samatto .

#[^493-1] po. Ma. Saññā mānusikā . [^493-2] ma. Yu. Ca piṇḍitā . [^493-3] ma. Yu. Uppāṭiya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 494 -->
<a id="page-494"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 494]*

Therīgāthāya cattāḷīsanipāto    
[473] [^494-1] nagaramhi kusumanāme         pāṭaliputtamhi paṭhaviyā    
maṇḍe sakyakulakulīnāyo      dve bhikkhuniyo guṇavatiyo .

Isidāsī tattha ekā        dutiyā bodhīti sīlasampannā ca    
jhānajjhāyanaratāyo         bahussutāyo dhutakilesāyo .

Tā piṇḍāya caritvāna [^494-1]    bhattatthaṃ  kariya dhotāpattāyo    
rahitamhi sukhanisinnā         imā girā abbhavadesuṃ [^494-2] .

Pāsādikāsi ayye isidāsi   vayopi te aparihīno    
kiṃ disvāna valikaṃ [^494-3] athāsi   nekkhammamanuyuttā .

Evamanuyuñjiyamānā sā      rahite dhammadesanākusalā    
isidāsī idaṃ vacanamabravi [^494-4]   suṇa bodhi yathāmhi pabbajitā .

Ito paraṃ visajjanakathā [^494-5]    
ujjeniyā puravare mayhaṃ     pitā sīlasaṃvuto seṭṭhī    
tassamhi ekā dhītā [^494-6]     piyā manāpā dayitā ca .

Atha me sāketato varako    āgacchi uttamakulīno    
seṭṭhī pahūtaratano tassa      maṃ suṇhaṃ adāsi tāto .

Sassuyā sassurassa ca        sāyaṃ pātaṃ paṇāmamupagamma    
sirasā karomi pāde        vandāmi yathāmhi anusiṭṭhā .

#[^494-1] ma. Yu. Caritvā . [^494-2] ma. Yu. Abbhudīresuṃ . [^494-3] ma. Byālikaṃ . [^494-4] po. Ma. Isidāsī  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 495 -->
<a id="page-495"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 495]*

Yā mayhaṃ sāmikassa         bhaginiyo bhātuno parijano vā [^495-1]    
taṃ ekavārakaṃpi disvā       ubbiggā āsanaṃ demi .

Annena ca pānena ca khajjena ca  yañca tattha sannihitaṃ    
chādemi upanayāmi ca        demi yaṃ yassa paṭirūpaṃ .

Kālena uṭṭhahitvā [^495-2]      patigharaṃ samupagamiṃ    
ummāradhotahatthapādā [^495-3]    pañjalikā sāmikamupemihaṃ [^495-3] .

Kocchaṃ pasādaṃ añjanañca      ādāsakañca gaṇhitvā    
parikammakārikā viya         sayameva patiṃ vibhūsemi .

Sayameva odanaṃ sādhayāmi     sayameva bhājanaṃ dhoviṃ [^495-4]    
mātāva ekaputtakaṃ         tathā bhattāraṃ paricarāmi    
evaṃ maṃ bhattikataṃ anuttaraṃ     kārikaṃ taṃ nihatamānaṃ    
uṭṭhāyikaṃ analasaṃ           sīlavatiṃ dussate bhattā .

So mātarañca pitarañca bhaṇati   āpucchāhaṃ gamissāmi    
isidāsiyā na saha vacchaṃ      ekāgārehaṃ saha vatthuṃ .

Mā evaṃ putta avaca        isidāsī paṇḍitā paribyattāya    
uṭṭhāyikā analasā         kiṃ tuyhaṃ na rocate putta .

Na ca me hiṃsati kiñci        na cāhaṃ isidāsiyā saha vacchaṃ    
dessāva me alaṃ me       āpucchāhaṃ gamissāmi .

Tassa vacanaṃ suṇitvā         sassū sassuro ca maṃ apucchiṃsu    
kissa tayā aparaddhaṃ bhaṇa      kataṃ vissaṭṭhā yathābhūtaṃ .

#[^495-1] yu. Vāsaddo natthi . [^495-2] ma. Upaṭṭhahitvā gharaṃ samupagamāmi . [^495-3] ma. Ūmmāredhovantī  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 496 -->
<a id="page-496"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 496]*

Napihaṃ aparajjhaṃ kiñci         napi hiṃseva [^496-1] na gaṇāmi dubbacanaṃ    
kiṃ sakkā kātuye          yaṃ maṃ videssate bhattā .

Te maṃ pitu gharaṃ paṭi         nayiṃsu vimanā dukkhena adhibhūtā    
puttamanurakkhamānā          jināmhase rūpiniṃ lacchiṃ [^496-2] .

Atha maṃ adāsi tāto aḍḍhassa  gharamhi dutiyakulikassa    
tato upaḍḍhasuṅkena         yena maṃ vindatha seṭṭhi .

Tassapi gharamhi māsaṃ avasiṃ     atha sopi maṃ paṭicchati [^496-3]    
dāsīva upaṭṭhahantiṃ          adūsikaṃ sīlasampannaṃ .

Bhikkhāya ca vicarantaṃ damakaṃ     dantaṃ me pitā bhaṇati    
so [^496-4] sihi me jāmātā    nikkhipa pontiñca ghaṭikañca .

Sopi vasitvā pakkhaṃ         atha tātaṃ bhaṇati dehi me    
pontiṃ ghaṭikañca kapallakañca [^496-5] punapi bhikkhaṃ carissāmi .

Atha naṃ bhaṇati tāto ammā    sabbo ca me ñātigaṇavaggo    
kinte na kirati idha         bhaṇa khippaṃ yante karihiti .

Evaṃ bhaṇito bhaṇati yadi me    attā sakkoti alaṃ mayhaṃ    
isidāsiyā na saha vacchaṃ      ekagharehaṃ saha vatthuṃ .

Vissajjito gato so ahaṃpi    ekākinī vicintemi    
āpucchitvāna gacchāmi       marituṃ vā pabbajituṃ [^496-6] .

Atha ayyā jinadattā        āgacchi gocarāya caramānā    
#[^496-1] ma. Napi hiṃsemi na bhaṇāmi . [^496-2] ma. Lakkhiṃ . [^496-3] ma. Paṭiccharati . [^496-4] ma. Hohisi .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 497 -->
<a id="page-497"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 497]*

Tātakulaṃ vinayadharī           bahussutā sīlasampannā .

Taṃ disvāna amhākaṃ         uṭṭhāyāsanaṃ tassā paññāpayiṃ    
nisinnāya ca pāde         vanditvā bhojanamadāsiṃ .

Annapānena [^497-1] khajjena ca   yañca tattha sannihitaṃ    
santappayitvāna [^497-2] avacaṃ     ayye icchāmi pabbajituṃ .

Atha maṃ bhaṇati tāto idheva    puttike [^497-3] carāhi taṃ [^497-4] dhammaṃ    
annena ca pānena ca       santappaya [^497-5] samaṇe dvijātī ca .

Athāhaṃ bhaṇāmi tātaṃ         rodantī añjaliṃ paṇāmetvā    
anujānāhi [^497-6] maṃ tāta mayā pakataṃ  kammaṃ taṃ nijjaressāmi .

Atha maṃ bhaṇati tāto         pāpuṇa bodhiñca aggadhammañca    
nibbānañca labhassu          yaṃ sacchikari dvipadaseṭṭho .

Mātāpitū abhivādayitvā      sabbañca ñātigaṇavaggaṃ่    
sattāhaṃ pabbajitā          tisso vijjā aphussayiṃ [^497-7] .

Jānāmi attano satta jātiyo    yassā yaṃ phalaṃ vipāko    
taṃ tava ācikkhissaṃ          taṃ ekamanā nisāmehi    
nagaramhi erakakacche        suvaṇṇakāro ahaṃ pahūtadhano    
yobbanamadena matto so     paradāraṃ asevihaṃ .

Sohaṃ tato cavitvā         nirayamhi apaccisaṃ ciraṃ    
pakko tato ca uṭṭhahitvā    makkaṭiyā kucchimokkamiṃ .

#[^497-1] ma. Yu. Annena ca pānena ca khajjena ca . [^497-2] ma. Yu. Santappayitvā .

#[^497-3] yu. Puttaka . [^497-4] ma. Tvaṃ dhammaṃ . [^497-5] ma. Tappaya . [^497-6] yu. Pāpaṃ hi mayā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 498 -->
<a id="page-498"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 498]*

Sattāhaṃ jātakammaṃ [^498-1]       mahākapi yūthapo nillacchesi    
tassetaṃ kammaphalaṃ           yathāpi gantvāna paradāraṃ .

Sohaṃ tato cavitvā kālaṃ     karitvā sindhavāraññe    
kāṇāya ca khañjāya ca       eḷakiyā kucchimokkamiṃ .

Dvādasavassāni ahaṃ         nillacchito dārake parivahitvā    
kimināvaṭṭo akallo        yathāpi gantvāna paradāraṃ .

Sohaṃ tato cavitvā         govāṇijakassa gāviyā jāto    
vaccho lākhātambo         nillacchito dvādase māse .

Te puna [^498-2] naṅgalamahaṃ       sakaṭaṃ ca dhārayāmi    
andhovaṭṭo akallo        yathāpi gantvāna paradāraṃ .

Sohaṃ tato cavitvā         vīthiyā dāsiyā ghare jāto    
neva mahilā na puriso       yathāpi gantvāna paradāraṃ .

Tiṃsativassamhi mato          sākaṭikakulamhi dārikā jātā    
kapaṇamhi appabhoge         dhanikapurisapātabahulamhi .

Taṃ maṃ tato satthavāho       ussannāya vipulāya vaḍḍhiyā    
okaḍḍhati vilapantiṃ          acchinditvā kulagharassa [^498-3] .

Atha soḷasame vasse        disvāna [^498-4] maṃ pattayobbanaṃ kaññaṃ    
oruddhatassa putto         giridāso nāma nāmena .

Tassapi aññā bhariyā        sīlavatī guṇavatī yasavatī ca  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 499 -->
<a id="page-499"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 499]*

Anurattā bhattāraṃ tassāhaṃ    viddesanamakāsiṃ .

Tassetaṃ kammaphalaṃ yaṃ maṃ       apakiritūna gacchanti    
dāsīva upaṭṭhahantiṃ          tassapi anto kato mayāti .

Isidāsī .

Cattāḷīsanipāto samatto .

therīgāthāya mahānipāto    
[474] [^499-1] mantāvatiyā nagare rañño    koñcassa aggamahesiyā    
dhītā āsi [^499-1] sumedhā      pāsādikā sāsanakarehi .

Sīlavatī cittakathikā          bahussutā buddhasāsane vinītā    
mātāpitaro upagamma        bhaṇati ubhayo nisāmetha .

Nibbānābhiratā ahaṃ         asassataṃ bhavagataṃ yadipi dibbaṃ    
kimaṅgaṃ pana tucchā kāmā     appassādā bahuvighātā .

Kāmā kaṭukā āsīvisūpamā    yesu mucchitā bālā    
te dīgharattaṃ niraye         samappitā haññante [^499-2] dukkhitā .

Socanti pāpakammā         vinipāte pāpabuddhino sadā    
kāyena vācāya ca         manasā ca asaṃvutā bālā .

Bālā te duppaññā acetanā   dukkhasamudayoruddhā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 500 -->
<a id="page-500"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 500]*

Desente ajānantā       na bujjhare ariyasaccāni .

Saccāni amma buddhavaradesitāni   te bahutarā ajānantā ye    
abhinandanti bhavagataṃ          pihanti devesu upapattiṃ .

Devesupi upapatti          asassatā bhavagate aniccamhi    
na ca uttasanti [^500-1] bālā    punappunaṃ jāyitabbassa .

Cattāro vinipātā         dve ca gatiyo kathañci labbhanti    
na ca vinipātagatānaṃ         pabbajjā atthi nirayesu .

Anujānātha maṃ ubhayo        pabbajituṃ dasabalassa pāvacane    
appossukkā ghaṭissaṃ        jātimaraṇappahānāya .

Kiṃ bhavagatena [^500-2] abhinanditena  kāyakalinā asārena    
bhavataṇhāya nirodhā         anujānātha pabbajissāmi .

Buddhānaṃ uppādo vivajjito   akkhaṇo khaṇo laddho    
sīlāni brahmacariyaṃ          yāvajīvaṃ na dūseyyaṃ .

Evaṃ bhaṇati sumedhā         atha [^500-3] naṃ mātāpitaro ahaṃsu    
na mayaṃ [^500-4] gahaṭṭhā maraṇavasaṃ   gatāva jahessāma [^500-5] .

Mātā dukkhitā rodati pitā ca   dukkhito [^500-6] tathā sokasamabhibhūto    
ghaṭenti taṃ saññāpetuṃ       pāsādatale chamā patitaṃ .

Uṭṭhehi puttike kiṃ socitena  dinnāsi vāraṇavatimhi    
#[^500-1] ma. Yu. Santasanti . [^500-2] ma. Bhavagate . [^500-3] ma. Mātāpitaro na tāva .

#yu. Mātāpitaro na tāva āhāraṃ . [^500-4] ma. Āhāraṃ āharissaṃ . Yu. Āhariya .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 501 -->
<a id="page-501"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 501]*

Rājā anikaratto          abhirūpo tassa tvaṃ dinnā .

Aggamahesī bhavissasi         anikarattassa rājino bhariyā    
sīlāni brahmacariyaṃ          pabbajjā dukkarā puttike .

Rajje āṇā dhanamissariyaṃ     bhogā sukhā daharikāpi [^501-1]    
tasmā bhuñjāhi kāmabhoge    vāreyyaṃ hotu te putta .Tha    
atha ne bhaṇati sumedhā       mā edisakāni bhavagataṃ asāraṃ    
pabbajjā vā hotu [^501-2] maraṇaṃ vā  me na ceva vāreyyaṃ .

Kimiva pūtikāyaṃ asuciṃ         savanagandhaṃ bhayānakaṃ kuṇapaṃ    
abhisaṃviseyyaṃ bhastaṃ          sakipaggharitaṃ asucipuṇṇaṃ .

Kimiva tāhaṃ jānantī         vikūlakaṃ maṃsalohitupalittaṃ [^501-3]    
kimikulālayaṃ sakuṇabhattaṃ        kaḷevaraṃ kissa diyyatīti .

Nibbuyhati susānaṃ aciraṃ       kāyo apetaviññāṇo    
chaḍḍito [^501-4] kaliṅgaraṃ viya     jigucchamānehi ñātīhi .

Chaḍḍūna [^501-5] naṃ susāne parabhattaṃ nhāyanti jigucchantā    
niyakā mātāpitaro         kiṃ pana sādhāraṇā janatā .

Ajjhositā asāre kaḷevare  aṭṭhinhārusaṅghāte    
kheḷassuccārassava          paripuṇṇe pūtikāyamhi .

Yo naṃ vinibbhujitvā         abbhantaramassa bāhiraṃ    
#[^501-1] ma. Daharikāsi . [^501-2] yu. Hohiti . [^501-3] ma. Maṃsasoṇitupalittaṃ . Yu. Maṃsasoṇitapalittaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 502 -->
<a id="page-502"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 502]*

Kayirā gandhassa asahamānā    sakāpi mātā jiguccheyya .

Khandhadhātuāyatanaṃ           saṅkhataṃ jātimūlakaṃ dukkhaṃ    
yoniso aruciṃ [^502-1] bhaṇanti     vāreyyaṃ kissa iccheyyaṃ .

Divase divase tisattisatāni    navanavā pāteyyuṃ kāyamhi    
vassasatampi paghāto [^502-2]      seyyo dukkhassa ceva khayo .

Ajjhupagacche ghātaṃ yo       viññāyevaṃ satthuno vacanaṃ    
dīgho ca [^502-3] tesaṃ saṃsāro    punappunaṃ haññamānānaṃ .

Devesu manussesu ca        tiracchānayoniyā asurakāye    
petesu ca nirayesu ca       aparimitā dīyante [^502-4] ghātā .

Nirayesu bahū vinipātagatassa    kilissamānassa    
devesupi attāṇaṃ          nibbānasukhā paraṃ natthi .

Pattā te nibbānaṃ ye yuttā   dasabalassa pāvacane    
appossukkā ghaṭenti       jātimaraṇappahānāya .

Ajjeva tāta abhinikkhamissaṃ    bhogehi kiṃ asārehi    
nibbiṇṇā me kāmā        vantasamā tālavatthukatā .

Sā cevaṃ bhaṇati pitaraṃ        anikaratto ca yassa dinnā    
upayāsipi [^502-5] taruṇāvuto     vāreyyaṃ upaṭṭhite kāle .

Atha asitanicitamuduke         kese khaggena chindiya    
sumedhā pāsādañca pidhetvā [^502-6]  paṭhamajjhānaṃ samāpajji .

#[^502-1] ma. Anuvicinantī . [^502-2] yu. Ca ghāto . [^502-3] ma. Yu. Casaddo natthi . [^502-4] ma. Dissare .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 503 -->
<a id="page-503"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 503]*

Samāpattīhi [^503-1] samāpannā    anikaratto ca āgato nagaraṃ    
pāsādeva sumedhā         aniccasaññā subhāveti .

Sā ca manasi karoti         anikaratto ca āruhi turitaṃ    
maṇikanakabhūsitaṅgo           katañjalī yācati sumedhaṃ .

Rajje āṇā dhanamissariyaṃ     bhogā sukhā daharikāpi [^503-2]    
bhuñjāhi kāmabhoge         kāmasukhā dullabhā loke .

Nissaṭṭhaṃ te rajjaṃ bhogeva    bhuñjassu dehi dānāni    
mā dummanā ahosi         mātāpitaro te dukkhitā .

Tantaṃ bhaṇati sumedhā         kāmehi  anatthikā vigatamohā    
mā kāme abhinandi         kāmesvādīnavaṃ passa .

Cātuddīpo rājā mandhātā   āsi kāmabhoginaṃ aggo    
atitto kālakato          na cassa paripūritā icchā .

Satta ratanāni vasseyya      vuṭṭhimā dasadisā samantena    
na catthi titti kāmānaṃ       atittāva maranti narā .[^503-4]    
asisūnūpamā kāmā          kāmā sappasiropamā    
ukkopamā anudahanti        aṭṭhikaṅkalasannibhā .

Aniccā addhuvā kāmā      bahudukkhā mahāvisā    
ayoguḷova santatto        aghamūlā dukkhapphalā .

Rukkhapphalūpamā kāmā        maṃsapesūpamā dukhā  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 504 -->
<a id="page-504"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 504]*

Supinopamā vañcanīyā        kāmā yācitakūpamā .

Sattisūlūpamā kāmā         rogo gaṇḍo aghaṃ nighaṃ    
aṅgārakāsusadisā          aghamūlaṃ bhayaṃ vadho .

Evaṃ bahudukkhā kāmā       akkhātā antarāyikā    
gacchatha na me bhavagate       vissāso atthi attano .

Kiṃ mama paro karissati        attano sīsamhi ḍayhamānamhi    
anubandhe jarāmaraṇe        tassa ghātāya ghaṭitabbaṃ .

Dvāraṃ apāpuṇitvānāyaṃ [^504-1]   mātāpitaro anikarattañca    
disvāna chamaṃ nisinne        rodante idamavoca    
dīgho bālānaṃ saṃsāro       punappunañca rodataṃ    
anamatagge pitu maraṇe       bhātu vadhe attano ca vadhe .

Assu thaññaṃ rudhiraṃ saṃsāraṃ      anamataggato saratha    
sattānaṃ saṃsarataṃ sarāhi       aṭṭhīnañca sannicayaṃ .

Sara caturodadhī upanīte       assuthaññarudhiramhi    
sara ekakappamaṭṭhīnaṃ         sañcayaṃ vipulena samaṃ .

Anamatagge saṃsarato         mahiṃ jambudīpamupanītaṃ    
kolaṭṭhimattaguḷikā          mātāpitūsveva nappahonti .

Sara tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ      upanītaṃ anamataggato    
pitūsu caturaṅgulikā ghaṭikā     pitupitūsve nappahonti .

Sara kāṇakacchapaṃ pubbe       samudde aparato ca yugacchiddaṃ  


<!-- Siam Rath Page 505 -->
<a id="page-505"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 505]*

Siraṃ tassa ca paṭimukkaṃ        manussalābhamhi opammaṃ .

Sara rūpaṃ pheṇapiṇḍopamassa     kāyakalino asārassa    
khandhe passa anicce        sarāhi niraye bahuvighāte .

Sara kaṭasiṃ vaḍḍhente        punappunaṃ tāsu tāsu jātīsu    
sara kumbhīlabhayāni ca         sarāhi cattāri saccāni .

Amatamhi vijjamāne         kiṃ tava pañcakaṭukena pītena    
sabbā hi kāmaratiyo        kaṭukatarā pañcakaṭukena .

Amatamhi vijjamāne         kiṃ tava kāmehi ye pariḷāhā    
sabbā hi kāmaratiyo        jalitā ca kuthitā kupitā [^505-1] santāpitā .

Asapattamhi samāne         kiṃ tava kāmehi ye bahusapattā    
rājaggicoraüdakappiyehi      sādhāraṇā kāmā bahusapattā .

Mokkhamhi vijjamāne        kiṃ tava kāmehi yesu vadhabandho    
kāmesu hi vadhabandho        kāmakāmā dukkhāni anubhonti .

Ādīpitā tiṇukkā gaṇhantaṃ    dahanti neva muñcanti [^505-2]    
ukkopamā hi kāmā        dahanti ye te na muñcanti .

Mā appakassa hetu kāma-    sukhassa vipulaṃ jahi sukhaṃ    
mā puthulomova balisaṃ        gilitvā pacchā vihaññasi .

Kāmehi [^505-3] mā paribbhamasi    sunakhova saṅkhalābaddho    
kāhanti [^505-4] khu taṃ kāmā chātā  sunakhaṃva caṇḍālā .

#[^505-1] ma. Kampitā . [^505-2] ma. Yu. Muñcantaṃ . [^505-3] ma. Yu. Kāmaṃ kāmesu damassu tāva .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 506 -->
<a id="page-506"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 506]*

Aparimitañca dukkhaṃ           bahūni ca cittadomanassāni    
anubhohisi kāmayutto [^506-1]     paṭinissaja addhuve kāme .

Ajaramhi vijjamāne         kiṃ tava kāmehi ye sujarā [^506-2]    
maraṇabyādhigahitā           sabbā sabbattha jātiyo .

Idamajaramidamaraṇaṃ            idamajarāmaraṇapadamasokaṃ [^506-3]    
asapattamasambādhaṃ           akhalitamabhayaṃ nirupatāpaṃ .

Adhigatamidaṃ bahūhi amataṃ        ajjāpi ca labhanīyaṃ idaṃ    
yo yoniso payuñjati        na ca sakkā aghaṭamānena .

Evaṃ bhaṇati sumedhā         saṅkhāragate ratiṃ alabhamānā    
anunentī anikarattaṃ         keseva chamaṃ khipi sumedhā .

Uṭṭhāya anikaratto pañjaliko  yāci tassā pitaraṃ so    
vissajjetha sumedhaṃ pabbajituṃ    vimokkhasaccadassāvī [^506-4] .

Sā [^506-5] vissajjitā mātāpitūhi pabbaji sokabhayabhītā    
cha abhiññā sacchikatā        aggaphalaṃ sikkhamānāya .

Acchariyamabbhutantaṃ           nibbānaṃ āsi rājakaññāya    
pubbenivāsacaritaṃ yathā       byākari pacchime kāle .

Bhagavati konāgamane         saṅghārāmamhi navanivesamhi    
sakhiyo tisso [^506-6] janiyo     vihāradānaṃ adāsimhā [^506-7] .

Dasakkhattuṃ satakkhattuṃ         dasasatakkhattuṃ satāni ca satakkhattuṃ    
#[^506-1] yu. Kāmesu yutto . [^506-2] ma. Yesu jarā . [^506-3] ma. Idamajaramidamamaraṃ idamajarāmaraṃ padamasokaṃ .


<!-- Siam Rath Page 507 -->
<a id="page-507"></a>

##### *[Siam Rath Page 507]*

Devesu upapajjimhā        ko pana vādo mānusakesu [^507-1] .

Devesu mahiddhikā ahumhā    mānusakamhi ko pana vādo    
sattaratanassa mahesī         itthīratanaṃ ahaṃ āsiṃ .

So hetu so pabhavo        taṃ mūlaṃ ca sāsane khamaṃ [^507-2]    
taṃ paṭhamasamodhānaṃ           taṃ dhammaratāya nibbānaṃ .

Evaṃ kathenti [^507-3] ye saddahanti  vacanaṃ anomapaññassa    
nibbindanti bhavagate         nibbinditvā virajjantīti .

Sumedhā .

Mahānipāto samatto .

Gāthāsatāni cattāri        asīti puna cuddasa    
theriyekuttarasattā [^507-4]      sabbā tā āsavakkhayāti .

Theriyā gāthāyo samattā .

#[^507-1] yu. Manussesu . [^507-2] ma. Taṃ mūlaṃ tāva sāsane khanti . [^507-3] ma. Evaṃ karonti .
